# Me'eraf: Ethiopian Divine Office

**Author**: Me`erāf. Commun de l'office divine éthiopien pour toute l'année  
**Series**: PO Tome 33  
**Columns**: pp. 1–392  

[p. 1] {#po.33.p1}

### PRELIMINARY PRAYER
ተ እነ ከቨር ቦለቪየ1ክ አ፡11፡4 ለ ጸሉተ ፡ ኪናን ፤ ዘሠለስቱ ፡ ጊዜያት ፤ ግራ ቀቶስ ፡ አገዚለብሔር ፡ ቀቶስ ፡ ገያል ፡ ቀቶስ ፡ ሕያው ፡ ዘሊይመውት ፡ ዘተወልደ ፡
[to be said three times] [G-A] Holy God, Holy Mighty, Holy Living, Immortal, who was born of Mary, the holy Virgin, have mercy on us, O Lord.
አማርያም ፡ አምቅድስት ፡ ድንገል ፤ ተሣሀለ ፡ አገዚኦ ። ቀቶስ ፡ አገዚለብሔር ፡ ቀቶስ ፡ ንያል ፡ ቀቶስ ፡ ሐያው ፣ ዘሊይመውት ፡ ዘተጠዎቀ ፡ በዮርግኖስ ፡ ወተሰቀለ ፤ ዲበ ፤ ዕፀ ፤ መስቀል ፤ ተሣሀለ ፤ አገዚኦ ። ቀቶስ ፤ አገዚለብሔር ፡ ቀቶስ ፤ ንያል ፤ ቀቶስ ፤ ሐያው ፣ ዘሊይመውት ፡ ዘተንሥለ፡ አሙታን ፡ ለመ ፡ ሣልስት ፡ ዕለት ፡ ዐርገ ፡፤ በስብሐት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማያት ፤ ወነበረ በየ ማነ ፡ ለቡሁ ፤ ዳገመ ፣ ይመጽአ ፡ በስብሐት ፡ ይኩንን ፣ ሕያዋ ፤ ወሙታነ ተሣሀለ አገዚኦ ። ስብሐት ፤ ለለብ ፤ ስብሐት ፥ ለወልድ ፡ ስብሐት ፣ ለመንፈስ ፥ ቀዱስ ፡ ይአዚኒ ፡ ወዘልፈኒ ፤ ወለዓለመ ፡ ባለም ፡ ለጫን ፣ ወለጫን ፡ ለይኩን ፡ ለይኩን ። ቀቶስ ፤ ሥሉስ ፡ አገዚለብሔር ፡ ሕያው ፤ ተሣሀለነ ።
Holy God, Holy Mighty, Holy Living, Immortal, who was baptized in the Jordan and crucified on the wood of the Cross, have mercy on us, O Lord. Holy God, Holy Mighty, Holy Living, Immortal, who rose from the dead on the third day, ascended into heaven in glory, is seated at the right hand of his Father, who will come again in glory to judge the living and the dead, have mercy on us, O Lord. Glory to the Father, glory to the Son, glory to the Holy Spirit, now and always and unto the ages of ages. Amen! Amen! So be it! So be it! | Holy God, living Trinity, have mercy on us. | - MIDNIGHT KIDAN 1) [FIRST KIDAN]

ይሕ ። ርቱዕ ፡ ይደሉ ። ይ ፡ ቫ። ለከ ፥ አግዚኦ ፡ ለገባሬ ፡ ክሉ ፡ ለዘሊታስተርሊ ፡ ለምላክ ፡ ንሰፍሐ ፡ ነፍሰ ። ወስብሐተ ፡ ዘነህ ፡ ንፌኑ ፡ ለክ ፡ አገዚአ ፡ ለጥበበ ፡ ኛሉ ‹ ጎያል ፥ ብዙን ፡ ሣህል ፡ ለም ላክ ፡ ሣራረሃ ፡ ለነፍስ ። ንሴብሐክ ፤ ለዘአምቀድመ ፡ ባለም ፣ ተወልደ ፤ አምለብ ። ቃል ፡ ዘባሐቲቱ ፤ በቅቶሳኒሁ ፡ ዕሩፍ ፡ ኪያክ ፡ ዘትሴባሕ ፤ አምስብሐታት ፡ ዘሊያረምም ፡ ወአ ምሰሬዊተ ፡ ሊቃ ፡ መላአክት ። ኪያከ ፡ ዘሊተገብዝረ ፡ በአድ ፡ ፈጣሬ ፡ ዓቡላት ፣ ዘሊታ ስተርሊ ፡ ንጹሕ ፤ ወቅቶስ ፡ ዜናዊ ፡ ዘክ]ረ1 ፤ ጥበበ ፤ ዓቡላተ፡፣ ቀድሳቲከ ። ወብርዛ ፤ ለ ፤ ዘሊይጠፍአእ ፡ ለሰፈውክነ ፡ ስብሐተ ፡ ወለኩቴተ ፡ ንፌኑ ፡ ወቅድሳተ ፡ ንጹሐ ፡ ንብል ፡ ንሕ ፤ አሊለክ ፤ ለገብርት ፤ ወሕዝብኒ ፡ ኪያክ ፡ ይሴብሑ ። ይ፤ ሐ። ኪያከ ፤ ንሴብሕ ፤ አገዚኦ ።
The Priest says: For You, O Lord, Author of all things, invisible God, we open our soul and address to You the morning glorification, You, O Lord, Wisdom of all things. Mighty God, full of mercy, founder of the soul, we glorify You. You who were begotten by the Father before the world, Word, alone at rest among your saints, You who are glorified by glorifications that do not fall silent, from the cohorts of the Archangels. You, who were not made by [human] hand, invisible Creator of hidden things, pure and holy, messenger who revealed to us the wisdom of the mysteries of your holiness and who promised us, for our sake, the light that does not go out. We address to You glorification and thanksgiving in pure holiness and we say [it], we who are your servants. And the people also glorify You. The People say: We glorify You, O Lord. 2) [SECOND KIDAN]
ዷ ይ ፡ ካ ። ለምላክ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ወላደ ፡ ሐይወት ፡ ርአሱ ፡ ለለአምሮ ። ወዛቤ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ ዘ አምጸጋ ፤ ፍጹም ፡ ገባሬ ፡ ነፍስ ፤ በቋዔ ፤ ጸጋዊ ፣ መንፈስ ፡ ቅቶስ ፡ መዝገበ ፣ ጥበብ ፣ ረዳሊ ፡ መምህረ ፣ ቀቶሳን ፣ ወመሠረተ" ፡ ዓለም ፡ ዘይትዌከክፍ ፡ ጸሉተ ፣ ንጹሓን ። ለ ከ ፡ ንሴብሐ፤ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ዋሕድ ፡ ቀዳሜ ፡ በኩር ፡ ቃለ ፡ ለብ ፡ ዘለኩሉ ፡ ዚለክ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ ዘወሀብከክ ፡ ለአለ ፡ ንዱውዐክ ። ለብ ፡ ንጹል ፡ ዘለልቦ ፡ ነውር ፡ ዘቦቱ፤ ፡ ጥረት ። ጎበ ፡ 9ዒ ፤ ወንቀኔ ” ፡ ሊያማስኖ " ፡ ለዘበዥሉ ፡ ዓሊና ፡ ለአለ ፡ ይትዌክሉ ‹ ብክ ፡ ትሁብ ። ዘያፈትዎሙ ፡ ለመላአክት ፤ የሐውጵዎ ፡ ለዘአምቀድመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ብርሃን ፤ ዐቃቢ ፤ ዘሊይጣስን ። መዝገበ ፣ ጽልመተ ፡ አንተ ፡ ብ ፤ በሥምረተ ፡ ለቡክ ፡ ለብሬህክ ፡ ለ ፤ ዘለውፃአክ ፤ አማዕምቀ ፤ ውስተ ፥ ብርዛን ፡ ወወሀብከነ ፤ ሕይወተ ፡ አሞት ። ወጸጎክ!፤ አምገዝርናት ፡ ገዕዛነ ። ዘበመስቀልክ ፤ ለቅረብክ ፤ ንበ ፤ ለቡክ ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ በወንጌል ፡ መራሐክ ፡ ወበነቢያት ፡ ናዘዝከነ ፡ ዘለሊክ ፤ ለቅረብከ! ፤ ለምላክ ። ለላ ፡ ብ ርነ ፡ ሀበ ፥ አግዚኦ ። ለክ ፡ ለለምላክ ፥ ንወድስ ” ፡ ክመ ፡ ወትር ፤ በዘሊያረምም
The Priest says: God of light, generator of Life; principle of Science, giver of grace, [coming] from perfect grace, creator of the soul, generous benefactor, giver of the Holy Spirit, treasure of wisdom, helper, doctor of the Saints, foundation of the world, who accepts the prayer of the pure. We glorify You, only-begotten Son, first-born, Word of the Father who gave us all your grace, to us who call upon You. Pure Father, without stain who possesses goods, where moths and mites do not spoil anything; You who give to those who trust in You all the intelligence of what the angels have the desire to contemplate. Light before the world, our guardian, incorruptible treasure. The accumulation of darkness that was in us, by the good pleasure of your Father, You have illuminated it for us, You who brought us out of the abyss into the light and who gave us life through death, You who gratified us with freedom by pulling us out of servitude. By your Cross, You brought us closer to your Father, who is in the heights of the heavens. By the Gospel, You led us and by the Prophets, You consoled us. You Yourself brought us closer to God; but give us the light, O Lord. Our God, we praise You continually with thanksgivings that will not fall silent. We say it, we, your servants and your people also praise You. The People say: We praise You, O Lord.
ለኩጌተ ፡ ንብፅ ፡ ንሐ ፤ አሊለክ ፤ ለገብርት ፤ ወሕዝብኒ ፡ ኪያከ ፤ ይዌድሱ *” ። ፪

The Priest says: We triple for You from our mouth this glorification which, with your reign, is eternal, O Jesus, Son of the Lord, who are above all with the Father. All creatures glorify You in the trembling and fear of the spirit; You whom every soul fears and in whom all the souls of the just take refuge. You who calmed the waves, the torrents, the winds; You who were for us the haven of
መናፍስት ፡ ዘኮንክ! ፡ መርሶ ፡ ሐይወት ፣ አሙስና ፡ ወዘኮንክ ፡ ምጉያያ ፡ ዘቦቱ ፡ ተስፋ ፡ መድጊት ፡ ዘለዓለም ። በባሐር ፡ አለ ፤ ይትመሄቡ ፤ ታድንን” ፡ ወለአለ ፤ በበድው ፡ በጸጋ ፡ ትፊውስ” ፡ ወለአለ ፤ በመዋቀሐት ፡ ጽኑባን ፡ ዘምስሊሆሙ ፣ ትሂሉጆ። ፣ ዘአጣ አሰረ ፡ ሞት ፡ ፈትሐ ። ዘለምስኪናን ፡ ወለልህዋን ፡ ይናዝዞሙ ፡ ወለአለ ፤ ጻመዉ ፣ በመ ስቀሉ ፡ ይባልሐ ዘቫ'ሎ ፡ መዐተ ፣ ይመይጥ ፡ ወያሴስል ፤ አም፪ ፡ ለአለ ቦቱ ፡ ተወክ ፅ ። ዘነቢያት ፡ ወሐዋርያት ፡ ለአኩቱክ ፡ በነቡአ ፡ ኪያክ ንዌድስ ጅ ፡ አገዚኦ ፣ ወንሴ ብሐክ ፡ ከመ ፡ ለቢወነ ፡ ብክ ፡ ናዕርፍ ፡ በማጎደረ ፡ ሐይወት ፡ አንዘ ፡ ንገብር ፡ ፈቃደክ ። ሀበ ፡ ንሑር ፡ በትአዛዝክ ። ወሉ ፡ አገዚኦ ፡ በምሐረተክ ፡ ሐውጵ ፡ ንሉሳ ወዐቢያ! ፡ መኩን ፡ ወሐዝቦ ፡ ኖላዊ ፡ ወመርዔቶ ። አስመ ፣ ዚለክ ፣ መንገሥት ፡ ቡሩክ ፡ አገዚኦ ፣ ለምላክ!። ስብሐት ፡ ለለዝ ፤ ወወልድ ፤ ወመንፈስ ፤ ቀቶስ ፡ አምቀድመ ፡ ዓለም ፣ ይአ ዘዚ ፡ ወዘልፈኒ ፡ ለዓለም ፡ ወለትውልደ ፤ ትውልድ ፡ ዘሊየጎልቀ ፡ ለባለመ ፤ ዓለም ”።
life against corruption, and who were for us the refuge in which is found the hope of eternal salvation: You save those who are in peril at sea. By Your grace, You heal those who are in the desert. You are with those who are in harsh captivity. He has detached us from the bonds of death, He who consoles the indigent and the afflicted and those who have suffered. By his Cross, He delivers them. He turns away all anger from us who have placed our trust in Him. You, to whom the prophets and the Apostles give thanks in secret, we praise You, O Lord, and glorify You, so that having trusted in You, we may rest in the dwelling of life by doing your will. Grant us to walk in your commandments; and in your mercy, O Lord, cast a look upon us all, small and great, prince and people, shepherd and flock, for your reign is blessed, O Lord, our God. Glory to the Father and to the Son and to the Holy Spirit, from the beginning of the ages, now and always and unto the ages and for the generations of generations that do not pass away unto the ages of ages. Amen!

### II. - MORNING KIDAN

4) [FIRST KIDAN] The Priest says: May the grace of the Lord be with you! The People say: With your Spirit. The Priest says: Let us glorify our God. The People say: It is worthy. The Priest says: Strengthen the thoughts of your heart. The People say: We have turned them toward the Lord. Our Father who art in heaven. Our Father who art in heaven. Our Father who art in heaven. Lead us not, O Lord, into temptation. The Priest says: O Lord, Father, Giver of light, power of all things, visitor of every soul, marking with the seal of the light that exists before the ages; guide of life, and dispenser of the bliss | that does not die! You, who brought us out of the obstacle of darkness, You who granted us the benefit of inaccessible light. You who detached from their bonds those who believe in You, You crowned us with faith. He does not distance Himself from his servants, || is ceaselessly with them. He is not deaf to the soul that prays to Him with fear and trembling. Before the thought, He knows everything and before the thought || scrutinizes and before we pray to Him, He satisfies our desire. By his will, || He answers us when we call upon Him without doubting. Ineffable light, King of the army of heaven, who hears the glorification of the canticle of the angels, upon whom || rests. Hear us, we pray You, O Lord. Give us, to glorify You, with confidence, with a voice that does not fall silent. May

The Priest says: O Lord, Jesus Christ, hear us, O Holy One. Be for the mute the word, for the crippled, the staff, for the blind, the light, for the lame, the path, for the lepers, the purifier, O Lord, You have healed those who were seized by sufferings, physician of the deaf. He rebuked death and tormented the darkness, He who made the light: sun that does not set and lamp that does not go out, which shines ceaselessly upon the saints. And He fixed everything for the ornamentation of the world. By a benevolence exercised openly toward all, He rose like a star as salvation for men, converter of the soul, You began to take care of everything, as is fitting. Creator of the angels, Father of all the adornment of the world, Founder of the earth, wisdom and science of the Father that existed from the beginning, was sent into the world. He is indestructible essence and which the spirit cannot explain. Announcer of the invisible, You are glorious, and admirable is your name. That is why, we your servants, we praise You, O Lord. The People say: We praise You, O Lord. 6) [THIRD KIDAN]
ይ ፡ ሐ ኪያክ ንዌድስ ፡ አገዚኦ ። ይ ፡ ካ ። ንጫልስ ፡ ለክ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ቅቶሰ ፡ ስብሐተ ፡ ዘወሀብክ! ፡ ዚለክ ፡ ዛይማኖተ ፡ ዘሊይተህሠት ፡ ወቦቱ” ፡ ገበርክ ፡ ለ ፡ ንማአ ፡ ማአሰሪሁ ፡ ለሞት ። ዘፈጠርክ ፡ ጎሊናተ ፡ ርቱዓተ ፤ ለአለ ፤ የለዎኑ ፡ ዝክ ፡ ክመ ፥ አምሰብአ ፡ ይክ+ ፡ ለማልክተ ፡ ዘወሀብክነ። ፡ በመንፈስ ንኪድ ፤ ኛሉ ንይሉ ፤ ለጸላሊ ፤ ክመ ንፍታል ፤ ዘሊይተረታል ፡ ፍቀረ ፡ ለ ፡ ንበ ፡ ለቡክ ፡ ገበርክ ፡ ወዐረቀ” ፣ ሟአክሌ)” ። ስማዕ ፡ አገዚኦ ፡ አለ ፡ ኪያክ ፡ ይስአሉ ። አገዚኦ ፣ አለ ፤ ዘንስአለክ ዖ ፤ ሊንሰተት ” ፡ ለላ ፡ በስክየት ፡ ዲበ ፣ ጸላሊ 1" ፡ ሀሉ ። ሀበ ፡ ዘወትር ፤ ጸሉተ ፡ አምጊጠተ ፡ ጸላሊ ፡ ንትዐቀብ ። ስማዕ ፡ ዘለዓ ለም ፡ ንጉሥ ፣ መዓስበ ፡ ናዝዝ። ፣ አጓለ ፡ ማውታ ፡ ተወከፍ ፡ ሚሩያነ ፤ በምሕረትክ ፡ ለንጵሐ ኞ ፡ ለብዳ ፤ ለጥብብ ወህጉላ ፤ ሚጥ ፡ አለ ፤ ውስተ ፡ ቀል ፡ ለድጎን ፡ ወ ለል ፡ ኩን ፡ ጸወነ ፡ አስመ ፡ ለክ ፡ አገዚኦ ፡ ለምላክ ፡ መንገሥት ፡ ቡሩክ ፣ ለጫን” ። ተና
The Priest says: We triple for You, O Holy One, this glorification, You who gave us an indestructible faith in Your person and with the help of which You made us overcome the bonds of death; You who created upright | intelligences for those who believe in You, so that from men they become gods; You who gave us that, through the spirit, we crush all the forces of the Enemy so that we may untie the untieable. You have made for us charity before your Father and You have reconciled us with Him. Hear, O Lord, those who pray to You, O Lord: what we ask of You is that we may not be abandoned but that we remain accusers before the Enemy? Grant us that, through our incessant prayer, we may be preserved from the seduction of the Enemy. Hear, O eternal King, console the widow; support the orphan. Purify in your mercy the impure. Give wisdom to the foolish and bring back the strayed. Save the prisoners and be for us all, a refuge, for You possess, O Lord, our God, a blessed kingdom. Amen!

### III. - EVENING KIDAN
ኪቶን ፡ ዘሰርክ ። ፲. ይ ካ። ጸጋሁ ፤ ለኣግዚለብሔር ፡ የሀሉ ፤ ምስሌክሙ ። ይ" ሐ። ምስለ ፤ መንፈስከ ። ይ፤ ካ። ለአኩትዎ ፤ ለለምላክነ ። ይ፡ ሐ። ርቱዕ ፤ ይደሉ፤ ። ይ ቫ። ለክ ፥ ለለብ ፥ ዘሊይማስን ፤ መድን ፡ ነፍስነ ፤ ወመሠረተ ፡ ጥበባት ፡ ዐቃ ቤ ፡ ለልባቢነ ። ዘአንተ፣ ውስጥ ፡ ዐይክ ፣፤ ለብራህክ፣ ወጽልመወተ ጎሊና! ፤ በዘዚለ ክ ፥ ለአምሮ ፡ ክለልከነ ። ለንተ ፡፤ ዘተውህበ ፤ ለሙስና ፤ ዘትካት ፡ ብአሴ ፡ ለድንንክ በ መስቀሉ ፤ ለዋሕድክ ፤ ወሐደስኮ ፡ በዘሊይማስን ፤ ዘበስሕተታተ ፤ በጠላ ፡ በትአዛዝክ ፡ ወበሞተ ፡ ወልድክ ፤ ቤዘውክ ፣ ወዘተገድፈ ፤ ጎሠሥክ ። በአንተ፣ ዝንቱ" ፡ ንሐ)፣0 ለ ገብርቲክ ፤ ንሴብሐክ"። አገዚኦ ።
7) [FIRST KIDAN] The Priest says: May the grace of the Lord be with you. The People say: With your spirit. The Priest says: Give thanks to our God. The People say: It is worthy and just! The Priest says: You, O incorruptible Father, Savior of our souls and foundation of sciences, guardian of our hearts, You who illuminate our inner eye and the darkness of our thought by this science which is Yours and with which You crown us. You who saved the ancient man, delivered to perdition, by the Cross of your only-begotten Son and who renewed him by that which is incorruptible! What had been abolished by sin, by your commandments and by the death of your Son, You have redeemed and the one who was lost You went to seek. That is why we, your servants, we glorify You, O Lord. The People say: We glorify You, O Lord. 8) [SECOND KIDAN]
ይ ፤ ሕ ። ንሴብሐከ። አገዚኦ”" ። ጺ ይ ፡ ካ ። ንዌድሰክ ፡ አግዚዶ ፡ ዘወትረ ፡ ይሴብሑ ፡ ስብሐተ ፡ ማሐሌተ ፡ ወዘሊቃ)። ፡ መላአክት ፡ በሊያርምሞ ፡ ፡ አንበለ ” ፡ ዕረፍት ፡ ውዳሴ ፤ ስብሐት ፤ ለከቴት ፡ ዘለጋ ዕዝት ፡ በመሓሐልይ ፡ ይሴብሑክ ፡ አገዚኦ ፡ ዘፈኖክ ፡ ምክረክ ፡ ዚለክ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ዚለክ ፡ ጥበበ ፡ ወዘዚለክ ፤ ሐዋዴ ፤ ዘሀሎ ፤ ምስሌክ ፤ አምቀድም"፡ ፡ ባለም ፡ አንበለ ፡ ይተገ በር ፤ ዘሊተገብረ ፤ ቃል ፤ ዘተርአየ ፡ በሥጋ ” ፣ ለመድቴኒተ ፣ ትዝምደ ፡ ሰብአ ። ወልድ ክ ወፍቁርክ ፤ አገዚአ ፤ ሊየሱስ ፥ ዘለገዐዘ ፡ አምለርዑተ ፤ ኀጢለት ። ወበአንተ ፡ዝ ንቱ ፡ ንል ፥ ለገብርቲክ ፤ ንዌድሰክ ፤ አገዚሶ ።
The Priest says: We praise You, O Lord, You whom the glorification of the canticle and those of the archangels glorify ceaselessly, without truce or rest, the praise of glory, the thanksgiving of the Dominations. | In the canticles, they glorify You, O Lord, You who sent your Counsel, your Word and your Wisdom, your Providence which existed with You before the world was created, Word which was not created, which was seen in the flesh, for the salvation of the human race. Your beloved Son, Our Lord Jesus Christ, who delivered us from the yoke of sin. That is why we, your servants, we praise You, O Lord. The People say: We praise You, O Lord. 9) [THIRD KIDAN]


[p. 6] {#po.33.p6}
ይ ል ። ንዌድስክ ፣ አገዚኦ "" ። ይ ቫ ። ለክ ፥ ዘአምለብ ፥ ውዳሴ ፡ ንጫልስ ወፃቤ ሐይወት ፣ አገዚኦ ። ሐዋ ፡ ነፍሰ ፡ ትሑታን ፡ መንፈሰ ፤ ምንዱበ ፣ ሊተሀየየ ፡ ዘይትዌክፎሙ ፡ ለአለ” ፡ ይሰደሩ ፡ ረ ዳሊ ፡ ወለአለ ፤ ውስተ ፡ ሠርም ፣ ይተመ ቡ ፡ መድንን ፡ ለርጉባን ፤ ዘይቴሊ ፡ ወይት ቢቀል " ፡ ለገፉባን ። ዐርክ ፥ መዛይምናን ፣ መስተናገር ለቋድቃን ወማንደር፣0ለንጹዱሓሐን ። ወለአለ ፤ በጽድቀ ፤ ይዴውዕዎ ፡ ሰማዒ” ፣ ለመበለት ፣ ከዳኒ ፤ ወባላሒ ፤ ለአጓለ ፤ ማ ውታ ፡ ዘይሁብ ፡ መርሐ ፤ ርቱዐ ፡ ለቤተ ፤ ክርስቲያን ፤ ዘተከላ ፤ ላቲ ፤ ምዕራፈ ፡ ስብ ሐተጆ ፡ ዛይማኖት ፡ ጉባሌ ፡ መንፈስ ‹ ሀብተ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ ወንይል ። ለክ ፡ አንዘ ፡ ንዌድስ ፡ ወ ሊናዐርፍ ፡ ለዝሉፉ ፤ በለልባቢ ፡ ናስተማስል ፤ ለምሳለ ፤ መንገሥተከ ፤ ቦቱ ። በአንቲለ ከ ፤ ወበአንተ ፣ ፍቁር ፣፤ ወልድከ ፤ አገዚአ ፤ ሊየሱስ ፡ ዘቦቱ ፡ ለከ ፡ ስብሐት ፣ ወአጊ ዝ ፡ ለባለመ ፡ ባለም ፡ ለሜን ።
The Priest says: For You, with [all] our heart, we triple [our] praise; giver of life, O Lord, visitor of the soul of the humble who does not abandon the afflicted spirit, who receives those who suffer persecution; helper of those who are afflicted in the abyss, and savior who provides for the needs of the hungry and avenges the oppressed, friend of the faithful, holding conversation with the just, and dwelling of the pure; hearing those who call upon Him in justice, protector of widows and defender of orphans, who gives an upright guide to the Church in which He has established a place of rest, the splendor of faith, the society of the Spirit, the gift of grace and power. Praising You without taking rest, ceaselessly, we reproduce in our hearts the image of your kingdom because of You and because of your beloved Son, Our Lord Jesus Christ, through whom for You are glory and power unto the ages of ages. Amen!


[p. 7] {#po.33.p7}

### 1. EVENING LITON
ወሥዖሦሎዮኞሦ ፲፲ ፲ ሊጦን ፡ ዘሰርክ ። ዘዋዜማ ። ራ ይ ፡ ካ ። ለምላክ ፡ ዘዲበ ፡ ኪሩቤል ፡ ትነብር ፤ ፡ አምጎበ ፤ መላአክት ፡ ተተለኩት፡ ፡ ወትሴባሕ" ። ለንተ ፤ ውአቱ ፡ ዘክፈልክ ፤ ንባአ ፡ ውስተ ፤ ድርሐ ፡ ቅቶስ ፡ ማጎደ ርክ ፤ ንዘክር ፡ ቅድሳተ ፤ ስብሐቲከ ፡ ወንፍቂ ፡ ለከ ፤ ዘሰርክ ፤ ለኩቴተ ። ለንተ ፤ ተ ወክፍ ፡ ዘዥል ፥ ሰክዮተ ፣ ወበሩዔተ ፤ በተለምኖ ፣ ዘውስተ ፤ ልብው ፣ ዘበሰማያት ፣ ም ሥጢርክ፤ ፡ በዴና ፡ ሠናይ ፡ መዐዛ ። ዕቀብ ፡ ለ ፡ ዘአንበለ ፥ ጸሐብ ፥ ወዘአንበለ ፡ ተመውዖ ፡ ወዘአንበለ ፤ ማዕቀፍ ። ለድጎን ፡ ለአጋረ ፤ አምዳንፅ ፤ ወለዕይንተነ ፤ አምለን ብዕ ፡ ወነፍስተ ፤ አሞተ ፤ ኝጢለት ወደምረ! ፡ ምስለ ፤ አለ ፤ ድጉ ፥ ብክ ፥ በጸሉተ ‹፤ ኩሎሉሙ ፡ አለ ፥ አምባለም ፣ ለሥመሩክ ” ። በ፪ቶ ወልድከ ፡ ዘቦቱ ፡ ለክ ፡ በፅ ። ዘዘወትር ። ግራ ይ ፤፡ ካ ። ናስተበቀዕ ፤ ፤ ዘሾሉ ፡ ይአጎዝ ፤ አገዚለብሔር ፡ ለብ ፡ ለአገዚአ ፡ ወመድን ኒ ፥ ሊየሱስ ፥ ክርስቶስ ፥ አስ፲ ፡ ፡ ረሰየ ፥ ተድላ ፡ ካዕበ ፥ ውስተ ፣ ዝንቱ ፡ መካን ፣ ቀቶስ ፤ ንቁም ፡ ንሰብሕ ፡ ወንዘምር ፡ ለዘበዥሉ ፤ ቀቶስ ፡ ወብፁዕ ፡ ስመ ፡ ዚለሁ። ዘኛሉ ፡ ይእጎሽ ፡ እገዚለብሔር ፡ ለምላክ ። ይ፡ዲ።ጸልዩ ። ይ ፡ ካ ። አግዚሶ ፤ ዘሉ ፡ ትአጎዝ ፡ ንስአለክ ፡ ወናስተበቱዐክ ፡ በክመ ፡ ዐቀዝክ ፡ ኑን ፤ ሌሊት ፡ ወለብዳጳሐክ ፤ ካዕበ ፤ ውስተ ፡ ብርፃ ፤ ፅርክ ፡ ዕቀበ ፤ በጎ ፤ ሊ ሊት ። ሱላሜ ፡ ዘአንበለ ፤ ገጢለት ፤ ሰርክ ፤ ወሌሊተ ፤ ጸገወነ"” ። ወለድጎክ ፥ አምፍ ርሀተ ፡ ሌሊት ፤ ወአምትቫካዝ ፣ ዘይወስድ ፤ ውስተ -" ፤ ጽልመት " ። አምሰብአ”"፡ ዐማ ዒ ፡ ወሐባሊ ፤ ባልሐ ዘለበስ ፡ ወዘተሰወረ ፡ ሐገ ፡ ሰብአ ለናሕሲ ፡ ለ ። አስመ ፡ ቴር ፤ ወመፍቀሬ ፤ ሰብአ ፤ ለንተ ፤ እግዚለብሔር ፡ ለምላክ ። በቶ ፤ ወልድክ ፡ ዘቦቱ ፡ ለክ ፡ በፅ ።
1. [Dominical Liton] [A] The Priest says: Our God, who sittest upon the Cherubim, from the Angels You receive thanksgivings and You are glorified. It is You who gave us to enter into the upper room of your holy dwelling in order to remember the holiness of your glory and to discharge toward You the duty of evening thanksgiving. As for You, accept from us the recourse and the supplication [that we address to You] with confidence in your understanding, [You] whose celestial and wise mystery is surrounded by a perfume of pleasant odor. Keep us all without trouble, and without defeat and sheltered from scandals. Save our feet from falling; our eyes, from tears and our souls, from the death of sin. And mingle us with those who have been delivered by You because of the prayer of all those who have pleased You at all times. Through your only-begotten Son through whom for you [with him and with the Holy Spirit are glory and power, now and always and unto the ages of ages. Amen!] 2. [Ferial Liton] [G-A] The Priest says: We beseech the Lord who governs all things, Father of the Lord and of our Savior Jesus, for He has made our delights. Let us stand in this holy place in order to celebrate and to sing in psalmodies the name of Him who in everything is holy and blessed, the Lord, our God who governs all things. The Deacon says: Pray. The Priest says: O Lord, who governs all things, we pray You and beseech You; just as You kept us during the length of the night, and You brought us again into the light of the evening, keep us during the length of the night. Grant us concord without sin, in the evening and at night. And save us from the fear of the night and from the sadness that leads into darkness. From the iniquitous and deceitful man, deliver us, You whom we have offended and who hid Yourself from us in human nature, forgive us. For You are good and friend of man, Lord, our God. Through your only-begotten Son, through whom, etc.


[p. 8] {#po.33.p8}

### II MORNING LITON
ሊጦን ፡ ዘነገህ ። ዘሑይ ። ገልራ ይ ፤ ዛካ ። ወካዕበ ፡ ናስተበዕ ፡ ዘሉ ፡ ይአንዝ ፤ አገዚለብሔር ለዝ ፤ ለአገዚአ ፡ ወመድንኒ ፡ ሊየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። አንዘ ፡ ናለሸቶ ፡ አስመ ፡ ዐቀበ)! ፡ በኑጎ ፡ ሌሊት ። ወለብጽሐ ፡ አምድልወት ፣ ውስተ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ አሙስና ፣ ውስተ ፡ ዘሊይማስን ፤ አምሊ ያአምሮ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ለአምሮ ፡ ለጣን ፡ ከመ ፡ ኑን ፡ ዕለተኒ ፡ በሉ ፡ ሰላም ፡ ወጥዒና ፡ ይረስየ ፡ ነሀሉ ፤ ወይልጽር ፡ሕዝቦ ፤ በንይለ ፥ መላአክቲሁ ፡ ዘለዥሉ ፡ ገብረ ፡ በረክት ፡ ሥልጣን ፡ ቦቱ ፡ አገዚለብሔር ፡ ለምላክ ። ይዲ።ጸሪዕዩ ። ይ ፡ ካ ። አገዚአ ፡ ዘሉ ፡ ትአጎዝ ፡ ለንተ፣ ኑን፣ ዕለዢ ፡ በሉ ፡ ሰላም ፡ ወንና ፡ ጸገወነ ፡ ሄሉ ። አስመ ፡ ለንተ ፡ ዐቀብክ ፤ በኑን ፡ ሌሊት ። ለንተ ፡ አገዚኦ ፡ ዘዙሉ ፡ ትአነዝ ፡ መልለክክ ፡ ጌረ ፥ መራሒ ፡ ፈኑ ፤ ለ ። ወተ፡ሀለ ፡ በክወ ፡ ዕበየ ፡ ጣህልክ ። ወበከመ ፡ ብዝን ፡ ምሕረትከ ፡ ደምስስ ፡ ለበሳ ። ወሊለሐደ ፡ አም፲! ፡ ውድለ ፡ ወገሑ ሠ ፡ ሊትረሲ ፡ አምገስክ ፡ በአንተ ፡ ዐቢይ ፡ ስምክ ፡ ዘተሰምየ ፡ በላዕሌ ። ሰሪዩ ፡ ኩን ፡ ለነ ፡ ወሊተጎድገ ፡ ሀበ ፡ ንርክብ ፡ ሞገሰ ፡ በቅድጫክ ፡ ወበቀድመ ፡ ክርስየስ ፡
1. Of Monday [G-E-A] The Priest says: And we beseech again the Lord who governs all things, Father of Our Lord and of our Savior Jesus Christ, at the same time as we give Him thanks for having kept us during the length of the night and for having brought us from darkness into light, from corruption into that which is incorruptible and from ignorance into true science, - in order to be throughout the day in total peace and in perfect health | and that He may defend his people by the power of his angels, the Lord our God who has power for every work of blessing. The Deacon says: Pray. The Priest says: Lord, who govern all, grant us to be throughout the day in total peace and in perfect health, for You kept us during the length of the night. You, O Lord, who govern all, send us your angel as a good guide. And be merciful to us according to the greatness of your mercy and according to the abundance of your compassion, blot out my fault. See to it that not a single one among us be put outside nor separated from your grace because of your great name which has been pronounced over us. Be for us He who forgives. Do not abandon us and grant us to find grace before You and before Christ, your Son through whom for You, with Him and with the Holy Spirit, are glory and power, now and always and unto the ages of ages. Amen!
ወልድክ ። ዘዞቱ ፡ ለክ ፡ በል ። ዘሠሉስ ። ግልራ
[G-E-A] 2. Of Tuesday


[p. 9] {#po.33.p9}
ይ ፤ ካ ። ስምከ ፡ ሐያው ፡ ዘሊይመውተ ፡ ተንህ ፡ ዘሊይደቀስ፤ ፡ ፈጣሪ ፡ ዘሊይይክም ፡ አገዚአ ፡ ሥሉጥ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ኛሉ ፡ ለምላክ ፤ ዘሊይተነባአ ። ዘኪያክ ፡ ያንቀፀቶ ፡ ኛሉ ፡ ዘበሰማይ ፡ ወዘበምድር ፡ ዘፈጠርክ ፡ ለስምክ ፡ ይሰብሑ፣ ወያአቫቱ፤፡ ለንተ፣ ለኃ ዜ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አገዚለብሔር ፡ ለምላክ ። ይ፡ዲ፡።።ጸሪዕዩ ። ይ ፡ ካ ። ለምላክ ፡ ለምላክ ፡ ተፍሥልት ፡ ወለምላክ ፡ሐጫት ፡ተወክከፍ ፡ ጸሉየው፤ ፡ ለለገብርቲክ ፡ ወለለአማቲክ ፡ አለ ፡ ቋመዉ ፡ ወመጽሉ ፡ በተ፣ መቀደስከ ፡ ንበ ፣ የንድ ር ፡ ገይለ ፡ ስብሐቲከ ። ከመ ፡ ይስለሉ ፡ ዘአምጎበክ ፡ ርቀየተ ፣ ወሐይወተ ፡ ንድገ" ፡ ሉሙ ፡ ለበሳሆሙ ፡ ወሊተ'፲ጽር ፡ ምገባረ ፡ ንኀጢለቶሙ ፡ ወተወክፍ ፡ ንስሓሆሙ ፡ ይባ አ” ፡ ጸሉቶሙ ፡ በቀድጫክ ፡ ክመ ፡ መዐዛ ፡ ሠናይ ። አወ ፡ አገዚኦ ፡ ለምላክ ፡ ትሬ ሊ ፡ ወሊታስተርሊጆ። ፡ መሓረ ፡ ነድር ፡ ወተመየጠ ፡ ክመ ፡ በገዚ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ወበዘይመ ጽአ ፡ ንስገድ ፣ ወናልዕል ፡ ኖአዥት ፣ ወንሰብሕ ፡ ስመከ ፡ ዓቡረ ፡ በክርስቶስ ። ዘቦቱ ፡
The Priest says: Your name, Living One who does not die, Watcher who does not slumber, tireless Creator, all-powerful Lord, God, who does not hide, toward whom rise the eyes of all beings that in heaven and on earth You have created. May they glorify your name and give You thanks, O You who govern the world, Lord, our God. The Deacon says: Pray. The Priest says: Our Lord, God of joy and God of gladness, accept the prayers of your servants and of your handmaids who have labored and have come into the house of your sanctuary, where resides the power of your glory, to ask You for help and life. Forgive them their faults, do not look at the works of their sins and accept their penances. May their prayer rise in your presence like a pleasant perfume. Yes, O Lord, our God, You see and You do not appear. Merciful, look and convert us so that in this world and in the one to come, we may adore, we may exalt, we may praise and we may glorify together your name. Through Christ, through whom for you, etc.
ለክ፤ በል ። ዘረቡዕ ።
[G-E-A] 3. Of Wednesday


[p. 10] {#po.33.p10}
ገልራ ይ ፥ ካ።ለትሩጽ ፡ አገዚኦ ፡ ምሐረትክ ፡ ላዕሌነ ፡ ወዝርኘነ፡ ገጽከ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ነፍስ! ። ይ ምጻአ ፡ ምሐረትክ ፡ ዘያሐይወ ፡ ያስተርሊ ፡ ቲሩትክ ፤ ዘያስተፌሥሐ ፤ ዕቀበ ፤ አገዚ ሶ ፡ አምሹሉ ፡ አኩይ ፡ ወአምዥሉ ፤ ስሰተት ። ወተወክፈ)፤ በክመ ፣ ነቢብክ ። ገበር ፡ ሰላመ ፡ ማአከሊነ ፡ ወማአከሲክ ፡ አስመ ፡ ለዘኒ ፥ ስሐተ ፡ ለንተ ፥ ትክል ፤ መይጦቶ ፣ መ ንገሌክ ። ወንሰገድ ፡ ለክ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ለዕቋፃቲክ ፡ ዘሊይትኋለፉ ። አስመ ፡ ለወ ፡ ዕለተ፤ ፡ ጸራዝ ፡ ጎቤክ ፡ ለንተ ፡ ትሰምዐ ። ወትጸውር ፡ ለ ፡ ድካመ ፡ ወትትዔዒገሠ ፡ ኀጢለተ ። አስመ ፡ መሓረ ፡ ለንተ ፡ አገዚለብሔር ፡ ለምላክ ። ይዲ።።ጸሪዕዩ ። ይ ፡ ካ ። አስመ ፡ ለንተ ፡ ከመ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ለዝ ፡ ቴር ፡ ዘለስተርለይክ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ለም ፡ ክመ ፡ ትስቂ ፡ ወትሐፅን ፡ አጓለ ፤፡ አወሐያውክ ፣ ወሊተኒ ፤ ፡ ለገብርክ ጸገዉኒ ፡ ወትረ ፡ ብዕለ ፡ ሠናያቲክ' ። አስመ ፡ ለንተ ፡ ትሁብ ፡ ሠናያተ ፡ ለጎሩያኒክ ፣ ወተዴጉ ፡ መድቴኒተ ፡ ለሕዝብክ" ። ወፈቂደክ ፡ ታስተርሊ ፡ ለስተርለይክ ። ወመኑ ፤ ለምላክ ፡ መሓረ ፡ ወቀቶስ ፡ ዘከማክ ። ወተሣሀለ ፡ በክመ ፤ ዕበየ ፡ ሣህልከ ፤ ወበከመ ፤ ብዝነ ፡
The Priest says: O Lord, may your mercy hasten upon us and the light of your face upon our soul. May your mercy come which vivifies us, may your goodness appear which rejoices us. Keep us, O Lord, from all evil and from all straying; and receive us according to your word. Make peace in our midst and with You, for You can bring back toward You the one who has strayed. And we adore You in your innumerable courts, for the day we cried toward You, You heard us and You bore for us our weakness, and You tolerated patiently our sin, for You are merciful, Lord, our God. The Deacon says: Pray. The Priest says: Indeed, You are truly the Son of the excellent Father, You who appeared in the world in order to quench the thirst and to feed the human race which is yours. To me, your servant: grant me ceaselessly the wealth of your goods; for it is You who give goods to your elect, and who grant salvation to your people. Visible at your will, You appeared. And what God is great and holy, like You? Be merciful to us, according to the greatness of your mercy; and according to the abundance of your compassion, blot out my fault. Through your only-begotten Son, [through whom for You, with him and with the Holy Spirit are glory and power, now and always and unto the ages of ages! Amen!]
ምሕረትከ ፤ ደምስስ ፡ ለበሳ ። በ፳ፉ ፣ ወልድክ ፤ በል ። ዘንሙስ ።

ገልራ ይ ፡ ካ ። ከመ ፡ ታድጎዘ ፡ አገዚኦ ፡ አምዕለት ፡ አኪት ፡ ወአምሰዓታተ ፡ መንሱት ፡ ወ አምሹሉሙ ፡ ዘአኩያን ፤ ትምይንት ፤ አምህየት ፤ ፡ ወአምፅርዐት"፣ አምድድቅ፣ ወአ

misfortunes and from accidents. Deliver us, O Lord, our God, in this hour. You who govern the world, O Lord our God. The Deacon says: Pray. The Priest says: Yes, O Lord, our God, for it is from You that help and salvation come, and for all those who have taken refuge in Your divinity. It is You who saved David from the murderous spear and Daniel from the lions' den and Susanna from the hand of the elders, Ananias and Azarias and Misael from the furnace of flaming fire. Save us, O Lord, our God, from the perverse and violent generation. Receive our souls and our bodies in deposit with Your goodness. Keep the churches in glorification, in prayer, and in the orthodox faith. Be merciful to us, our God, Father who art in heaven. Lead us not into temptation, but save us and deliver us from all evil, from defeats before Satan and from impiety and falls. Help us to serve You without weariness or nonchalance, always, at all times and at every hour. Through Your only-begotten Son, etc.


[p. 11] {#po.33.p11}

5. For Friday
ዘዐርብ ። ግልራ ይ ፡ ካ «። ተዘክር ፡ አገዚኦ ‹ ለለ8ቶ ፡ ፳ቶ ፡ ስአለቶ ፡ ተዘክር ፡ አገዚኦ ፡ ለለ8ቶ ‹ ፳ቶ ፡ አምክርሠ ፤ አሙ ። ተዘከር አገዚኦ ፤ ብዙኃ ፤ ለሐዛበ አለ ፤ በርትዕት ፣ ዛይማኖት ፡ የሐይዉ ፡ ለንስቲያሆሙ ፡ ዕቀብ ፤ ወራዙቶሙ፤ ፡ ሠርሐ ፡ ርሱዓ ፤ ለጽንዕ ፣ ቀጥቁጣ ፡ ባልሕ ፤ ወዝርዋነ -፤ ለስተጋብአ ። ለአለ ፤ ይነገቶ ፤ ንገድ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ ወለአለ ፤ ይ ተመነደቡ ፡ ለድጎኖሙ ። ወለአለ ፤ ውስተ ፡ ተስናን ፡ ወቀንየት ፤ መረር ፣ ይሂልለዉ፡ ለን ተ ፡ ለኃዜ ፡ ባለም ፡ ተዘከር ፣ አገዚለብሔር ፡ ለምላክ ። ይ፡ዲ።ጸጸዕዩ ። ይ ፡ ካ ። አወ ፡ አገዚኦ ፡ ለምላክ ። ለመዐስብ ፡ ከዳኒ ፡ ወበላሒ ፡ ለአጓለ ፡ ማውታ ፡ ለቶያን ፡ ዐቃቤ ፤ ሥራይ ። ለአለ ፡ ይነገቶ። መርስ ፡ ወዛሕን አስመ ፡ ለንተ ፡ አገዚ ለብሔር ፡ ተለምር ፡ ወትጤይቀ ፡ ለለጸቆ ፤ 8ቶ ፡ ስአለቶ ፡ ወንብረቶ ፣ ወትካዘ ፡ ቤቱ ። ለድንን ፤ አገዚኦ ፡ ዛተኒ ፤ ሀገረ ፤ ወካልለተኒ ፡ ለህጉረ ፡ ወበሐውርተ ፡ አለ ፤ በይማኖ ተ ፡ ዚለክ ፡ ያንድሩ ፤ ውስቴጥን ። ቀዱሳነ!፣ ለድንን አምድልቅልቀ ፤ ወአምረኃብ ፡ ወአመሬደ ፡ ካልአ ፡ ሕዝብ ። ሐዋዘ ፡ ብርሻ ፡ ጸገወነ ፣ ወለአተት ፡ ኑፋቄ ፡ አምበተ ፡ ክ ርስቲያንክ ። ወደምረ!” ፡ ምስለ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ ብርሻነ ፤ ስብሐቲከ ፡ ሀበ ፡ ከመ ፡ በ3ቶ ፡
The Priest says: Remember, O Lord, the requests of each [of us]. Remember each one from his mother's womb. Remember the many peoples who live in the orthodox faith. Keep their wives and support their adolescents. Strengthen the elderly. Deliver those who are broken. Gather those who are dispersed. As for travelers, travel with them. Save those who are in affliction. And of those who are in discord and in harsh servitude, remember, O You who govern the world, O Lord, our God. The Deacon says: Pray. The Priest says: Yes, O Lord, our God, protector of widows and liberator of orphans, physician of the sick, haven and tranquility of travelers, for You are the Lord and You know exactly the requests and the way of life of each one and the sadness of his house. Save, O Lord, this country and the other countries and the regions that dwell in Your faith. Deliver the saints from earthquakes, from famine, and from the incursion of another people. Grant us a pleasant light and remove the divisions of Your Church. And mingle us with the children of the light of Your glory. And grant us that, with one [single] heart and one [single] mouth, we may glorify the Father and the Son and the Holy Spirit, now and always and unto the ages of ages. The People say: Amen!

6. For Saturday The Priest says: Principle of grace, Jesus Christ, power and wisdom of Your Father, for | You are the Lamb of the Lord who takes away the sins of the world. Be merciful to us, for You descended from heaven according to the measure of the wisdom of Your Father, in order to save

Your creatures whom You fashioned from the death that corrupts. He who walks on the wing of the wind, who makes His angels wind and those who serve Him flames of fire; who looks at the earth and makes it tremble; touches the mountains and they smoke. By the spirit and Your grace, strengthen Your people, O You who govern the world, O Lord, our God. The Deacon says: Pray. The Priest says: Yes, O Lord, our God, by the spirit of Your grace, rejoice Your people, You who are the help of the persecuted, You who lighten what is heavy; ornament of the apostles, fortune of the poor, hope of the desperate, resurrection of the dead, Jesus Christ, power and wisdom of Your Father, we give You thanks, now and always and unto the ages of ages. The People: Amen!

7. For Holy Saturday The Priest says: Ps. 124:1 If it had not been the Lord who was on our side; 2 Let Israel say - if it had not been the Lord who was on our side, When men rose up against us. 3 Then they would have swallowed us up alive, When their wrath was kindled against us. 4 Then the waters would have overwhelmed us. Our soul has escaped from the torrent. 5 Our soul has escaped from the tumultuous water. 6 Blessed be the Lord, our God, who has not given us over to the nets of their snares. 7 Our soul has escaped as a bird from the snare of the fowler. 8a Our help is in the name of the Lord. The Deacon says: Pray.

The Priest says: 8b Who made heaven and earth. Who made heaven and earth. Who made heaven and earth. In the ages and unto the ages of ages.

For Sunday The Priest says: And we further beseech the God of mercy, You who helped Abraham when he | fought the kings, who saved Isaac from the knife, who guided Jacob on his journey, who appeared to him in the vision of the night, where he set up a stele, who magnified Joseph in a foreign land

ዕቆብ ፤ በውስተ ፤ መንገድ ፡፤ ወለስተርለይኮ ፡ በራእየ ፤ ሊሊት ፡ በንበ ፣ ለቀሞን ፣ ለሐ ውልት ፡ ዘለዕበይኮ ፤ ለዮሴፍ ፡ በብሔረ ፤ ባዕድ ፤ አገዚለብሔር ፡ ለምላክነ ። ይ፡ዲ፡። ጸሪዩ ። ይ ፡ ቫካ ። አወ ፡ አገዚዶ ፥ ለምላክ ፡ ዘንደፍኮ ፡ ለኖጎ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ታቦት ፡ ወለንሌፅ ፡ አምለፈ ፡ ለናብስት ፡ ወለሶስና ፡ አምአደ ፡ ረበናት ። ዘለለኮ ፡ ለሙሴ ፡ ምስለ ዐቢይ ፣ ረድሌት ፡ ዘመራሕኮሙ ፤ ፤ ለልዝብክ ፤ መዓልተ ፡ በደመና ፤ ወኩሉ ፡ ሊሊተ ፡ በብር ሻ ፥ አሳት ። ወተናገርክ" ፡ በዲበ ፡ ነቢያት ። ወለስተርለይክ ፡ ጸጋክ ፡ ላዕለ፣ ሐዋር ያት ፡ አንዘ ፡ ጉቡላን ፡ አሙንቱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ 8ቶ ፡ ቤት ፡ ወረደ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቀ ቶስ ፡ ክመ ፡ ዘአሳት ፡ ወተናገሩ ፡ በክረ ፡ ኛሉ ፡ በሐውርት ፡ ውአተ ፡ መንፈሰክ ፡ ፈኑ ፡ ዐማደ ፡ ሰማይ ፤ ወምድር ፡ ባርክ ፡ ጣጎበረነ ፡ በሉ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ወበዥሉ ፡ ሰዓት ። በጸቆ ፤ ወልድክ ፤ ዘቦቱ ፡ ለክ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፣ ወምስለ ፡ ቀዱስ ፣ መንፈስ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ወአጊዝ ፡ ይ
The Deacon says: Pray. The Priest says: Yes, Lord, our God, You who saved Noah inside the ark, Daniel from the lions' den, and Susanna from the hand of the elders. You who delegated Moses, helping him greatly. You who guided Your people, by day, by the cloud, and by night by the light of fire. You spoke through the prophets and manifested Your grace upon the apostles. While they were gathered in a house, the Holy Spirit descended upon them like fire, and they spoke in the language of all countries. Send this Spirit which is Yours, pillar of heaven and earth. Bless our assembly at all times and at every hour. Through Your only-begotten Son, through whom, for You, with Him, and with the Holy Spirit, are glory and power, now and always and unto the ages of ages.
አዚዜኒ ፤ ወዘልፈኒ ፤ ወለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ለጫን ። ርቨለ፻፲ፐጀክ፪ ፲፲፲ ፲ ወመስተብቀዕ ። ፲
J MASTABQU'E |. Preliminary supplication for all Actions


[p. 14] {#po.33.p14}
ገሬ በአንተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ገብር ። ይ ፡ ካ ። ናስተበቀዕ ፡ ዘኩሉ ፡ ይአጎዝ ፡ አገዚለብሔር ፡ ለብ ፡ ለአገዚአ ፡ ወወድቴኒ ፡ ሊየሱስ ፤ ክርስቶስ ። አንዘ ፡ ናለቶ ፤ በዥሉ ፤ ወበውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ። አስመ ፡ ከደክ ፡ ወረ ድለ ፡ ሶቀ ፡ ወለብድሐ ፡ አስከ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ሰዓት ፡ ንስአል ፡ ወናስተበቱዕ ። ክመ ፡ ተረፈ ፡ ሊሊተኒ ፤ በዥሉ ፤ ሰላም ፡ ወጥዒና ፡ ይረስየ)፤፡፣ ዘለምሕረት ፤ ሥልጣን ፡ ቦቱ ፡ አገዚ ለብሔር ፤ ለምላክነ ። ይዲኅ። ጸሪዩ ። ይ ፡ ካ ። አገዚኦ ፡ ዘዥሉ ፡ ትአጎዝ ፡ ንስአል ፡ ወናስተበቱዕ ፡ ቀቶሰ ፡ ስመክ ። ነጵር ፡ ላዕሌ ፥ ወውስተ ፡ ስአለተ ፡ ሕጽር ፡ ሕዝበከ ፣ በኀይለ ፤ መላአክቲከ ። ዥሉ ፣ ሕመሜ ፡ ወሉ ፡ መንሱተ-፡ ደምስስ ፤ አምላዕሌየ ፡ ወአምላዕለ ፡ ኛሉ ፡ ሐዝብክ ። ወተረፈ ፡ ሌ ሊትኒ ፤ በዥሉ ፡ ሰላም ፡ ወዳጎና ፡ ጸገወ'" ፡ ነሀሉ ፤ በክርስቶስ ፤ ዘቦቱ ፡ ለክ ፡ ምስሌ ሁ ፤ ወምስለ ፡ ቀቶስ ፣ መንፈስ ፣ ስብሐት ፣ ወአጊዝ ፡ ይአዚኒ ፤ ወዘልሪኒ ፤ ወለባለ” ፤ ባለም ። ለሜን ። %
The Priest says: We shall beseech the Lord who governs all; Father of the Lord and of our Savior Jesus Christ, while we give Him thanks, for all things and in all things. For He has protected and helped us, supported and brought us to this hour. We pray and beseech to establish us in complete peace and in good health for the rest of the night, He who has power over mercy, the Lord, our God. The Deacon says: Pray. The Priest says: O Lord, who govern all, we shall pray and beseech Your holy name. Cast Your eyes upon us and thanks to our requests, protect Your people by the power of Your angels against all suffering and erase all temptation from above me and from above all Your people. And grant us to pass the rest of the night | in complete peace and in good health through Christ, through whom for You and with Him and with the Holy Spirit are glory and power, now and always and unto the ages of ages. Amen!


[p. 15] {#po.33.p15}


[p. 16] {#po.33.p16}

### II. For the Sick
በአንተ ፡ ቶያን ። ግራ ይ ፡ ካ ። ወካዕበ ፥ ናስተበቃዖ ፡ ለዘኩሉ ፡ ይአጎዝ ፣ እገዚለብሔር ፡ ለብ ለአገዚአ ፡ ወመድቴኒ ፡ ሊየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ በአንተ ፣ ቶያን ፡ ለኀዊ ፡ ክመ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ደዌ ፡ ወኩ ሉ ፡ ሐማመ ፡ ያሰስል ፥ አም፤ሆሙ ። መንፈሰ ፡ ደዊ ፡ ስቧሮ ፣ ሐይወተ ፡ የሀቦሙ ። ዘለ ሉ ፤ ፈውስ ፡ ሥልጣን ፤ ቦቱ ፤ አገዚለብሔር ፡ ለምላክነ ። ይ ዲ ሉ‹። ጸለዩ ፥ በአንተ ፡ ቶያን፤ ። ይ ካ ። አገዚለብሔር ፡ ዘዥሉ ፡ ትአገዝ ፡ ንስአለክ ፡ ወናስተበቀዐክ ፡ ለቶያን ፡ ለ ጎው ፡ ሐይወተ ፡ ሉሙ ፡ ጸጉ ። መንፈሰ ፡ ደዊ ፥ ሰዐር ፡ ዥሎ ፣ ደዌ ፣ ወሉ ፡ ሐማመ ፡ አምላዕሊሆሙ ፤ ፡ ለጎልፍ ። ፍጡ ፡ ይርክበ ፥ ሣህልክ ፥ አገዚኦ ። ይዲ። ንበል ቫልነ ። ይ ሐ ። አገዚኦ ፡ ተሣሀለ)። ይ ፡ ካ ። መፈውሰ ፡ ነፍስ ፥ ወመፈውሰ ፡ ሥጋ ። ሐዋጺዛፃ ፡ ለንተ ፡ ለላይ ፡ ዘሥጋ ፡ ወ ለአለ ፤ አምንበ ፤ መናፍስት ፥ ርኩሳን ፤ ይዴዐሩ ለገዕዝ ፤ ለኩላ ፣ ነፍስ ፡ ምንድብት ፤ ወጥውቀት፡ ሀብ ጣጎተ ፡ ወሀብ ፡ ዕረፍተ ። ሹሉ ‹ ደዌ ፤ ስድድ ፤ አምዝንቱ ፡ ቤት ፡ ወአምአለ" ፡ ይዴውድ ፡ ቀቶሰ ፡ ወቡሩክ ፡ ስመክ ። ወለአለ ፡ ቱልቄ ፡ ነፍሳቲነ ፡ አንክ ፡ ደዊ ፡ ፈዊሰክ ” ፡ ፍዱመ ፣ መድቴተ ፡ ጸጉ ። በ8ዱ ፡ ወልድከ ፡ ዘቦቱ ፡ ለክ ። በል ።
The Priest says: And we shall further beseech [the Lord who governs all things, Father of the Lord and of our Savior Jesus Christ], for our sick brothers, so that He may remove from them all diseases and all sufferings; and that after having destroyed the germ of the disease, He may give them life, He who has power over all remedies, the Lord, our God. The Deacon says: Pray for the sick. The Priest says: Lord, You who govern all, Father of the Lord and of our Savior Jesus Christ, we pray and beseech You for the sick brothers. Grant them life. Destroy the germ of the disease. Make the disease and all sufferings pass from them. May Your mercy come quickly before us, O Lord. The Deacon says: Let us all say. The People say: O Lord, be merciful to us. The Priest says: Physician of the soul and physician of the flesh, You the visitor of all this flesh and of all those who suffer from the part of unclean spirits, deliver all afflicted and tormented souls. Give tranquility and give rest. Drive away all diseases and all sufferings from this house, and from those who invoke Your holy and blessed name. And after having healed the many diseases of our souls, grant [them] perfect salvation. Through Your only-begotten Son who has for You, [with him and with the Holy Spirit, glory and power, now and always, and unto the ages of ages. Amen!]

### III. For Travelers
በአንተ ፤ አለ ፤ ይነባቶ ። ገራ ይ ፡ ካ ። ወካዕበ ናስተበቱዕ ፡ በል ። በአንተ ፡ አለ ፡ ይነገቶ ፡ ለጎዊ ፡ ወበአንተሂ ፡ አለ ፤ ሀለዉ ፡ ይንገቶ ፡ አመሂ ፤ በባሕር ፤ ወአመሂ በለፍላገ ፣ ወአመሂ ፤ በቀላያት ፡ ወአመሂ ፡ በፍኖት ፡ በዘኮ ፡ መንገደ ፣ ይገብሩ፤ ፥፤ ከመ ፣ ሹሉ ፣ ያብጽሕ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መ ርሶ ፤ መድጎን ። ዘዙሎ ፡ ይአጎዝ ፤ አገዚለብሔር ለምላክ ። ይ ፡ ዲ ። ጸልዩ ፡ በአንተ ፡ አለ ፡ ይነገቶ ። ይ ፡ ካ ። አገዚለብሔር ፡ ዘዥሉ ፡ ትአጎዝ ፡ ንስአለክ ፡ ወናስተበቀሮክ ፡ ለአለ ፡ ነገ ፡ ለጎዊ ፡ ወበአንተሂ ፡ አለ ፤ ሀለዉ ፡ ይንገቶ ፤ ለቪድ ፡ ለርትዕ ፡ ጎቡረ ፡ ምስሊሆሙ ፡ ክ ዊነ ፡ ፍቅድ ፥ ፍጡ ፡ ይርክበ ፡ ጣህልክ ፡ አግዚኦ ። ይዲሉ። ንበፅ፤ ልነ ። ይ፤ ሐ ። አገዚኦ ፡ ተሣሀለነ ። ይ፤ካ ። ለብጽሖሙ ውስተ ፡ ዘመድጎን ፡ መርሶ ፡ ለውሬ ፣ ለሰብሶሙ ፡ በፍሥሓ ፡
The Priest says: And we shall further beseech [the Lord who governs all things, Father of the Lord and of our Savior Jesus Christ] for our brothers who are traveling and for those who are about to travel, whether by sea, or by rivers, or by the depths, or by the roads, whatever route they may take, so that the Savior may lead them all to the port, the Lord who governs all, our God. The Deacon says: Pray for those who travel. The Priest says: Lord, You who govern all, we pray and beseech You for our brothers who are traveling and for those who are about to travel, direct their journey; be pleased to be together with them. May Your mercy come quickly before us, O Lord. The Deacon says: Let us all say. The People say: O Lord, be merciful to us. The Priest says: Bring them to the port of salvation. Send the men back in joy and gladness while they will rejoice and make [others] rejoice. Through Your only-begotten Son, etc.

### IV. For Rains
ወበሐጫት- አንዘ ፡ ይትፌሥሑ ፤ ወያስተፌሥሑ ። በአቶ ፡ ወልድክ ፡ በል ። ፲ኛ በአንተ ፡ ዝናማት ። ገፊ ይካ ። ወካዕበ ፥ በል ። በአንተ ፥ ዝናማት ፡ ከመ ፣ ዝናሞ ፡ ይፈኑ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዓበ ፡ ይተፈቀድ ፡ መካን ፤ ዘዥሉ ይአጎዝ ፤ አገዚለብሔር ለምላክነ ። ይዲ ጸልዩ ፥ በአንተ ፥ ዝናማት፤ ። ይ፤ ካ ። አገዚለብሔር ፤ ዘሉ ፡ ትአጎዝ ፣ ንስአለክ ፤ ወናስተበቱዐክ ፥ ዝናሟቲከ ፡ ፈኑ ፥ ውስተ ፡ ኀበ ይትፈቀድ ፤ መካን ። ለስተፍሥሐ ፤ ገጻ ለምድር ፣ ወለርዊ ትለሚ ፃ ፡ ለብጽሐ ፤ ለዘርአ ፤ ወለማአረር ፡ ዘአምጎበ ፡ ቲሩተክ ፡ ፍጡ ፡ ይርክበ! ፡ ሣሀልክ ፡ አገዚኦ ። ይዲሉ። ንበል ፡ ቻል ። ይ ሕ አገዚኦ ፡ ተሣሀለ ። ይ ካ ። በአንተ ፡ ነዳያን ፡ ሕዝብክ ፡ ወበአንተ ፡ ቫሉሙ ፡ አለ ፡ ይሴፈዉክ ፡ ገበር ፡ ምስሌ ፤ በክመ ፣ ምሕረትክ ፣ ወሴሷ ልበ ፡ በመለኮተ ፤ ትምህርት፥ ወበለብ ፡
The Priest says: And we shall further beseech [the Lord who governs all things, Father of the Lord and of our Savior Jesus Christ] for the rains; so that He may send us His rain in the places where it is necessary, Lord who governs all, our God. The Deacon says: Pray for the rains. The Priest says: Lord, You who govern all things; we pray and beseech You to send Your rains in the places where it is necessary. Rejoice the face of the earth and irrigate its furrows. Bring [the rain] for the sowing and the harvest by Your goodness. May Your mercy come quickly before us, O Lord. The Deacon says: Let us all say. The People say: O Lord, be merciful to us. The Priest says: Toward the poor, Your people, and toward all those who hope in You, act with us according to Your mercy, and nourish our intelligence by the divinity of the doctrine and by the intelligence that proceeds from You. Through Your only-begotten Son, etc.

### V. For the Fruits of the Earth
ዘአምንቤክ ። በ8 ፡ ወልደክ ፡ በል ። ፕ በአንተ ፡ ፍሬ ፡ ምድር ። ገሬ ይ ፡ ካ ። ወካዕበ ፡ በል በአንተ ፍሬ ፡ ምድር ፡ ከ፲ ፍፃ ፤ ለምድር ፡ ያልህቀ ፡ ወያሥምር ፡ ያብጽሐ ፤ ለዘርአ ፤ ወለማአረር ፥ ዘብለ ጸጋ ፤ ይዴጉ ፤ አገዚለብሔር ፤ ለምላክ ። ይ ፡ዲ ። ጸዕዩ ፡ በአንተ ፡ ፍሬ ፡ ምድር! ። ይ ፡ ካ ። አገዚለብሔር ፤ ዘሎ ፡ ትአጎሽ ፡ ንስአለክ ፤ ወናስተበፉዐክ ። ፍሬሃ ፡ ለምድ ር ፣ ለለህቅ ፤ ወለሥምር ። ለብጽሐ ለዘርአ ፥ ወለማአረር ፡ ዘአምጎበ ፡ ቲሩትክ ፡ ፍጡ ነ ፤ ይርክበ ፤ ሣህልከ ፥ አገዚኦ ። ይዲ። ንበል ፥ል)። ይ ፡ ሕ ። አገዚኦ ተሣሀለነ ። ይ ፡ ካ ። በአንተ ፡ ነግያን ፤ ሐዝብክ ፥ ወበአንተ ፡ ክሉሙ ፡ አለ ፥ ይዴውቡ ፡ ቀዳሰ ፤
The Priest says: And we shall further beseech the Lord who governs all things, Father of the Lord and of our Savior Jesus Christ, for the fruits of the earth, so that He may make them grow | and make the fruits of the earth abundant, that He may bring them to sowing and to harvest, He who grants precious graces, the Lord, our God. The Deacon says: Pray for the fruits of the earth. The Priest says: Lord, You who govern all, we pray and beseech You for it. Make the fruits of the earth grow and make them abundant. Bring them to sowing and to harvest, by Your goodness. May Your mercy come quickly before us, O Lord. The Deacon says: Let us all say. The People say: O Lord, be merciful to us. The Priest says: Toward the poor of Your people, and toward all those who invoke Your holy and blessed name. Through Your only-begotten Son, etc.

### VI. For the Waters of the Rivers
ወቡሩክ ፡ ስመክ ። በአቶ ፡ ወልደከ ፤ በል ። ዝ፤ በአንተ ፥ ማያተ ፤ ለፍላገ ። ገሬ ይ ፡ ካ ። ወካዕበ ፡ በል ። በአንተ ፡ ማያተ ፡ ለፍላገ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያዕርገ ፡ አስክ ፡ መስፈር ተሙ ፡ ያስተፍሥሕ፤ ፡ ገጻ ፤ ለምድር ፣ ወያርዊ ፣ ትለሚፃ ያብጽሐ። ለዘርአ ፤ ወ
The Priest says: And we shall further beseech [the Lord who governs all things, Father of the Lord and of our Savior Jesus Christ] for the waters of the rivers so that He may raise them to their normal level, that He may rejoice the

face of the earth, that He may irrigate its furrows and that He may bring the fruits to sowing and to harvest, He who grants precious graces, the Lord, our God. The Deacon says: Pray for the waters of the rivers. The Priest says: Lord, You who govern all things, we pray and beseech You for it, raise the waters of the rivers to their normal level. Rejoice the face of the earth and irrigate its furrows. Multiply the wheat and bless the cycle of the year by Your own goodness. May Your mercy come quickly before us, O Lord. The Deacon says: Let us all say. The People say: O Lord, be merciful to us. The Priest says: Toward the poor, Your people, and toward all those who invoke Your holy and blessed name. Through Your only-begotten Son, etc.

### VII. For the King
ነ፲1 በአንተ ፡ ንጉሥ ። ግራ ይካ ። ወካዕበ ፡ በል ። በአንተ ፡ መፍቀሬ ፡ አገዚለብሔር ፡ ንጉሥነ ፡ ከመ ፣ መንገሥ ቶ ፥ ዘአንበለ ፥ ሐጣም ፣ በሰላም ፡ ወበጵድቀ ፣ ይዕቀብ ፡ ዘቫሉ ፡ ይአንዝ ፣ አገዚለብ ሔር ፤ ለምላክነ ። ይ ፡ዲ ሉ። ጸልዩ ፡ በአንተ ፡ ንጉሥ፤ ። ይ ፡ ካ ። አገዚለበሔር ፡ ዘሉ ‹ ትአጎዝ ፡ ንስአለክ ፤ ወናስተበዐበክ ‹ ለንጉሠ ፡ ዛቲ ምድር ፡ መፍቀሬ ፡ አገዚለብሔር ፡ ንጉሥነ ፤ ወባቀዐ ፡ ሉቱ ፡ ጸገ ፡ ለገርር ፡ ፀሮ ለሐዛበ። ዘመንገለ ፡ ዐረፍቱ ፤ ወተናገር ፡ ድድቅ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ልቡ ፥ በአንተ ፡ ቀድስት ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያንክ ፡ ፍጡ ፡ ይርክቦ ፡ ሣህልክ ፡ አገዚኦ ። ይዲ፡። ንበል ልነ ። ይ ሐ ። አገዚኦ ፡ ተሣሀሎ ፡ ለንጉሥነ ። ይ ፡ ካ ። ሀቦ ፡ ዘአንበለ ‹ ስልተት ፡ ይዕቀብ ፥ ልቡና ፡ ዘፃይማኖት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሥነ ፡ ለ ምፅኮተትክ ። በኔቶ ወልድከ ፤ በል ።
The Priest says: And we shall further beseech [the Lord who governs all things, Father of the Lord and of our Savior Jesus Christ,] for the friend of the Lord, our King N., friend of God, so that He may keep his kingdom | without suffering, in peace and in justice, He who governs all, the Lord, our God. The Deacon says: Pray for the King. The Priest says: Lord, You who govern all things, we pray and beseech You for the King of this country, friend of the Lord, our King. Help him! Grant him the grace to crush his enemies, lead the peoples toward their rest. Maintain him in his heart of justice, toward the Holy Church. May Your mercy come quickly before him, O Lord. The Deacon says: Let us all say. The People say: Be merciful to our King, friend of the Lord. The Priest says: Grant him to keep the intelligence of the faith without straying, in his piety toward You. Through Your only-begotten Son, etc. ...

VIII. For those who sleep [The Deceased]
በአንተ ፡ አለ ፤ ኖሙ ። ገ ይ ፡ ካ ። ወካዕበ ፡ ናስተበሩዓክ ፡ ለዘሹሉ ፡ ትአጎዝ ፤ ፡ አገዚለብሔር ፡ በል ። ለምላክ ፡ ዘመናፍስት ፡ ወለሉ" ፡ ዘሥጋ ፡ አገዚለ ፡ ለጋዕዝት ፤ ወለምላክ ፡ በሁዔት ፡ ዘሞተ ፡ ለፅራዕክ ፡ ወሥልጣኖ ፡ ሄድክ ፡ ሕይወተ ፡ ለትዝምደ ፡ ሰዝአ ፡ ጸነክ ፡ ዘለዕረፍት ፣ ሥ ልጣን ፡ ብክ ፣ አገዚለብሔር ፣ ለምላክነ ።
A The Priest says: And we shall further beseech the Lord who governs all things, [Father of the Lord and of our Savior Jesus Christ], God of souls and all bodies, Lord of lords and God of benefits. You who abolished death and tore away its power, You have granted life to the human race, You who have power over death, Lord, our God. The Deacon says: Pray for those who sleep.
ይዲ ። ጸልዩ ፡ በአንተ ፡ አለ ፡ ኖሙ፤ ። ይ ፤ ካ ። ለምላክ ፡ ለበዊ ፡ ወለምላክ ፡ ቀፍ4ን ፡ ዕረፍት ፡ ለጵዑራን ። በገ ፡ ዝክ ር ፤ ለስማቲሆሙ ፡ ለሙታን ፡ ለዕረርሙ፤፡ ነፍሳተ ፡ ለገብርቲክ ፡ ወለአማቲክ ፤ ክርስቶ 4ውያን ፤ ውስተ ፡ ብሔር ፡ ሥድር ፡ ውስተ ፣ መካን ፣ ሐዋዝ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ተኀጥለ ፡ ሐዘን ፡ ወናአክ ፡ ወሀብ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሕፅ ፡ ለብርዛም ፡ ይስሐቀ ፡ ወያዕቆብ ። ወሰአለሂ ፤ ይላል ዉ ፡ ናህየ ፡ ወዕረፍተ ፡ ጸጉ ። ለጽዑራን ፣ መድንን ፡ ወለዚ ፡ ለድገዝ' ፡ በዝ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ወበዘይመጽአ ። በ፪ ፡ ወልደከ ፡ በፅ ።
The Priest says: God of our fathers, and God of the Saints, rest of those who are tormented by suffering. Remember here the names of the dead. Give rest to the souls of Your Christian servants and handmaidens, who have died, in a fertile land and in a pleasant place where there is neither sadness nor groaning. And give, in the bosom of Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob, to those who lament, relief. Grant them rest, Savior of those who are tormented by suffering, and to ourselves. Save us in this world and in the one that will come. Through Your only-begotten Son, etc.
ቹ ገ ወካዕበ ፡ ናስተበፅ ፡ ዘኛሉ ፤ ይአጎዝ ፤ አገዚለብሔር ፡ ለብ ፤ ለአገዚአ ፤ ወመድ ጊ ፡ ሊየሱስ ‹ ክርስቶስ ፡ በአንተ ‹ አለ ፡ ኖሙ ፡ ለንዊ ፣ ክመ ፡ ነፍሶሙ፤ ፣ ያገዕዝ" ፥ ውስተ ፡ መካን ፡ ሥዑር ፡ ወኀበ። ፡ ማየ ፡ ዕረፍት ፡ ወያጎብር ፡ ለንሥሶ ፡ ሥጋሆሙ ፡ በዘ ባቲ ፡ ሠርፀ ፣ ዕለተ ፤ በዘሊይተሔሰው ፡ ተስፋሁ ፡ ዘበሰማያት ፡ መንገሥተ ፡ ይክፍል ። ዘለዕረፍት ፣ ሥልጣን" ፡ ቦቱ ፡ አገዚለብሔር ፡ ለምላክነ ። ይዲ ። ጸልዩ ፡ በአንተ ፡ አለ ፤ ኖሙ ። ይ ፡ ካ ። አገዚለብሔር ለምላክ ፡ ዘኩሉ ፡ ትአጎዝ ፡ ንስአለክ ፡ ወናስተበሮክ ፡ በ አንተ ፡ አለ ፤ ኖሙ ፡ ለጎዊ ፡ ክመ ፡ ነፍሶሙ ፣ ታግዕዝ ” ፡ ውስተ ፡ መካን ፣ ሥድር ፣ ወ ኀበ " ፡ ማየ ፡ ፅረፍት ፡ ወታንብር ፤ ለንሥሶ ሥጋሆሙ ፤ በዘባቲ ፡ ሠራዕከ ፡ ዕለተ ፣ በ ዘሊይትሒሰው ፡ ተስፋክ ፤ ዘበሰማያት ፤ መንገሥተክ ፤ ክፍል "” ። በአንተ ፡ ነፍሰ ፣ ገብርክ ፤ አተ ፤ አገሌ ። ይ ፡ ካ ። ማጎለፍታተ። ፡ ነፍሶሙ ፡ ገዝ ፡ ዘአንበለ ፡ ክልለት ፡ ወዘአንበለ ፡ ሕወሜ ፡ ጸ ፡ በጊዜ ፡ መፍትው ። ሥጋ ፡ ለዓቢረክ ለንሥአ ፡ዘበሰሟያት ፡ መንገሥተ ፡ክፍሉሙ”። ወአንዘ ፆ ፡ ተሐሚ ፤ ሐጫተ ፡ ሠናየ" ፡ ዘዐይን ፤ ሊርአየ ፤ ወአዝን ፤ ሊሰምዐ ፡ ውስ ተ ፡ ልበ ሰብአ ፡ ዘሊተጎለ ፡ ወሊዐርገ " ፥ መጠ ፤ ለስተዳሉክ ፡ ለንተ ፣ አገዚለብ ሔር ፡ ሰአለ ፡ ያፈቀሩክ" ።
B The Priest says: And we shall further beseech the Lord who governs all, Father of the Lord and of Our Savior | Jesus Christ, for our brothers who sleep so that He may release their souls in a place of pastures beside the water of rest and that He may gather their bodies to resurrect them on the day He has established, so that He may give as a share the heavenly kingdom to those who have not considered His promises as lying. The Deacon says: Pray for those who sleep. The Priest says: Lord our God, You who govern all, we pray and beseech You for our brothers who sleep, so that You may deliver their souls and that You may make them rest in a place of pastures and beside the water of rest and that You may gather their bodies to resurrect them on the day that You have established. Give as a share Your heavenly kingdom to those who have not considered Your promises as lying. [Let us pray for the soul of Your servant, our brother N.]. The Priest says: Grant the free passage of their souls, without hindrance and without suffering at the desirable moment. After having gathered the bodies, resurrect [them]. Give them as a share the kingdom of heaven, while You [will] rejoice [them] with great joy. All that the eye has not seen, and all that the ear has not heard, all that has not been thought in the intelligence of man, all that, You, You have prepared, Lord, for those who love You. Send to men the Peacemaker and the Comforter. Give as a share. Through Your only-begotten Son, etc. The People say: Amen!


[p. 19] {#po.33.p19}
መናህየ ፡ወመናዝዘ ፡ለሰብኦሙ ፡ፈኑ። ከዌዝክ ፡ ክፍል ፡ በ፪ ወልድክ ፡ በፅ ። ይ፡ሕ።ለሜን። ገ ይ ካ ። ወካዕበ ፡ በል ። አገዚለ ፡ ሐያዋን ፣ ሕይወተ ፡ ሙታን ፡ ተስፋ ፡ ቅቡጳን ፡ ረ ዳሌ ፡ ምንቶባን ፤ ወመንጽሔ ፡ ኃጥላን ፡ ዘሞተ ፡ ለፅራዕክ ፡ ወማአለረ ፡ ሰ ይጣን ፡ በቲክክ ፡ ሐይወተ ፡ ለትዝምደ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ጸነክ ። ቤክ ፤ ንስአል ፤ ወናስተበሩ
[O The Priest says: And we shall further beseech, etc. God of the living, life of those who are dead, hope of the discouraged, support of the afflicted, and purifier of sinners, who died. You abolished [death] and by breaking the bonds of Satan, You made the human race benefit from life. We ask and beseech You that this treasure of life which is with You, may not die eternally. For those who sleep, who have died in the faith: the first Fathers, the archbishops, the metropolitans,
ፅ፤ ፡ ዘሊተመውት ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ባለም ፡ ዘመዝገበ ፡ ሐይወት ፡ ኀቤክ ፡ ውአቱ ። በአንተ ፡ አለ ፤ ኖሙ ፡ በዛይጣኖት ፡ ለዕረፉ" ፡ ለበው ፡ ቀደምት ፡ ሊቃነ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ቋጳሳት ፤ ሊ ጴስቁጸሳት ፣ ቀሳውስት ፡ ወዲያቆናት ፡ ለናጉንስጢስ ፤ ወመዘምራን ፡ ደናገል ፡ ወመነኩ ሳት ፡ ወሕዓናት፤ ፡ ዕቤራት ፡ ወአጐለ ፡ ሟወታ ። በአንተ ፡ ነፍሰ ፡ ገብርክ ፡ አጉ ፣ አገሌ ፡ ወበአንተ ፡ ቫሉሙ ፡ እለ ፡ ዝየ ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ ዘለንተ ጸዋዕክ ፤ አምውስተ ፣ ሕዝብክ ፡ በ ጽድቀ ፡ ወበርትዕ ፡ ሐፅን ፤ ነፍሶሙ ፣ ውስተ ፡ መዛን ፤ ሥዑር ፡ ወኀበ ፡ ጣየ ፡ ዕረፍ ተ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሕፅ ፡ ለብርፃም ፡ ይስሐቀ ፤ ወያዕቆብ ። በአንተ ፡ ነፍሰ ገዝርክ ፡ አ+ነ ፡ አገሊ ። ዘለዕረፍት ፤ ሥልጣን ፤ ቦቱ ፤ አገዚለብሔር ፤ ለምላክ ። ይ ፡ዲ ። ጸልዩ ፡ በአንተ ፡ አለ ፡ ኖሙ ፡ ለበዊ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ወለበዊ ፡ ሊዲስቀዶሳት ፡ ወለበ9 ፤ ቀሳውስት ፤ ወለኀዊ ፡ ዲያቆናት ፡ ወለበዊ1 ፤ መነኩሳት ፡ ወለኀ9 ፤ ልዝ ባውያን ፡ ወለጎቲ ፡ ለንስት ፣ ክመ ፡ ያዕርፍ ፡ አገዚአ ፤ ነፍሳቲሆሙ ፣ በሰላም ፡ ወይስ ረይ ፡ለ ፡ኃጣውሊ)። ይ ፡ ካ ። አገዚለበሔር ፡ ለምላክ ፡ ዘኩ ፡ ትአገዝ ፡ ንስአለክ ፡ ወናስተበቀዐክ ፡ በ አንተ ፡ አለ ፤ ኖሙ ፡ ለገብርቲከ ፡ ወለአማቲከ" ። በአንተ ፡ ነፍሰ ፤ ገብርክ ፡ አጉነ ፡ አገ ሌ ። ፻ጊዜ ፡ በል ። ይ፤ ካ ። ወበአንተ ፡ ኩሉሙ ፡ አለ ፡ ዝየ ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ ዘለንተ ፡ ጸዋዕክ ፣ አምውስተ ፥ ሐዝብቭክ ፡ በድድቀ ፡ ወበርትዕ ፡ ለዕርፍ ፡ ነፍሶው ውስተ ፡ መካን ፤ ሥዑር ፡ ወንበ” ፡ ማየ ዕረፍት ፤ ውስተ ፡ ሐፅ ፡ ለብርዛም ፣ ይስሐቅ ፡ ወያዕቆዝ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገነተ ፡ ትፍሥ ሐት ዖ ፡ በመራሐያነ ፡ ብርሣን ፡ መላአክቲክ ። ፡ ሐፅን ፡ ነፍሶሙ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ቀፍሳኒክ ፡ ው ስተ ፡ ዘለዓለም ፡ ማጎደርክ ”* ፡ ወሥጋሆሙጊ ፡ ለንሥአ በዕለተ " ፡ ዐደምክ ፡ አንተ ፡ ባቲ ፤ ሠረዕክከ ፤ በከመ ፤ ቀቶስ ፡ ወቡሩክ ፡ ዘሊይትሔሰው ፡ ተስፋክ ፡ ዘበሰማያት ፡ መንገሥተክ ፡ ክፍሉሙ " ። አስመ ፡ ለልቦሙ ፡ ለለገብርቲክ ፡ ምት ፡ ፡ ለላ ፡ ፍልሰት ፣ ወለአዉኒ ፡ ቦ ፡ ዘለበሱ ፡ ወዘተሠወሮሙ ፡ ሕገ ሰብአ ለናሕሷ ፤ ሉሙ ። አስመ ፡ ቴር ፤ ወመፍቀሬ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ለ ንተ ፣ አገዚለብሔር ፡ አስመ ፡ ለልቦ ፡ ንጹሐ ፡ አምርስሐት ፤ በቅድሜክ ፡ ወለአዉኒ ፡ ቦ ፡ ለሐተ ፡ ሰባተ ፡ ሐይዉ ፡ በዲበ ፡ ምድር ፡ ማጎለፍታተ ፡ ነፍሶሙ ፡ ገዑዘ ፡ ዘአንበለ ፡
| the bishops, the priests, and the deacons, the readers and the psalmists, the virgins and the monks and the children, the widows and the orphans. For the soul of Your servant N. and for all those who are here, whom You have called to You, out of the people, in justice and in righteousness; nourish their souls in a place of pasture beside the water of rest in the bosom of Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob. For the soul of Your servant, our brother N. The Lord, our God, who has power over death. The Deacon says: Pray for those who sleep: our fathers the metropolitans and our fathers the bishops and our fathers the priests, and our brothers the deacons, and our fathers the monks and our brothers, the laity and our sisters the women, so that the Lord may make their souls rest in peace, and that He may forgive us our sins. The Priest says: Lord our God, You who govern all, we ask and beseech You for Your servants and Your handmaidens who sleep. For the soul of Your servant such-and-such. (Three times). The Priest says: And for those who are here, whom You have called to You, out of Your people, in justice and righteousness, make their souls rest in a place of pasture and beside the water of rest in the bosom of Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob, in the garden of joy under the guidance of Your angels. Nourish their souls with Your saints in Your eternal dwelling and resurrect their bodies on the day that You have fixed, and that You have established as holy and blessed. Give as a share Your heavenly kingdom to those who have not considered Your promises as lying. For for Your servants, there is no death, but a change of place, and if there are any who have sinned, and to whom human laws have been hidden, forgive them. For You are good and friend of man. Lord, for there is no soul absolutely immaculate before You, and if there were any who lived a single hour on earth, the passage of their souls would be free, and without hindrance. | You, grant the Comforter Spirit and send to men the Peacemaker. Comfort them and rejoice them. Through Christ, through whom, for You, with Him and with the Holy Spirit, are glory and power, now and always and unto the ages of ages.

[p. 21] {#po.33.p21}

### IX. For the Neophytes
ክልለት ‹ ለንተ ፡ ጸጉ ፡ መንፈሰ ፡ ናዛዜ ፡ ወመና?የ ፡ ለሰብሶሙ ፡ ፈኑ ፡ ናዝዞሙ ፡ ወለስ ተፍሥሖሙ ። በክርስቶስ ፤ ዘቦቱ ፥ ለክ ፣ ምስሊሁ ፣ ወምስለ ፥ ቅዱስ ፥ መንፈስ ፥ ስብ ሐት ፣ ወአጊዝ ፡ ይአዘኒ ፡ ወዘልፈ ፣ ወለዓለም ፣ ወለባለመ ፡ ባለም ። ፲ በአንተ ፡ ንሉሰ ፡ ክርስቲያን ። ገ ይ ፡ ክ ። ወዛዕበ ፡ በል ። በአንተ ፣ ለገብርቲሁ ፡ ንሉሰ ፣ ክርስቲያን ፣ ክመ ፣ ይክፍሉ ሙ ፡ በጊዜ ፥ ርትዕ ፡ ንፅበተ ፡ ገም ፡ ልደት ‹ ለስርየተ ፣ ኀጢለት ። ዘዥሉ ፡ ይአገዝ ፡ አገዚለብሔር ፡ ለምላክ ። ይ ዲ ። ጸዕዩ ፡ በአንተ ፡ ንሉሰ ፡ ክርስቲያን፤ ። ይ ካ ። እገዚለብሔር ፡ ዘዙ ፡ ትአጎሽ ፡ ንስአለክ ፡ ወናስተበሁዐክ ። ለገብርቲክ ፡ ንሉሰ ፥ ክርስቲያን ፡ ተሣሀል ። ክሎ ፡ ተረፈ ጣዖት ፡ ለርሐቀ ፡ አምኒሆሙ ፡ ሐገክ ፡ ወሥ ርዐተክ ፥ ፓድቀክ። ወስምዐክ"፣ ሚም ፡ ውስተ ፤ ዓሊናሆሙ ። ፍጡ ይርክበ፣
The Priest says: And we shall further supplicate [the Lord who governs all things, Father of the Lord and of our Savior Jesus Christ,] for his neophyte servants, that He may grant them at the time of justice, that is to say, of the ablution of the second birth, the forgiveness of sins, He who governs all, the Lord, our God. The Deacon says: Pray for the neophytes. The Priest says: Lord, You who govern all things, we pray to You and supplicate You; be merciful to your neophyte servants. Remove from them every remnant of idolatry. Place in their thoughts your Law, your ordinances, your justice, and your testimony. May your mercy quickly come before us, O Lord. The Deacon says: Let us all say.
ጣህልክ ፡ አገዚኦ ። ይ፡ዲ። ንበል። ቫል ። ይ ፤ ሐ ። አገዚኦ ፡ ተዛሀለነ ። ይ፤ ካ ። በጊዜ ፥ መፍትው ፡ ጎፅበተ ፡ ዳገም ፡ ልደት ፡ ለስርየተ ፣ ጎጢለት ፣ ማጎፈ ደ ‹ መንፈስ ፥ ቀቶስ ፣ ረስዮሙ ፡ በክርስየስ ። ዘቦቱ ፡ ለክ ፡ በፅ ። ፳ ገሬ በእንተ ፣ ሰላም ።
The People say: O Lord, be merciful to us. The Priest says: At the desirable moment of the ablution of the second birth for the forgiveness of sins, transform them into dwellings of the Holy Spirit in Christ. Through your only-begotten Son, etc.

### X. For Peace
ይ ፡ ካ ። ወካዕበ ፡ በል ። በአንተ ፡ ሰላም ፡ ለቅድስት ፡ ለሐቲ ፡ እን ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ኛሉ ፡ በተ ፤ ክርስቲያን ፡ ክመ ፡ ብዙን ፡ ሰላመ ፣ይጸ ፡ ላዕለ ፤ ፡ ቫሉ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ ወዲበ ፡ ኛሉ ፡ ወርዔት ፥ ዘለኛሉ ፥ ሰላም" ፡ ሥልጣን ፡ ቦቱ ፡ አገዚለብሔር"፣ ለምላክነ ። ይ ፡ ዲ ። ጸለዩ ፡ በአንተ ፡ ሰላም ። ይ ፡ ካ ። አገዚለብሔር ፡ ዘሉ ፡ ትአነሽ ፤ ንስአለክ ፣ ወናስተበቀዐክ ፣ ንገሠ፡ ሰ ላም ፡ ሰላመ ፡ ሀበ ፤ አስመ ፣ ዥሉ ፡ ወሀብከ ፣ ለጥርየ' ፥ አገዚለብሔር ፤ ወዕስየ አስመ ፡ ዘእንበሌክ ፡ ባዕድ ፡ ለልቦ ፡ ዘነለምር ። ስመክ፤፣ ቀዱሰ።፡ ንሰሚ ፣ ወንዴው ዕ ። ለንተ ፡ ዘአምሰጣያት ፡ ሰላመክ ፤ ክልል ፥ ላዕለ ፥ ዘቫል ፡ ነፍስ" ፣ ወንብረተዚ፤፡ በዝ ፡ ዓለም ፡ በሰላም። ጸጉ። ይ፥ ዛ ። ለንጉሠ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ምድር ፡ ሰላ ፡ ሀቦ ፡ በቀስ " ፡ ስምክ ። ሀብ ፡ ምክረ ፡ ለመኳንንተ ፣ ለሕዛብ ፡ ለገዋረ ፣ በለተ ፡ ወፀለተ ፡ በሰላም ዕቀብ ። ከመ ፡ በህቶአ ወበፅምው " ፣ ሐይወተ ፡ አንዘ ፡ ነሐዩ ፡ በሉ ፥ ለዎልኮ ፡ ሠናይ ፡ ወበፈሪሀ ዖ ፡ አገዚ ለብሔር ፣ ወበጊሩት ፡ ንትረከብ ፣ በክርስቶስ ። ዘቦቱ ፡ ለከ ፡ በል ።
The Priest says: And we shall further supplicate [the Lord who governs all things, Father of the Lord and of our Savior Jesus Christ], to grant abundant peace to the Holy, One, Universal Church, over all the people and over all the flocks, He who has his power over all peace, the Lord, our God. The Deacon says: Pray for peace. The Priest says: Lord, You who govern all things, we ask You and supplicate You, King of peace, give us peace, for You have given us everything. Make us your property, O Lord, and reward us, for we know no other [God] besides You. We shall name and invoke your holy Name. You who, from the heights of the heavens, crown the soul of each one of us with your peace, grant us that our sojourn in this world may pass in peace. The Priest says: Grant your peace to the king of this country. Give it through your holy name. Give counsel to the leaders of the peoples, our neighbors. Keep our comings and our goings in peace, so that we may pass our life peacefully and quietly in all piety, in the fear of the Lord and in goodness, and that we may be found in Christ through whom for you, etc.
08ለ7፲፲88 ፲፤፤


[p. 22] {#po.33.p22}

### I. ZA-YENAGES OF YARED
፲ ዘይነሥ ፡ ዘለቡ ፤ ያሬድ ። ፤ ግ ዘጾም ፤ ወዘክረምት ፡ ወዘለስተምህሮ ።
1. For Lent, the rainy season, and the periods of Astamehero
ይ ፡ ካ ። ወካዕበ ፡ ናለኩቶ ፡ ወንሴብሖ ፡ ለአገዚለ ፤ ኩሉ ፤ ለምላክ ፡ ለኃዜ ፡ ኛሉ ፡ ባለም ፡ ዘይነገሥ ፡ ለኩሉ ! ፡ ዘኪያሁ ፡ ይሴዝሑ ፤ ኩሉ ፡ ጎይለ ፤ ሰማያት ፤ ወይሴብሕ ዎ፡ ፡ መሳአከት ፡ ወሊቃነ ፡ መሳአክት ። ወሰከ ፡ ይትቀነዩ" ፡ ሾሉ ፡ ፍጥረት ። ኪያከ ፡ ናለዥት ፤ ወንይለክ ፤ ንቄድስ ፤ ወለስምክ ፡ ንገኒ ፤ በዥሉ ፤ ጊዜ ፣፤ ወበዥሉ ፡ ሰዓት ና ለዥተክ ፡ ወንሴብሐክ ፡ በከመ ፡ ዐቀብክ ፤ ኑን ፡ ሌሊት ፤ ወለብዳሕክነ ፤ ውስተ ፤ ብርዛ ፡ መዓልት ። ብሦዐ ፡ አገዚኦ ፡ ዛተኒ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ በሰላም ፡ ወበዳጎና ፡ ዕቀብ ፡ ዘአንበለ ፡ ኅጢለት ፡፤ ወመንሱት ፡ ዘለንተ ፡ ሥሉጥ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ምሕረት ፤ አገዚለብሔር ፡ ለምላክነ ። ይዲ። ጸሪዩ ። ይ ፤ ካ ። አወ ፤ አገዚኦሶ ፡ ለምላክ ፡ ለምላክ ፡ ልዑል ፤ ለምላክ ፣ መሓሪ ፡ ለምላክ ፤ መስተሣሀል ፡ ርሑቀ ፡ መባት ፤ ወብዙን ፡፤ ምሕረት ፤ ወጻድቀ ። ናለሦተከክ ፡ ወንሴብሐክ ፤ ዘይሴብሑክ ፡ ኮሉ ፤ ንይለ ፤ ሰማያት ፤ ወይቄድሱክ'፡ መላአክት ፤ ወሊቃነ ፡ መላአ ክተ ፡ ወለክ ፡ ይትቀሃ ፡ ኛሉ ፡ ፍጥረት ፡ ኪያክ ፡ ናለኩት ፡ ወንይለከ" ፡ ንቄድስ ፡ ወ ለስምክ ፡ ንገኒ ፡ በሉ ፥ ጊዜ ፤ ወበኩሉ ፡ ሰባት። ናለቫተክ ፡ ወንሴብሐከ ፡ በክመ ፡ ዐቀ ብክ ፤ ኑን ፡ ሌሊት ፤ ወለብዳሕከ ፤ ውስተ" ፡ ብርዛነ! ፤ መዓልት ፤ ብቱብ' ፡ አገዚዶ ፡ ዛተኒ ፡ ዕለተ ፤ በሰላም ፤ ወበዳጎና ። ዕቀብ ዘአንበለ ፤ ኝጢለት ፡ ወመንሱት ። በ3 ፡ ወልደከ ፡ በል ።
The Priest says: And we shall further thank and glorify the Lord of all things, our God, who governs the entire world, who reigns over all things, whom all the powers of the heavens glorify, whom the Angels and Archangels praise, whom all creatures serve. We thank You and we shall praise your power. We shall render humble homage to your name at all times and at every hour. We thank You and glorify You for having kept us throughout the night and for having brought us into the light of day. Grant us, O Lord, [to pass] this day in peace and in good health. Keep us without sin and without temptation, You who have power over mercy, Lord, our God. The Deacon says: Pray. The Priest says: Yes, O Lord our God Most High, merciful God, gracious God, slow to anger and rich in mercy and just. We thank You and we glorify You, You whom all the powers of the heavens glorify and whom the Angels and Archangels praise, and to whom all creatures render homage. We thank You and we praise your power and we render humble homage to your name at all times and at every hour. We thank You and glorify You for having kept us throughout the night and for having brought us into the light of day. Grant us, O Lord, [to pass] this day in peace and in good health. Keep us without sins and without temptation. Through your only-begotten Son through whom for you, with him, etc.


[p. 23] {#po.33.p23}

### II. For the Sick
፲፲ በአንተ ፡ ቶያን ። ይ ፡ ካ ። ወካዕበ ፡ ናስተበሥዖ ፡ ለአገዚለ ፡ ዥሉ፤ ፡ ለምላክ ፡ ለኃዜ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዘይ ነገሥ ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ለቡሁ ፡ ለአገዚአ ፤ ወመድኀኒ! ፡ ሊየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ሐዋ ጺፃ ፡ ለነፍስ ፡ ወመፈውሰ ፡ ሥጋ ፡ ዘያወርድ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሲኦል ፡ ወካዕበ ፡ ያፀርገ ፡ ዘ ይፌውስ ፤ ቀጥቃጤዛ ፡ ለነፍስ ፡ በአንተ ፣ ክሉሙ ፡ ቶያን ፡ ከመ ፡፤ ቫሎ"፡ ይፈውስ ፡ ወያንሥአ ። ዘውአቱ ፡ ሥሉጥ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ምሐረት ፡ አገዚለብሔር ፡ ለምላክ ። ይዲ ሉ። ጸልዩ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ቶያን! ። ይ ፡ካ ። አወ ፡ አገዚሶ ፡ ለምላክነ ፡ ለኃዜ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዘይነገሥ ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ባለም ፡ ለቡሁ ፡ ለቀቶስ ፤ ወልድከክ' አገዚአ)” ፡ ለገዌ! ፣፤ ቶያ ፤ ፈውስ ፣ ወለአለሂ ፡ ለጋንንተ " ፣ ለ ውዕአ ፡ ወለአለሂ ፤ ለርሕቁ ፡ ደዌ ፡ ናዝዝ ፤ ሕማማ ፡ ለነፍስ ፡፤ ሐውጵ ፤ ወኩሎ ፣፤ ፈው ስ ፡ አስመ ። ፡ ትክል ፤ ወጽኑዕ ፡ ለንተ ፡ ዘትገብር ፡ መድምመ ። ይ ዲ ። ተጣሀለ)” ። ይ ፡ ሕ ። አገዚኦ ፡ ተሣሀለ)” ። ሽ ይ ፡ ካ ። ዘሽወሟተ ፡ ክረምተ ፡ መፃጉፀ ፡ ለቋየክ ፡ ወለልለዛርሃ ፡ አሙታን ፡ ለንሣአክ ፤ ወለዘጸቶ ፡ ለአሙ ፡ ጸጎክ ፤ ወለዘአንተ ፤ ደም ፤ ይውሐዛ ፡ ለሐዮክ ፤ ወለሐማተ ፡ ጴጥ ሮስ ፡፤ አምፈጸንታ ፡ ለንሣአክ " ፣ ከማሁ ፡ ዲበ ፤ አለኒ ፤ ቶያን ፤ ገበር ፤ ትአምርተ ፡ ዘ ሠኖይ ፤ ወፈኑ ፤ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡፤ ነጠብጣበ ፤ ምሐረትክ ፤ አስመ ፤ ጠል ፤ ዘአምንበከ ፤ ሕይ ወቶሙ ፡ ውአቱ ፤ ክመ ፣ አሙንቱሂ ፡ ተንጂሶሙ ፡ ይሰብሑከ " ፡ ምስሌ ፤ ወናልዕል ፣ ስመከ ፡ ንቡረ ። በዋሕድ ፡ ወልድክ ፡ አገዚአነ ፡ ወመድንኒ ፡ ሊየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፤ ዘቦቱ ፤
The Priest says: And we further supplicate the Lord of all things, our God, who governs all things, who reigns over the universe, Father of Our Lord and Savior Jesus Christ; visitor of the soul and physician of the body, who brings down to Sheol and brings up from it, who heals the oppression of the soul. We supplicate Him for all the sick, that He may heal them all and set them back on their feet, He who has power over mercy, the Lord our God. The Deacon says: Pray for the sick. The Priest says: Yes, Lord, our God who governs all, who reigns over the universe, Father of your holy Son Jesus Christ, Our Lord. Heal our sick brothers and expel the demons [from their soul] and console those whom sickness has distanced. Examine the sufferings of the soul and heal [them] all; for You can [do it] and You are strong, You who perform miracles. The Deacon says: Be merciful to us. The People say: Be merciful to us, O Lord. The Priest says: You who restored life to [the man] paralyzed for 38 years, who raised Lazarus from the dead and who returned the only son to his mother, who restored life to her who suffered from a flow of blood, You who made the mother-in-law of Saint Peter rise [from] fever. Likewise, upon those who are sick, perform miracles of goodness. And send upon them drops of your mercy, for the dew that comes from You is their life, so that they, restored to their feet, may glorify You with us. And may we exalt your name together. Through your only-begotten Son, Our Lord and Savior Jesus Christ, through whom to You, with him and with the Holy Spirit, are glory and power and honor, now and always and unto the ages of ages! Amen.


[p. 24] {#po.33.p24}

### III. For Travelers
ሰከ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ወምስለ ፤ ቀቶስ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ስብሐት ፤ ወመለኮተ ፡ ወክብር ፡ ይአዚኒ ፤ ወዘልፈኒ ፡ ወለዓለመ ፡ ባለም ፤ ለሚን ። ፲፲ በአንተ ፡ አለ ፤ ይነገቶ ። ዝ ይ ካ ። ወካዕበ ፡ በል ። ዘይሁብ ፡ ፍኖተ ፡ ባሐር ፤ ወውስተ፤ ፡ ማይ ፡ ገያል ፡ ምሐ ዋረ ፤ ዘያወፅሶሙ ፡ አመዛገብቲሆሙ ፡ ለነፋሳት" ። በአንተ ፤ ክሎሙ ፡አለ ፤ ይነገቶ ፡ አለ ፤ በኩለፄ ፤ ወበዥሉ ፡ ለአለሂ ፤ ውስተ ፡፤ ባሐር ፣ ወለአለሂ ፤ ውስተ ፡ ለፍላገ ፣፤ ወ ለአለሂ ፤ ውስተ ፡ በድው ፤ ናስተበሦዐከ ፡፤ ከመ ፤ ለሹሉሙ ፡ ታብጽሖሙው ፡ ውስተ ፡ ለ ብያቲሆሙ ፡ ዘበፍሥሓ ፤ አንዘ ፤፡ ይትፌሥሑ ፤ ወያስተፊሥሑ ፡ ዘላዕለ ፤ ኛሉ ፤ ሥለጣ ን ፡ ቦቱ ፤ እገዚለብሔር ፡ ለምላክነ ። ይ ፡ ዲ ። ጸልዩ ፡ በአንተ ፡ አለ ፤ ይነገቶ ። ይ ፡ ካ ። አወ ፡ አገዚኦ ፡ ለምሳክ ፡ ለኃዜ ፡ ሉ ፡ ዘይነገሥ ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ለቡሁ ፡ ለእግዚአ ፤ ወመድጊኒነ ፡ ሊያሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፤ ኩሎው፤ ፡ አለ ይነገዱ ፡ አለ" ፡ ዘበአእ
The Priest says: And we shall further supplicate [the Lord of all things, our God who governs all things, who reigns over the universe, Father of Our Lord and Savior Jesus Christ,] who gives the path of the sea, and a road in the mighty water, who brings the winds out of their storehouses [We supplicate You] for all those who travel, in all places and everywhere, for those who are on the rivers and for those who are in the desert. We supplicate You that You may bring them all home rejoicing with joy and that they may rejoice in Him who has power over all things, the Lord, our God. The Deacon says: Pray. The Priest says: Yes, O Lord, our God, who governs all things, who reigns over the universe, Father of Our Lord and Savior Jesus Christ; we pray to You and supplicate You for all those who travel; we pray to You that You may be a guide for all.


[p. 25] {#po.33.p25}
ንቲለሆሙ ፡ ንስአል ፤ ወናስተበፉዕ ፡ ንስአለከ ፤ ክመ ፤ ለኛሎሙ ፡ ትኩኖሙ ፡ መርሐ ። ይ ፡ ዲ ። ተሣሀለነ ። ይ" ሕ ። አገዚኦ ፡ ተሣሀለ ። ይ ፡ ካ ። ለአለሂ ፤ ይነገቶ"፣ ንገድ ፤ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ ወለአለሂ ፤ በአገር ፣ የሐውሩ" ፡ ሑር" ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ። ወለአለሂ ፡ ይትመነደቡ ፤ ለብጽሖሙ ፡ ውስተ =" ፡ መርሶ ፡ መድ ቲት ሞ ፡ ለአለሂ ፡ ይሳኩዩ ፡ ምርሖሙ ፡ ፍኖተ ፤ ጽድቀ ። ወሰለአለሂ ፡ አለ ፤ ሀለዉ ፤ በ'ቢድ ” ፡ ዕቀቦሙ ፡ ዘአንበለ ፤ ማዕቅፍ ፡፤ ሀቦሙ ፣፤ ሥርዐተ ፡ መንገዶሙ ። ለኩሉሙ ፤ አለ ፤ ዘበአንተለሆሙ ፡ ንስአል ፤ ወናስተበቱዕ ፤ ሠርሖሙ ። ክመ ፡ ለንተ ፡ በአሉጊ ፡ ወ ክመ ፡ በሉ ፡ ይሰባሐ " ፡ ስምክ ፡ ቅቶስ ?" ። በፍቁቄር ፤ ወልድከ ፤ አገዚአ ፤ ወመድኀ ኒነ ፡ ሊዖሱስ ፡ ክርሰቶስ ፡ በል ። ፲ኛ
The People say: Be merciful to us, O Lord. The Priest says: For those who travel, travel with them; for those who go on foot, walk with them, and for those who are in affliction, bring them to the harbor of salvation. For those who wander, show them the path of justice, and for those who are about to travel, guard them against occasions of falling. Give them the arrangements for their journey. Assist all those in favor of whom we [pray to] You and [supplicate] You; so that through them You and your holy name may be glorified by all. Through your beloved Son Our Lord and Savior Jesus Christ, etc.

### IV. For Rain
በአንተ ፤ ዝናማት ። ይ ፡ ካ ። ወካዕበ ፡ ናስተበሦዖ ፡ በል ። አገዚኦሙ ፡ ወለምላኮሙ ፡ ለማያት ፡ ንስአለክ ፤ ወናስተበፉዐክ ፡ ክመ ፤ ፡ ትፈኑ ፤ ዝናመ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ፍና ፡ ኀበ ፤ ይተፈቀድ"፡ ለርዊ ፡ ገጻ ፡ ለምድር ፤ ወምላአ ፡ትለሚዛ ፡ወበነጠብጣብክ ትበል ፡አንዘ ፡ትትፌትሣአ ፡ሀባ ፣ ለመድር ፡ ዘርለ ፤ ወማአረረ ፡ ወለለፍላኻ ፤ ምላአ ፡ ማያተ ፤ በመዋዕለ ፤ ጊዜሁ ፡ ፍ ጹመ ። ዘላዕለ ፤ ክሉ ፤ ሥልጣን ፡ ቦቱ ፣ አገዚለብሔር ፡ ለምላክነ ። ይዲ ሉ። ጸልዩ ፡ በአንተ ፤፡ ዝናጣት ። ይ ፡ ካ ። አወ ፡ አግዚኦ ፡ ለምላክ ፡ ለኃዜ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዘይነገሥ ፡ ለሉ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ለቡሁ ፡ ለእግዚአ ፤ ወመድንኒ ፡ ሊየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። አገዚኦሙ ፣፤ ወለምላኮሙ ፡ ለማያት ፡ ንስ አለክ ፤ ወናስተበቱዐከ ፡ በአንተ ፤ ዝናማት ፤ ከመ ፤ ተሀበነ ፤ ማያተ ፤ ወትምላአ ፤ ለፍ ላገ ፤ ለስተፍሥሐ ፤ ገጻ ፡ ለምድር ፤ ወለርዊ ፡ ትለሚዛ ፡ ሀባ ፤ ፍሬ ፤ ለዘርአ ፡ ወለማአ ረር ፤ ወባርክ ፤ ለክሊለ፡ ዓመተ ፡ ምሕረትከ ፤ በአንተ ፡ ነዳያን ፣ ሕዝብከ ፤ ወበአንተ ፣ ዥሉሙ ፡ አለ ፤ ይሴፈዉክ ፤ ወኩሉ ፤ መካ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ይትፈቀድ ፡ ሀብ ፡ ጸጋክ ። ይ፡ዲ ። ተሣሀለነ ። ይ፤ ሕ ። አገዚኦሶ ፡ ተሣሀለነ ። ይ ፡ ካ ። ለዝዞሙ ፡ ለደመናት ፡ ያውርቶ፣ ፡ ዝናመ ፡ ዘቀናሚ ፡ ወዘደኃረ ፡ መሐር ፡ ለገብርቲክ ፤ ወለአማቲክ ፤ ወተመየጥ ፡ ነያ ፤ ወምስኪናነ። ዕቤሬተ ፡ ወአጓለ ፤ ማ ውታ ፡ አስመ ፡ ኩሉሙ ፡ኀበቤክ ፡ ይጸርሑ ፡ ኪያክ ፤ መሴስየ ፡ ክሉ ፤ ይትመሀለሉ ፡ ወይ
The Priest says: And we shall further supplicate [the Lord of all things, our God who governs all things, who reigns over the universe, Father of Our Lord and Savior Jesus Christ. - Lord and God of the waters, we ask You and supplicate You to send rain where it is needed. Irrigate the face of the earth and water its furrows and by your showers, it will germinate in joy. Give it to the earth for sowing and harvest; and fill the rivers with water on the days when its time is fulfilled. You who have power over all things, Lord, our God. The Deacon says: Pray. The Priest says: Yes, Lord, our God, who governs all, who reigns over the universe, Father of Our Lord and Savior Jesus Christ. Lord and God of the waters, we ask You and supplicate You for the rains so that You may give us waters and that You may fill the rivers. Rejoice the face of the earth and water its furrows. Give it fruits for sowing and harvest and bless the crown of the year with your mercy for the poor of your people and for all those who hope in You; and give your grace wherever it is needed. The Deacon says: Be merciful to us. The People say: Be merciful to us, O Lord. The Priest says: Command the clouds to bring down the rain, the former and the latter. Have mercy on your servants and your handmaids and turn to the poor and the needy, the widows and the orphans, for all cry out to You who nourish all those who implore You and pray to You, dispenser of all things, be merciful to all and satisfy them so that they may [ensure] your glorification unceasingly in body and in spirit. Through your only-begotten Son Our Lord and Savior Jesus Christ, through whom, etc.


[p. 26] {#po.33.p26}

### V. For the Fruits of the Earth
ስአሉ ፡ ኪያክ ” ፡ መጋቤ ፤ ሹሉ ፤ ኩሉሙ" ፡ ተሣሀል ፤ ወለጵገብ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይክሀሉ ፤ ዘበሥጋ ፡ ወዘበመንፈስ ፡ ዘልፈ ፤ ሰብሖተክ ። በዋሕድ ፡ ወልድከ ፡ በል ። ሃ በአንተ ፡ ፍሬ ፡ ምድር ። ይ ፡ ካ ። ወካዕበ ፡ በል ። በአንተ ፡ ፍሬ ፡ ምድር ፡ ክቭ ፡ ፍሬዛ ፡ ለምድር፤ ፡ ያልህቀ ፡ ወያሥምር ፡ ወይዕቀዝ ፡ ዘአንበለ ፡ ሙስና ፤ መሴስየ ፡ ኛሉ ፡ ዘሥጋ ፡ ዘብቡለ፡ ጸጋ ፡ ይ
The Priest says: And we further supplicate [the Lord of all things, our God, who governs all things, who reigns over the universe, Father of Our Lord and Savior Jesus Christ,] for the fruits of the earth, that He may make the fruits of the earth grow, that He may make them abundant and preserve them safe from corruption.
ጹጉ ፤ አገዚለብሔር ፡ ለምላክ! ። ይዲ ። ጸልዩ ፤ በአንተ ፤ ፍሬ ፡ ምድር ። ይ ፡ ካ ። አወ ፡ አገዚኦ ፡ ለምላክ ፡ ለኃዜ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዘይነገሥ ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ለቡሁ ለአገዚአ ፤ ወመድንኀኒ ፤ ሊየሱስ ፤ ክርስቶስ ። ንስአለክ ፤ ወናስተበቱዐከ ፤ በአንተ ፤ ፍ ሪ ፡ ምድር ፡ ፍሬዛ ፤ ለምድር ፡ ለልህቀ ፤ ወለሥምር ፡ ወዕቀዝ ፡ ዘአንበለ ፡ ሙስና ፡ አ ገዚለብሐር ፤ ለምላክ ። " ይ ዲ ። ተሣሀለነ ። ይ ፡ ሕ ። አገዚኦ ፡ ተሣሀለ ። ይ ፤ ካ ። አስመ ፡ ለንተ ፤ ዘታሠርጵ ፡ ሣዕረ ፡ ለአንስሳ ፡ ወሐመልማለ ፡ ለቀ፤ ፡ አጓ ለ ፡ አመሕያውክ ፡ አስመ ፡ ዐይ ፡ ሹሉ ፡ ነፍስ ፡ ይሴፎ ፤ ኪያክ ፡ ለንተ ፡ ትሁቦሙ ፣ ሲ 4ዮሙ ፡ በጊዜሁ ፡ ትሰፍሕ ፤ የጣነክ ፤ ወታጸገብ ፡ ለኩሉ ፤ አንስሳ ፥ ዘበሥርዐትክ ። በ
Nourish all flesh, may the Lord, our God, grant a generous grace. The Deacon says: Pray for the fruits of the earth. The Priest says: Yes, O Lord, our God, who governs all, who reigns over the universe, Father of Our Lord and Savior Jesus Christ; we ask You and supplicate You concerning the fruits of the earth. Make the fruits of the earth grow, make them abundant and preserve them safe from corruption, Lord, our God. The Deacon says: Be merciful to us. The People say: Be merciful to us, O Lord. The Priest says: For it is You who make the grass grow for the animals, for the use of humans. For the eye of every soul hopes in you, it is You who give them their food in due time. You extend your right hand and you satisfy all animals according to your good pleasure. Through your beloved Son, our Lord and Savior.


[p. 27] {#po.33.p27}

### VI. For the Waters of the Rivers
ፍቁር ፤ ወልድክ ፤ አገዚአ ፡ ወመድቴኒ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ዘቦቱ ፡ ለክ ፡ በል ። ሃ፲ በአንተ ፡ ማያተ ፡ ለፍላገ ። ይ ፡ ካ ። ወካዕበ ፡ ናስተበሥ።ዖ ፡ በል ። በአንተ ፡ ማያተ ፡ ለፍላግ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያዕርግ ፡ አስ ከክ ፡ መስፈርቶሙ ፡ አስመ ፡ ለንተ ፡ ዘትገዝር ፡ ሠናያተ ፣ ወታርሩ ፡ ክረምተ-፤ ፡ ወትፈኑ ፡ ዝናማተ ። ለስተፍሥሕ ፡ ገጻ ፡ ለምድር ፤ ወለርዊ ፣ ትለሚፃ፤ ፡ ለብጵሐ ፡ ለዘርአ ፡ ወ ለማአረር ፡፤ ዘሥሉጥ ፡ ለንተ ፡ ላዕለ ፤ ምሕረት ፣፤ አገዚለብሔር ፡ ለምላክነ ። ይ ፡ ዲ ። ጸለዩ ፡ በአንተ ፡ ማያተ ፡ ለፍላገ ። ይ ፡ ካ ። አወ ፡ አገዚዶ ፤ ለምላክ ፡ ለኃዜ ፡ ሹሉ ፡ ዘይነገሥ ፡ ለሉ ፡ ባለም ፡ ለቡሁ ፡ ለአገዚአነ ፤ ወመድቴኒነ ፡ ሊየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ንስአለክ ፤ ወናስተበቀዐክ ፡ በአንተ ፡ ማ ያተ ፡ ለፍላገ ፡ ዘለፍላገ ፡ ማያተ ፡ ለዕርገ ፡ አስክ ፡ መስፈርቶሙ ፡ ለዝዞሙ ፡ ለደመ ናት ፡ ያውርቶ ፡ ዝናመ ፡ ወምልሶሙ ፡ ለለፍላገ ፤ ማያተ ፤ በዐቅሞሙ ። በጊዜ ፣፤ መፍት ው ፡ ለርዊ፤ ፡ ገጻ ፡ ለምድር ፡ ወምላአ ፡ ትለሚዛ ፡ ለብዝጎ ፡ አክለ ፡ ወባርክ ፡ ፀውደ ፡ ባመት ፡ በዘዚለክ ፡ ጊሩት ። ይ ፡ ዲ ። ተሣሀለነ ። ይ ፤ ሐ ። አገዚኦ ፡ ተሣሀለነ! ። ይ፤ ካ ። አስመ ፡ ለንተ ፡ ዘተገብር ፣ ሠናያተ ፡ ወታርጉ ፣ ክረምተ ፡ ወትፌፈኑ ፡ ዝናማ ተ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ገዳ ፡ ለምድር ፡ በአንተ ፡ ለትክልት ፤ ወጵጊያት ፡ ወፍሬያተ ፡ ምድር ። አስመ ፡ ለንተ ፤ መሴስይ ፤ ለሉ ”" ፡ ዘሥጋ ፡ ወለክ ፡ ይደሉ ፡ ስዝሐት ፡ ምስለ ፡ ወልድክ ፡ ፍ ቁርክ ፤ ሊየሱስ ፤ ክርስቶስ ። ዘቦቱ ፡ ለክ ፤ በል ። ዝ
The Priest says: And we further supplicate [the Lord of all things, our God who governs all things, who reigns over the universe, Father of Our Lord and Savior Jesus Christ,] for the waters of the rivers, that He may make them rise to their [normal] level; for it is You who do good, who open the rainy season and who send the rains. Rejoice the face of the earth and water its furrows. Bring about the sowing and the harvest, You who are powerful in mercy, O Lord, our God. The Deacon says: Pray for the waters of the rivers. The Priest says: Yes, O Lord, our God who governs all things, who reigns over the universe, Father of Our Lord and Savior Jesus Christ. We pray to You and supplicate You for the waters of the rivers. Make the waters of the rivers rise to their [normal] level. Command the clouds to bring down the rain and fill the rivers with water to their measure. At the appointed time, water the face of the earth and fill its furrows. Multiply the food and bless the cycle of the year in your goodness. The Deacon says: Be merciful to us. The People say: O Lord, be merciful to us. The Priest says: For it is You who do good, who open the rainy season and who send the rains upon the face of the earth for the plants and the flowers and the fruits of the earth. For You nourish all flesh and to You belongs glory with your beloved Son Jesus Christ, through whom, etc.

### VII. For the King


[p. 28] {#po.33.p28}
በአንተ ፤ ንጉሥ ። ይ ፡ ካ ። ወካዕበ ፡ ናስተበቃዖ ፡ በል ። አገዚኦሙ ፡ ለለጋዕዝት ፣ ወንጉሦሙ ፣ ለክሥ
The Priest says: And we shall further supplicate [the Lord of all things, our God who governs all things, who reigns over the universe, Father of Our Lord
ት፤ ፤ በእንተ ፤ መንገሥተ ፤ ገብርክ ፤ መፍቀሬ ፤ አገዚለብሔር ፡ ንጉሥነ ፡ ክመ ፣ መንገ ሥቶ ፡ ይዕቀብ ፡ በጵድቀ ፡ ወበሰላም ፡ መዋሊ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ጸብአ ፤ አገዚለብሔር ፡ ለ ምላክነ ። ይዲ ። ጸሪዩ ፡ በአንተ ፡ ንጉሥ ። ይ ፡ ካ ። አወ ፡ አገዚዶሶ ፡ ለምላክ ፡ ለኃዜ ፡ ዥሉ ፡ ዘይነገሥ ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ዓለም ፤ ለቡ ሁ ፡ ለአገዚአነ ፡ ወመድጌቴኒነ ፡ ሊየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፤ አገዚኦሙ ፡ ለለጋዕዝት ፡ ወንጉሥሙ ፡ ለክበሥት ፡ ንስአለክ ፤ ወናስተበቱዐክ ፤ በአንተ ፤ መንገሥተ ፡ ገብርክ ፤ መፍቀሬ ፤ አገዚ ለብሔር ፡ ንጉሥነ ፤ በሰላም ፣፤ ወበጽድቀ ፡ ዕቀቦ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዝንተ ፡ ዐይን ፤ ወበጽላሉተ ፤ ክነፊክ ፤ ክድኖ ። ይ፡ዲሉ፡ ተሣሀለ ። ይ ፡ ል ። አገዚኦሶ ፡ ተሣሀሉ ፡ ለንጉሥነ ። ይካ ። ለገርር ፡ ሉቱ ፡ ፀሮ ፡ ታሐተ ፡ አገሪሁ ፡ ክወ ፡ ብዙን ፡ ሰላቭ ፡ ጸገዖዎ ፡ በ ዥለፄ ፤ ወበሉ ፣ በሐውርት፣ ፡፤ ለዝዙን ፤ መዋዕል ፡ ወለጉንቶይ ፡ ባመታት ፡ ወለነዋ ን ፡ ክራማት ፡ ለተድላ ፣ ወለዳጎና ፤ ዚለ ፡ ወስኮ ፡ ለሚነ ፤ ወናይማኖተ ፡ ተናገር ፣ ውስ ተ ፡ ልቡ ፤ በአንተ ፤ ለብያተ ፡ ክርስቲያናቲክ ፡ ሀቦ ፡ ሰላመ ፡ የጎሊ ፤ ሠናየ፡ በአንቲ ለ ፡ ወበአንተ ፡ ሹሉ ፡ ሕዝብከ " ፡ ክመ ፡ ንሐኳ ፡ ዕሩፈ ፡ ነሀሉ " ፣ በሰላም ፣ ወንሐየ ው ፡ በፈሪሀ ፤ አገዚለብሔር ፡ ወበቲሩት ፡ ንትረክብ ። በፍቁር ፡ ወልድከ ፡ አገዚአነ ፤ ወ መድጉኒነ ፡ ሊየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ዘቦቱ ፡ ለክ ፡ በል ። 'ሃ፲፲፤
and Savior Jesus Christ,] Lord of lords, King of Kings for the kingdom of your servant, friend of the Lord, our King N., that you may keep his kingdom in justice and in peace, O victor of all battles, Lord, our God. The Deacon says: Pray for the King. The Priest says: Yes, O Lord, our God, who governs all things, who reigns over the entire universe, Father of Our Lord and Savior Jesus Christ. Lord of Lords and King of kings, we pray to You and supplicate You for the friend of the Lord, our King N. Keep him in peace and in justice like the apple of the eye and under the shadow of your wings place him under cover. The People say: Be merciful to us, O Lord. The Priest says: Subject his enemies under his feet, so that one may enjoy abundant peace everywhere and in all countries, for many days, many years, and long winters. For our prosperity and our personal salvation, increase in him confidence and faith. Maintain [it] in his heart concerning your churches. Give him peace so that he may have good thoughts concerning us and concerning your people, so that we may be tranquil, in peace, and that we may live in the fear of the Lord and that we may acquire goodness. Through your beloved Son, Our Lord and Savior Jesus Christ through whom, etc.

### VIII. For Peace
ገ በአንተ ፡ ሰላም ። ይ ፡ ካ ። ወካዕበ ፡ ናስተበቀዖ ፡ በል ። በአንተ ፤ ሰላም ፡ ለቅድስት ፡ ለሐቲ ፡ አንተ ፡ በዥለሄ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፤ ክመ ፡ ብዙን ሰላመ ፡ ይጸጉ ፡ ላዕለ! ፤ ክሉሙ ፡ ኖሉት ፡ ወለሐዛብ ። ውአቱ ፡ ወዛቤ ፡ ሰላም ፡ አገዚለብሔር ፡ ለምላክነ ። ይ ፡ ዲ ። ጸልዩ ፡ በአንተ ፡ ሰላም ። ይ ፡ ካ ። አው ፡ አገዚኦ ፡ ለምላክ ፡ ለኃዚ ፡ ሹሉ ፡ ዘይነገሥ ፡ ለሉ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ለብሁ ፤ ለአገዚአነ ፡ ወመድጊነ ፡ ሊየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ንስአለክ ፤ ወናስተበቱዐክ ፤ በአንተ ፣ ሰ ላም ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ሰላም ፡ ሰላመከ ፡ ሀበ ፡ አስመ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ወሀብክ ለጥርየ ፤ አገዚለ ብሔር ፡ ወዕስ. ፡ አስመ ፤ ዘአንበሌክ ፡ ባዕድ ፡ ለልቦ ፡ ዘነለምር ፤ ስመከ ፤ ቀቶሰ"፡ ንሰሚ ፤ ወንዴውዕ ፡ ለንተ ፡ ዘአምሰማያት ፡ ሰላመከ ፣ ፈኑ" ፤ ውስተ ፥ ለልባቢ)፤ ለ ል ። ይ ፡ ዲ ። ተሣሀለነ ። ይ ፥ ሕ ። አገዚኦ ፡ ተሣሀለ)ጾ ። ይ ፥ ካ ። ወንብረተዚ ፡ በዝ ፡ ዓለም ፡ በሰላም ፡ ጸገወነ ፡ ለንጉሠ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ምድር ፡ ሰላ መ፣፤ የገሊ”። በአንቲለ)ሾ ፣ ወበአንተ ፤ ጅሉ ፤ ሕዝብክ ፤ መኳንንተ ፤ ወለሐዛበ ፤ ለ ገዋሪ ፡ በለተ ፡ ወፀለተ፤ ፡ በሰላም ፡ ዕቀብ ፡ ከመ ፡ በዓና ፡ ወበዕረፍት ” ፣ ሐይወተ ፡ አንዘ ” ፡ ነሐዩ ፤ በሉ ፡ ለምልኮ ፡ ሠናይ ፡ በፈሪሀ ፡ አገዚለብሔር ፤ ወበጊሩት ፤ ንት
The Priest says: And we shall further supplicate the Lord of all things, our God who governs all things, who reigns over the universe, Father of Our Lord and Savior Jesus Christ, concerning peace for the holy, one, universal Church, that He may grant abundant peace to all pastors and to all peoples, He who is the giver of peace, the Lord, our God. The Deacon says: Pray for Peace. The Priest says: Yes, O Lord our God, who governs all things, who reigns over the universe, Father of Our Lord and Savior Jesus Christ, we pray to You and supplicate You concerning peace. King of peace, give us your peace, for You have given us everything. Take possession of us, Lord, and reward us, for we know no other Lord besides You. We shall pronounce your holy name and invoke You. You, from the heavens, send your peace into each of our hearts. The Deacon says: Be merciful to us. The People say: O Lord, be merciful to us. The Priest says: Grant us to dwell in peace in this world. May the king of this land have peaceful thoughts concerning us and concerning all your people. Keep our leaders and the peoples, our neighbors, our comings and our goings in peace, so that we may pass our existence in good health and in rest while we live in all things in faithful service and the fear of the Lord, and that we may be found in goodness. Through your only-begotten Son Our Lord and Savior Jesus Christ, through whom to You with him and with the Holy Spirit, are glory, power and honor, now and always and unto the ages of ages. Amen!


[p. 29] {#po.33.p29}

### THE TEACHING OF THE MYSTERIES
ረክብ ። በዋሕድ ፡ ወልድክ ፡ አገዚአ ፤ ወመድኀኒነ ፡ ሊየሱስ ፤ ክርስቶስ ፤ ዘቦቱ ፡ ለ ክ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ወምስለ ፡ ቀቶስ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ስብሐት ፤ ወመለኮተ ፤ ወክብር ፣ ይአዜ ኒ ፥ ወዘልፈኒ ፤ ወለዓለመ ፡ ባለም ፡ ለጫን ።
WHICH MUST BE SAID BEFORE THE PROSPHORA FOR THE FAITHFUL


[p. 30] {#po.33.p30}
ረ ን ት ንተዘር ፡ ለዎአመጩናን-። ል ዘቀድመ ፡ ሀሉ ፤ ወይጻሉ ፤ ዘመጽለ ፤ ዘሐመ ፡ ወሞተ ፡ ወተቀብረ ፡ ወተን ሥለ ፤ ጻዕረ ፤ ዘሞት ፤ ፈትሐ ፤ ወዘአምውታን ፡ ተንሥለ ሊኮነ ፡ ባሕቲቱ ፤ ከዊኖ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ዘአመንፈስ ፤ ቀቶስ ፤ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለለም ፡ ምስለ ፡ ነፍሱ ። ዘሊንደገ ፡ ነፍሶ ፡ ው ስተ ፡ ምት ፡ ዘበመንፈስ ፡ ለለፃም ፡ ጸወኖ ፣ ወለሐየዎ ፡ ። ዘለዝሶ ፡ ለለናም ፡ መዊቶ ፡ ሐይወ ፡ ዘዐርገ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ ተዋረዶ ፡ ለሞት ፣፤ ወአምድነረ ፡ መስቀል። ሞለ በቲኮ ፥ ማአሰሮ ፡ ለሞት ፤ በአንተ ፡ ዘተጎየለ ፥ ዲያብሉስ ፡ ትካት ። በዝንቱ ፡ በአንተ ፡ ሕማሙ ፡ ለርለየ ፡ ሙስናሁ ፡ በቲኮ ፡ ሕምዘ ፡ ንይሉ ፤ ሠጠጠ ፡ መሥገርቶ። ወገጹ፣ ዘፍጹም ፡ በጽልመት ፡ ፈርሀ ፡ ወደንገፀ ፡ ዲያብሉስ ፡ ርአዮ ፡ ብሑተ ፡ ልደት ፤ በሥጋ ፡ ለምላክ ፤ በሲኦል ። ወረዶ ፡ ዘአምልዑላን ፤ ሰማያት ፡ ዘሊይትገገር ፡ ወሪዶ ፡ ዓሊናሁ ፡ ዘሊይትክፈል ፡ በለሐቲ ፤ ምክር ፡ ምስለ ፤ ለቡሁ ፡ ሰማያተ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ምስለ ለቡሁ ፡ ለክሊሎሉሙ ፡ ለመላአክት ። ወለሊቃ ፤ መላአክት ፡ ኀይል ፤ ወለንጎያላን ፤ ዐድፍ ፤ ወለለ ጋዕዝት ፤ መንፈስ ፡ ዘአምቀድመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ መንገሥቱ ፡ ሰፋኒት ፡ ዘቀቶ4ን ፣ ዕዘዝ!። ዓ ሊናሁ ፡ ለለብ ፡ ዘይትረክብ ፤ ውአቱ ፤ ጥበቡ ፡ ለለብ ፡ ውአቱ ፡ ኀይሉ ፥ ውአቱ ፡ የማ ኑ ፡ ውአቱ ፡ ምክሩ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ጎሊናሁ ፡ አደ ፡ መዝራዕቱ፤ ፡ ዘለብ ፡ ክዌኖ ፡ አንክ ። አ ንዘ ፡ ነለምን ፤ በዛይጣኖቱ ፡ ንገኒ ፤ ከመ ፣ ውአቱ ፤ ብርዛን ፣ ጎፅበት፣0 መድጊት ፤ ምስ ዋር” ፡ ረድሌት ፡ ወመምህር ። ዘይትቃውም ፡ ለ ፡ ዘየዐሲ ፤ ተወካሬ ፡ መጽንዒ" ፡ ቀ ጽርነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኖላዊ ፡ ዣጎት ፡ ለንቀጸ ጆኖ ፡ ፍኖት ፡ ሕይወት ፡ ፈውስ ፡ ሲሳይ ፡ መስ ቴ ፥ መኩንን ። ነለምን ፡ ሐማም ፡ ለዘሊየሐምም ፡ ዘተወልደ ፡ በሥጋ ፡ ዘሊይትወለድ ፡ መ ዌዊቶ ፡ ሐያው ፡ ወልደ ለዝ ፡ ወተገምረ ” ፡ ዘሊይተገመር ። ዘጎጢለተ! ፡ ሃሥለ ፡ አንዘ ፡ ለልቦ ፤ ኅጢለት ፡ መዊቶ ፡ ሐይወ ፣ ወአምሰማያት ፡ አምንበ ለብ ፤ ለይጎለ ፡ በሕማሞ ተ ፡ ሥጋሁ በዘወ ወደሞ ፡ ክቡረ ፡ መንፈሰ ፡ ሐይወት ፣ ወቀድሳተ ፡ መንፈስ ፣ ወሀ በ ፤ ወማየ ፤ መንጽሔ ፤ ዚለ)። ዘያበርህ ፡ ውስተ ፤ ለልባቢሆሙ ፡ ለአለ ፤ ይፈርህዎ ፡
He who was before, who is, who came, who suffered, died, was buried, and rose again, has untied the sufferings of death, and has risen from the dead. He was not only man, He who, through the Holy Spirit, received the flesh of Adam with his soul, who did not leave his soul in death, who through the spirit protected and vivified Adam. He who clothed himself in dead Adam lived, He who ascended to the heavens, having descended for death; and after the Cross, He conquered, having broken the bonds of death, thanks to which the demon had formerly prevailed over us. Because of that, thanks to his Passion, He showed us the annihilation of the devil. Having broken the venom of his power: he tore his nets. The devil, whose face was in complete darkness, was afraid and was terrified. He saw the only-begotten Son, God incarnate, in Sheol. Having descended from the high heavens, the ineffable having descended, He whose thought is indivisible. By a unique design with his Father, He made the heavens with his Father, crown of the Angels, power of the Archangels, garment of the Powers, spirit of the Dominations, prince of a kingdom that existed before the world, strength of the Saints, inaccessible intelligence of the Father. He is the wisdom of the Father, his power, his right hand, his counsel, the hand, the arm of the Father. We believe and humbly confess that He is the Light, the ablution of salvation, the refuge and the Doctor who protects us, He who rewards and who welcomes, the support of our wall. He is the Shepherd, the Door, the Portal, the Way, the Life, the remedy, the food, the drink, the judge. We believe in the Passion of Him who is impassible, of Him who was born in the flesh, who was not born, being dead living, Son of the Father. He was contained, He who cannot be contained, He who took our sins, while being without sins. Being dead, He lived. From the heavens, from beside his Father, He rushed down. Through the sufferings of his flesh, He redeemed us. He gave us his glorious blood, spirit of life and holiness of the spirit, instrument of our purification. It is He who illuminates the hearts of those who fear Him, for He is with them forever. He made us strangers to all the ways of Satan, renewer of souls, this God in whom we all trust. Having existed before the world, with the Father, He saw the world that was perishing in the bond of sin. Through the power of
