አስመ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ ለዝሉፉ ፡ ሀሉ ፡ ዘረሰየ' ፡ ነኪሬ ፡ አምሉ ፤ ፍኖተ ፣ ሰይጣን ፤ ሐ ዳሴ ፡ ነፍሳት ፤ ዘቦቱ ፡ ሹል ፤ ንትዌክል ፤ ዝንተ ፤ ለምላክ ። ከዊኖ ፤ አምቀድመ ፡ ዓ ለም ፡ ምስለ ፡ ለብ ፡ ርአዮ ፡ ለባለም ፡ በማአሰረ ፤ ኀጢለት ፡ አንዘ ፡ ይትሀጐል ። በጎይ ለ"። ጥበቡ ፤ ለርዌ ፤ ይትከየድ ፤ በሊያአምሮ ፡ ገጋይ ፤ ለሞት ፡ ተቀንየ ። ፈቂዶ" ፡ ይፈውስ ፤ ትዝምደ ፡ ሰብአ ፤ ተገምረ ፤ በማሐፀ ፡ ድንገል ፤ ተንቢኦ ፤ ለኩሉ ንይፅ ፡ አለ ፤ በሰማያት ፣ ማጎደሮ ” ፡ ወአለ ፤ ይትቃወማ ፡ ንይል ፤ በሊያአምሮ ፡ ሰወሮሙ ፡ ወ ሥጋ” ፣ ለብሰ ፥ ዘይማስን ፤ ዘሊይማስን ኝ። ለሥጋ ፡ መዋቲ ፡ ረሰዮ ፣ ዘሊይማጣስን ፡ በ ዘለርለያ ፤ ሥጋ ፤ ዘመዋቲ ፡ ለም ፡ ለርለያ ፡ ዘሊይጣስን ፡ ዘቦቱ ፤ በለርለያ ። ፣ ዘይ ማስን ፤ ተነሥተ =። ወትአዛዘተ ፤ ድድቅ ፡ በወንጌል ፥ መጠወ ፡ ዘውአቱ ፣ ጸዋዒ ፡ ዘበ መንገሥቱ ፡ ንሕይው ኖ ፡ ተመሂረ ፡ ዘበውአቱዖ ፡ ወንጊል ፡ ማአሰረ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ተበ ትክ ክመ ፡ አሞት ፣ ሐይወተ ፡ ንትካፈል ፡ ወአም ፡ ሊያአምር ፡ ልቡና ፡ ንንሣእ ። ውአ ቱሂ ፤ ክዊኖ ፡ ሰብለ ፡ ወልደ ፡ አገዚለብሔር ፡ ለምላክ ፣ ዘተወልደ ፤ አምለናም ፣ መዋ ቲ ፡ በለምሳሉ ፡ ተሰዊጦ ፡ ነሥለ ፤ ዘቀፃሚ ፡ ለተወልዶ ፡ ተፀንሰ ፤ ብአሲ ። ዘውአቱ ፡ ለምላክ ፤ ዘበለማን ፡ ዘበነቢያት ፤ ለቀድመ ፡ ተዐውቀ ፡ ወበሐዋርያት ፡ ተሰብከ ፤ ወአመ ላአክት ፣ ተለተ ፥፤ ወአምንበ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ተሰብሐ ። ተሰቀለ ፤ በአንቲለ! ፥ ወበመስቀሉ ፡ ኮ ፤ ሕይወተ! ፣ ጽንዕነ ፡ ቤዛነ ፣ ዘውአቱ ፡ ምሥጢር ፡ ጎቡአ ፣ ፍሥሓሐ ፤ ዘሊይትዌዳአ ፡ ዘቦቱ ። ኩሉ ፡ ነፍሰ ፡ ፍትወተ ፡ ሰብአ ፤ ያርሕቅ ፡ አምእገዚለብሔር ፡ ሊይክል ፡ ኪያሁ” › አንዘ ፤ ትጸውር ፡ ወትረ ። ዘይአቲ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ፍቅር ኖ ፣ አገዚለብሔር ፤ ሊትሴስል። ወበአለ ፤ ክናፍር ፡ ጾታ ፡ ነጊር ፡ ሊይትክሀል ” ፡ ጥንቁቀ ፡ ዘአምተካት ፡ ንቡለ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ይ አዜሰ ፤ ክሠተ ምሥጢረ ፡ ኮነ ፥ ለምአመናን ፤ ለኮ ፡ ክመ ፡ ይትረለይ ፡ ለላ ፡ ክ ወ ፡ ይተንለይ ፡ ውአቱ ፡ ዝንቱ ፣ መስቀል ፡ ወቦቱ ፡ ንትሜካሕ ፡ ክመ ፡ ንሰብሖ ። ወቦቱ ፡ አለ ፤ ይጠመቁሂ ፡ ምአመናን ፡ ፍጹማን ፡ አምኩሉ ፡ ዘተነገረ ፡ ዘያስተርሊ ፡ ዘሊኮነ ፡ በ ለጣን ፤ ለድ“ ፡ ነፍሶሙ ። በዝንቱ ፡ አለ ፤ ተጸንዑሂ ፣ ሕቱ ፤ ርአሰክሙ ፣ ለጽምሙ ፡ አ ዘኒክሙ ፤ ወአለ ፡ ይትረለይ ፡ ወለድሩ ለዕይንቲክሙ ፡ አለ ፡ በገዛድ ። ክመ ፡ ታአምሩ ፡ፈ ቃዶ ፡ ለክርስቶስጆ ፤ ወኩሉ ፥ ምሥጢረ ፤ መድቴትክሙ ፣ ብአሲ ፤ ወለንስት ፤ ቀቶሳ ንጆ ፡ አለ ፥ ዘሀሎክሙ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ተመክሖ ፡ በእገዚብሔር ። ለፅምሉጆ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ውስጡ ፡ ለሰብአ ። ክርስቶስ ፡ ፀመደ ፡ ኀበጆ ፡ ለቡሁ ፡ ለቀረበ ፡ መጺኦ ፡ አምድንረ ፡ ሐመ ፡ ወ ወረዶ ፡ ውስተ ፤ ሲሶል ፡ ዘውአቱ ፡ ነፍሰ ፡ ምውታን ፣ ፄወወ ፡ ለሐይወተ ። ዘረአዮጆ ፡ ጦት ፡ አንዘ ፡ ይወርድ ፡ በሥጋ ፡ ጌገየ” ፡ ወለምሰሉ ፡ ወቦቱ ፡ ክመ ፡ ያለምድ ፡ ውጊ ጠ ፡ ርአዮጆ ፡ በላዕሊሁ ፥ ጫነ ፡ መለኮቱ ፡ ጸርን ፤ በቃል ፡ ዐቢይ ፡ አንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ መኑ ፡ ውአቱ ፡ ዝንቱ ፤ ዘአምሥልጣን' ፡ ሰብለ ፡ ለቢሶ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ሞለኒ። መኑ ፡ ውአቱ ፡ ዝንቱ ፤ ዘአምሥልጣንየ ፡ ተውህቦ ፡ ሥጋ ፡ ለሀጐል ፡ ይምሥጠኒ ። መኑ ፡ ውአቱ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዘምድረ ፤ ለብሰ ፡ ውአቱኬ ፡ ሰማያዊ ፤ ውአቱ ። መኑ ፡ ውአቱ ፡ ዝንቱ ፤ ዘበሙስና ተ
his wisdom; so that the Beast might be trampled underfoot, He submitted to death although He had ignored sin. Wishing to heal the human race, He was contained in the womb of the Virgin, hidden from all the armies that have their dwelling in the heavens. The adverse powers that stood against him, He protected himself from them by leaving them in ignorance. He clothed himself in corruptible flesh, He who is incorruptible. He made the mortal flesh incorruptible, He who by appearance was mortal Adam. The incorruptible appearance that he possessed was abolished by a corruptible appearance. He gave commandments of justice through the Gospel, He who invites us, after having taught us that, to live in his kingdom; and through this Gospel, the bonds of the Devil were broken, so that we might receive life as a share rather than death and that we might take intelligence instead of ignorance. This one having become man, He, Son of the Lord God, who was born of mortal Adam, took his appearance, He who is the first to be born was conceived as man. He who is God in truth, who was announced by the prophets, who was preached by the Apostles, who was the object of thanksgiving on the part of the angels and glorified by all. He was crucified because of us and on his Cross is our life, our strength, our redemption. This is a hidden mystery, joy whose content does not run dry. No desire of man can distance the soul from God while it always carries it. This is glory. Love does not distance from God. And by these carnal lips, the mode of expression cannot be precise for these things that were formerly hidden. But now they have become a mystery revealed to the believers, not so that it be seen, but so that it be meditated upon. This is the Cross in which we glory in order to glorify it. And those who are baptized by it are perfect believers: they have saved their soul from all that has been said, from all that appears, but does not exist in truth. You who strengthen yourselves by it, examine yourselves. Make your ears deaf and for the things that are visible, blind your eyes, to what is in evidence, so that you may know the will of Christ and all the mysteries of your salvation. Men and women, saints, you whose property it is to glory in the Lord, lend an ear to this, the interior of the man. Christ has placed us under his yoke: he has brought us closer to his Father, after having come among us. After He had suffered, having descended to Sheol, he who made captive for life the souls of the dead. Death saw him descend in the flesh. He misled it. And it assimilated him to what it was accustomed to devour. It saw the beauty of his divinity, it cried out with a loud voice and said: "Who is this who, after having clothed himself in man, has snatched him from my dominion and has conquered me?". Who is this who has snatched from my dominion the flesh that was given to me for perdition? Who is this who has clothed himself in earth, while he is celestial? Who is this who was born in corruption and who is incorruptible? Who is this who is a stranger to my Law? Who is this who has seized what belongs to me? Who is this who fights death by the power of the flame of fire? Who is this who has conquered the darkness? Who is this, this new glory, by the presence of whom I cannot do what I want? Who is this who, without sin, is dead? Who is this who by the abundance


[p. 32] {#po.33.p32}
ወልደ ፤ ወውአቱሰዎ ፣ ሊይማስን ። ወኑ ፥ ውአቱ ፤ ዝንቱ ፤ ዘነቪር ፡ አምሕገየ። መኑ ፤ ዝንቱ ፤ ዘአምዚለየ ፤ ይፄውው ” ፡ መኑ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዘበንይለ ‹ ነደ ፡ አሳት ፥ ምስለ ፡ ሞት ፤ ይትባለስ ። መኑ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዘሞለ ፤ ለጽልመት ፡ መኑ። ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፤ ሐዲስ ፡ ዘበ ለርለያዝ ፡ ተክልለኒ ፤ ገቢረ ” ፡ ዘአፈቀድ ። መኑ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዘአንበለ ፡ ጎጢለት ፡ ዘሞ ተ ፡ መኑ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዘበብዙን ፡ ብርዛኑ ፡ ለጽልመት ፡ ለዖሮ ። ዘሊንደገኒ ፡ ለአሊለየ ፡ አስፍን ፡ ለላ ኛሉ ‹ ለንበ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ ይስሕብ ፡ አለ ፡ ተውህኳኒ ፡ ነፍሳተ ። መኑ ፡ ዝን ቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፤ ዘይክልአ ፥ ሥጋ ፤ ሊትማስን ። መኑ ዝንቱ ፡ ዘለኪፎቶ ፤ ሊይክል ፡ ም ንተኑ ፡ ዘየዐውዶ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ዘሊይትሐተት ፡ ተሀጐልኩ ፡ አምዝ “ ፡ ወአምአሊለሁ ፡ ዘለማ ስን ፤ ለልብየ ፡ ዘበላዕሌሆሙ ። ዝንቱ ፡ ውአቱ ፡ ሊየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ዘተሰቀለ ፡ ዘቦቱ ፡ ፀጋማይ ፡ ያማና" ፡ ኮነ ፡ ወታሐታይ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዘላዕሉ ፡ ወደኃሪ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዘቀዯሚመ። አ ምውታን ፡ ተንሚኦ ፡ ወሲኦለ ፡ ከይዶ ፡ በሞቱ ፡ ለሞት ፡ ደምሰሶ ፤ አምድኅጎረ ተንሥለ ፡ አምውታን” ፣ በሥልስት ፡ ዕለት ፤ ለአኩቶ ፤ ለለብ ፡ አንዘ ፤ ይብል ፤ ዘለምላክ ፤ ቃል ፤ ጥአጡአ ፡ ክመዝ ። ላለቨተከ ፡ ለብ ፡ ለኮ ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ ክናፍር ፡ ውቶዳት ፡ ወለኮ ፡ በል ፡ ሥጋ ፡ ዘባቲ ፡ ጽድቀ ፡ ወሐሰት ፡ ይወፅአ ፡ ወሊበዝቃል” ፡ ዝዘበኪነ ፡ ነፍስት ፤ ግብር ፡ ይተገበር ፡ ለላ ፤ በዝኩ ፡ ቃል ። ላለኩተከ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ዘብክ ፡ ክሉጆ ፡ ይትዐወቀ ፡ አንተ ፡ ሊትትዐወቀ ፣ ለንተ” ፣ ሊትመጽአጆ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መናፍስት ፡ ዘሥጋ ። ዘበአዝነ ፡ ሥ ፤ ሊየጎልፍ ፡ እንተ ፤ በዓለም ፤ ሊሀለወት ፤ ወበምድር ፤ ሊትትኀደገ ፡ ለላ ፡ በዝ። ፡
of his light has blinded the darkness? who does not let me dominate my own, but draws toward the heavens the souls that had been given to me? What is this glory that prevents the flesh from being corruptible? Who is this whom I cannot touch? Who is this whom this glory surrounds which is unfathomable? Through him, I perish and to do harm to his own, I possess nothing. This is Jesus Christ, who was crucified, by whom the left has become the right, that which is below like that which is above, the last like the first. Having risen from the dead, and having trampled Sheol underfoot, having erased death by his death, after he had risen the third day, He gave thanks to the Father by saying: "the word of God is fulfilled. I give You thanks, Father, not with these fashioned lips, nor with this tongue by which truth and falsehood come out, nor by this word by which the work of artifices is accomplished, but by this Word. I give You thanks, King, by this Word which is in You, is as to all, which is unknowable and does not come from a carnal organ, which does not pass through ears of flesh, which does not exist in this world and which does not dwell on earth; but I give You thanks by this Word which is above us, that the Father uses for You alone. You, whom the Father loves, You whom He glorifies, You in whom is this dwelling of all the perfect saints, You, they call You friend, Father, the Dispenser of goods, the help, God of all, for to You is everything, everything is in You, that which is for You, - There is no other thing that is not of You: You alone who exist in the ages of ages. May the Shepherd therefore know the spirits of mystery of every nature, after having prayed to the Father. So that you may know and that you may see, I will ascend to heaven, said Jesus Christ.

And it is fitting, because of that, that the Shepherd say the exposition of the hidden things, so that you may know with whom you are in communion, with the holy mysteries and of whom you make the commemoration in the thanksgiving. And that he further expound by saying: "We, my brothers, let us take refuge in Him: let us take Him alone whose gift we have learned. He who spoke to give us what the eye does not see, what the ear does not hear: what has not been imagined in the intelligence of man. What God has prepared for those who love Him as Moses said to the other prophets. Trusting in Him, let us give the glorification. To Him, glory and power in the ages of ages. Amen!

[A] 1. First chant. Alleluia to the Father! Alleluia to the Son! Alleluia also to the Holy Spirit! Celebrate [this] feast in joy up to the horns of the altar: this Passover of the first Law. John was baptizing at AEnon, near Salim beyond the Jordan and Jesus Christ is the light of Justice. Feast of St. John the Baptist (1st Maskaram) [G] 2. Alleluia to the Father! Alleluia to the Son! Alle, Alleluia also to the Holy Spirit! He named Light in the darkness him who was born of a woman. [Having] known him from his mother's womb, the son of the barren woman adored the Son of the Virgin Feast of St. John the Baptist [G] 2bis The heavens celebrate the feast, the clouds celebrate the feast, and the earth celebrates Easter, having been washed by the blood of Christ. Feast of Easter. [G] 2ter Come, let us rejoice today, on this day of the feast of the Cross. The Cross is the tree of life; the Cross is the tree of salvation. Feast of the Cross - (17 Maskaram)

[G] 12. Alleluia to the Father! Alleluia to the Son! Alleluia to the Holy Spirit! We shall glorify and we shall sing in psalms Him who built His Sanctuary in the highest of heavens.

Lent, Monday II, Terce. [G] 14. Alleluia to the Father! Alleluia to the Son! Alleluia also to the Holy Spirit! A fleeting dwelling like a shadow and like the garment of falsehood: today man, and tomorrow dust! Lent, Friday III, None (Mehellä) (SD no. 876). [G] 15. Alleluia to the Father! Alleluia to the Son! Alleluia also to the Holy Spirit! Priest, chief of priests who saw the support of the lamp. Priest, chief of priests. Entirely of gold, its walls are [made] of precious stones. Priest, chief of priests. Zion! The Holy Church! Priest, chief of priests, Zechariah saw the son of Barachiah. Feast of Zechariah (8 Maskaram). [G] 16. Alleluia to the Father! We shall glorify the good news that the angel Gabriel announced to him. Alleluia to the Son, to the Holy Spirit! Rejoice with joy, he said to him, having whispered in his ear all the words that [God] had sent him. Feast of Gabriel (19 Tähsäs). [G] 17. Alleluia, Alleluia to the Father! We shall glorify. Alleluia to the Son! Alleluia, alleluia also to the Holy Spirit! Joy has spread over the whole earth. The Savior is born, the honor of the Saints, who will reign over the house of Jacob. Feast of Christmas (29 Tähsäs). [G] 18. Alleluia to the Father! We glorify the pure crown upon their head, like the rainbow at the time of the rainy season. Alleluia to the Son! We shall sing. Alleluia also to the Holy Spirit! We shall glorify and we shall sing the Savior. Like the percussion of the harp and the organon is the glory of these priests. Feast of the Priests of Heaven (24 Hedär).

ዛሌ ፡ ሉያ ፡ ለለዝ ፡ ገ ፡ ለለምላክ ፤ ለማልክተ ፡ ዛሊ ፡ ሉያ ፡ ለወልድ ፤ ዝ ገነ ፡ ለአገዚለ ፤ ለጋዕዝት ፤ ዛሌ ፤ ሉያ ፣ ወለመንፈስ ፡ ቀቶስ ። አስወ ፡ ውአቱ ፡ ለምላክ ፥ ምሕረት ፤ ዘይሴብሐዎ ፡ ለቃነ ፤ መላአከት ። ትጉዛን ፡ አለ ፤ ሊይነውሙ ፡ ዛሌ ሉያ ፥ ለለብ ፤ መላአክት ፤ በበሥርዐ ግ ቶሙ ፡ ዛሊ ሉያ ፡ ለወልድ ኪያክ ፡ ለብ ፡ ይሴብሑ ፡ ዛለ ፡ ሉያ ፡ ለመንፈስ ፡ ቀዱስ ፡ ወለክ ፡ የዐርጉ ” ፡ ለኩቴተ ። ዛሌ ፥ ሉያ ፥ ለለብ ፤ ዘልዑል ፤ አምሰማያት ፤ ወንጹሐ ፤ አምንጢለት ፡ ዛሌ ፡ ሉያ ፡ ለወልድ ፤ አገዚለብሔር ፡ ዘበለማን ፡ ዘየዐርፍ ፤፡ መልዕፅተ ፤ ለርያም ፡ ዛሊ ፤ ሉያ ፥ ወለመንፈስ ፤ ቀቶስ ። ሰለፅዎ ፤ ለሚቫሌል ፡ አስ ተብድጽዕዎ ፡ ቫሉሙ ፡ አለ ፥ ውስተ ፡ ደይን ። ዛሌ ፤ ሉያ ፤ ለለብ ፤ ይትዐይን ፤ መልለክ ፤ አገዚለብሔር ፡ ዛሌ ፡ ሉያ ፡ ግ ለወልድ ፤ ዐውዶሙ ፡ ለአለ ፤ ይፈርህዎ ፤ ወያድጎኖሙ ፡ ዛሊ ፡ ሉያ ፡ ወለ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቀዱስ ። መላአክተ ፤ ለሐዛብ ፡ ተጋብሉ ፡ ምስለ ፤ ለምላክ ፡ ለ ብርፃም ። ዛሌ ፡ ሉያ ፡ ለለዝ ፡ ረኪቦሙ ፤ ሐፃነ ፡ ዛሊ ፡ ሉያ ፡ ለወልድ ፣ ረኪቦሙ" ፡ ዝ ሕፃነ ፡ ዛሌ ፤ ሉያ ፤ ወለመንፈስ ፥ ቀቶስ ። ረኪቦሙ ፡ ሕፃነ ፡ ፡ ያበ ውሉ ፡ ቀርቫ ። ዛሌ ፤ ሉያ ፡ ለለብ ፡ ዛሊ ፡ ሉያ ፤ ለወልድ ፡ ዛሊ ፡ ሉያ ፣ ወለመንፈስ ፡ ግ ቀቶስ ፤ ይቤ ፥ አስጢፋኖስ ለንሰ ፤ ርሊኩ ፣ ወልድ ፡ ይነበር ፡ በየጣኑ ፤ ምክር ፣ ወሰላም ፣ ማአከክለ ፤ ፪ሆሙ ፡ ይቤ ፤ አስጢፋኖስ ፡ ለንሰ ፡ ርሊኩ ፣ ወልደ ይነብር ፤ በየጣኑ ። ዛሌ ፤ ሉያ ፤ ለለብ ፤ ተበሀሉ ፤ በበይናቲሆሙ ፡ ዛሌ ፤ ሉያ ፤ ለወልድ ፤ ግ ወይቤላ ፤ ሕዓን ፤ ለአሙጁ ፡ ባሌ ፤ ሉያ ፤ ለመንፈስ ፤ ቀቶስ። ጥብባኪ ፡ አም ፡ ዮም ፡ ንፈጽም ፡ ገድለ ። ሃሌ ፤ ሉያ ፡ ለለብ ፥ ለዘዐርገ ፣ ውስተ ፣ ሰማያት ፡ ባሌ ፡ ሉያ ፡ ለወልድ ፡ ቭ ዛሌ ፡ ሉያ ዛሌ ፥ ሉያ ፤ ወለመንፈስ ፣ ቀስ ። ተኩዙ ፤ ሉቱ ፡ መላአክ ተ ፡ ሹሉ ፡ ጎይል ፡ ወሉ ፡ ፍጥረት ።
[G] 19. Alleluia to the Father! Render humble homage to the God of gods. Alleluia to the Son! Render humble homage to the Lord of lords. Alleluia also to the Holy Spirit! For He is the God of mercy whom the Archangels glorify. [6] 20. The vigils that do not sleep. Alleluia to the Father! [These are] the angels who, according to their respective functions. Alleluia to the Son! Glorify Thee, O Father! Alleluia to the Holy Spirit! And offer up to Thee thanksgiving. [G] 21. Alleluia to the Father! who is higher than the heavens and pure from [all] sin. Alleluia to the Son! Lord who in truth rests above the highest of heavens. Alleluia also to the Holy Spirit! Pray to Michael, proclaim him blessed, all you who are in [eternal] punishment. Feast of St. Michael (12 Hedär) (?) [G] 22. Alleluia to the Father! The angel of the Lord will encamp. Alleluia to the Son! Around those who fear Him and He will save them. Alleluia also to the Holy Spirit! The princes of the nations have gathered with the God of Abraham (Lent, Friday III, None (Mehellä) no. 680). [G] 23. Alleluia to the Father! Having found the Child. Alleluia to the Son! Having found the Child. Alleluia also to the Holy Spirit! Having found the Child, they offered [Him] gifts and presents. Feast of Christmas (29 Tähsäs). [G] 24. Alleluia to the Father! Alleluia to the Son! Alleluia also to the Holy Spirit! Stephen said: "I have seen the Son sitting at His right hand." Counsel and peace were in their midst. Stephen said: "I have seen the Son sitting at His right hand." Feast of Saint Stephen (1st Ter). [G] 25. Alleluia to the Father! They spoke among themselves. Alleluia to the Son! And the child said to his mother. Alleluia to the Holy Spirit! Dare then, mother. Let us accomplish our combat today. Feast of Qirqos (15 Ter). [G] 26. Alleluia to the Father! to Him who ascended to the heavens. Alleluia to the Son! Alleluia, alleluia also to the Holy Spirit! The angels, all the powers, and all creatures are subject to Him.

2፲. ዛሌ ፤ ሉያ ፤ ለለብዝ ፤ ዛሌ ፤ ሉያ ፡ ለወልድ ፡ ዛሌ ፤ ሉያ ፤ ወለመንፈስ ፣ ቀቶስ ። ለዋልደ ፤ ንገሥት ፣ ቀቶሳት ፡ ለንስተ ፡ አለ ሖራ ፣ ከመ ፡ ይትቀ ባላ ፤ መርዓዌ ፤ ብአሴ” ፡ ንጉሠ ፣ ሰማያዌ ፤ ርሱየ ፤ ዘይለብስ ፡ ጽድ ቀ ፡ ወይትዐጸፍ ፡ ብር ፡ ቦለ ፡ ውስተ ፍሥሐ ፡ ዚለሁ ። ዛሌ ፥ ሉያ ፤ ለለብ ፡ ንሴብሐክ ‹ ዛሌ ፡ ሉያ ፤ ለወልድ ፡ ወንዜምር ፡ ለክ ፡ ዝ ዛሌ ፤ ሉያ ፤ ወለመንፈስ ፤ ቀቶስ ። ለክሊሉሙ ፡ ለሰማዕት ፡ መጽለ ፡ ን በ ፡ ዐሳዩ ፤ ልይወት ። ዛሌ ፥ ሉያ ፤ ለለ ፥ ዛሌ ፡ ሉያ ፡ ለወልድ ፡ ዛሊ ፡ ሉያ ፡ ወለመንፈስ ፡ ቀ ዝ ቶስ ። ዓሉ ፡ ተቀበሉ ፥ መርባዊ ፡ ሰማያዊ ፡ ዘተፈ)ወ ፡ አምኀበ ፡ ለቡሁ ፡ ለንሶሰወ ፤ ወለስተርለየ ፥ በመንፈስ ” ፡ ቀዱስ "" ።
[G] 27. Alleluia to the Father! Alleluia to the Son! Alleluia also to the Holy Spirit! The daughters of the queen, the holy women who went to meet the Bridegroom, a man, a heavenly king adorned, who had put on justice, and having wrapped himself in light, he entered into his joy. [G] 28. Alleluia to the Father! We shall glorify Thee. Alleluia to the Son! And we shall sing psalms. Alleluia also to the Holy Spirit! The crown of the Martyrs, the Rewarder of Life, has come to us. [G] 29. Alleluia to the Father! Alleluia to the Son! Alleluia also to the Holy Spirit! Go out, go to meet the heavenly Bridegroom who was sent from his Father. He walked and appeared in the Holy Spirit.

Alleluia also to the Holy Spirit! Justice is your testimony and your works are true. By your command were instituted the dawn and the morning. Lent: Wednesday IV, Terce (SD no. 1047). [G] 32. The wood of the Cross that the Jews threw on Golgotha has been found today. Feast of the Discovery of the Cross (10 Maggäbit).

[A] 37. Alleluia to the Father! The Cross illuminates everything. Alleluia to the Son! In the heavens, on high. Alleluia also to the Holy Spirit! And the Cross of the Son of the Father who reigns on earth in order to illuminate the nations. Feast of the Discovery of the Cross (10 Maggäbit). [A] 38. Alleluia to the Father! By His Cross, we were marked. Alleluia to the Son! By His cross, we were redeemed. Alleluia also to the Holy Spirit! By His cross, it became our life. Feast of the Discovery of the Cross (10 Maggäbit). [A] 39. Alleluia to the Father! Michael, that is to say, an extraordinary word! Alleluia to the Son! Gabriel, that is to say, the Son of the Lord. [A] 43. Alleluia to the Father! Alleluia to the Son! to the Holy Spirit! Its structure and its foundations are more pleasant, its throat more exquisite than clusters of grapes. [A] 44. Alleluia to the Father! Alleluia to the Son! to the Holy Spirit! Pleasant and pretty, white and brilliant is our mother, Zion. [A] 45. Alleluia to the Father! Alleluia to the Son! to the Holy Spirit! The structure and the foundations of Zion! I, I love them. Lent: Tuesday VII, Terce. [A] 46. Alleluia to the Father! Alleluia to the Son, to the Holy Spirit! Unique Trinity for the priests of heaven. Their union is like the emerald and their garments white as snow, said the Son of thunder. Feast of the Priests of Heaven, 24 Hedär. [A] 47. It is built of sapphires and emeralds. Alleluia to the Father! A rampart surrounds it. It shines more than the stars. Alleluia to the Son! Alleluia also to the Holy Spirit! Lent: Monday VII, Terce.

[E] 49. Alleluia to the Father! Alleluia to the Son! Alleluia also to the Holy Spirit! We shall glorify and sing psalms. Glory to the highest of heavens! Glory to Him who separated the light. He showed the wounds of his hands to his disciples after He had risen from the dead. They saw Him and adored Him, as God He who, thanks to death, received a youthful vigor. [E] 1. Holy and blessed, glorious and blessed, honored and exalted; gate of light, ladder of life and dwelling of the divinity. You are the holy of holies, O Our Lady, generator of God, Virgin Mary. You are called the good pleasure of the Father, and dwelling of the Son and overshadowing of the Holy Spirit. O blessed more than all creatures. You are like the empyrean of the heavens, you who were the empyrean on earth. The holy prophets, the priests and the kings made in your likeness the holy of holies, and in it the tables of the Covenant. May your Son give us his clemency as a share. Pray for us, O Holy One. R. Because of the Incarnation of the Son of God, through you and in you, we have become closer than the earth, to the dwelling that is in the empyrean. Through you and through the name of your Son, we have become close.
ጺ በአንተ ፡ ተሠግዎቱ ፣ ለወልደ ፤ ለምሳክ ፡ አምኔኪ ፡ ወብኪ፡ ቀሩባነ ፡ ኮነ ፤ አምድ ር ፡ ለማጎደር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ለርያም ፤ ብኪ ፡ ወበስመ ፡ ወልድኪ ፡ ቀሩባ ፡ ኮነ ። ዷ ዐሠርቱ ፡ ቃላት ፤ አለ ፥ ተጽሕፋ ፡ በለዳብዒሁ ፡ ለአገዚለ፤ዥሉ ፡ ፍጥረት ፡ ንደረወል ደ ፡ ለምላክ ፡ ላዕሌኪ ፡ ለብ ፡ በየማኑ ፣ ክደነኪ ፤ወቀቶስ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ጸለለኪ ፤ ወንይለ ፡ ል ዑል ፣ ለጽንዐኪ ፤ ወሊየሱስ ፡ ለብሰ ፣ ሥጋ ፡ ዚለኪ ፥ክልሐ ፥ ወይቤ ፡ ዘጸምለ ፥ይምጳእ ፡ ንበየ ወይስተይ ፤ በክመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ መጽሐፍ ፤ ለፍላገ ፡ ማየ ፡ ሕይወት ፡ዘይውሕዝ ፡ አምክር
2. Ten words which were written by the fingers of the Lord of all creatures. The Son of God dwelt upon you. The Father covered you with his right hand. And the Holy Spirit took you under his shadow And the power of the Most High strengthened you. And Jesus clothed himself with your flesh. He cried out and said: "Let him who is thirsty, come | to me and let him drink” As the Scripture says: "rivers of water of life will flow from his bowels” May your Son give us his clemency as a share. Pray for us, O Holy One.

3. The Father first explained to us down to the iota the ten words which were written by his fingers. The Lord, your Son, Our Lord and Savior Jesus-Christ taught us that You alone, You are his Father and that, He alone, is a Son for You. Thus, we believe that he has no mother in the heavens nor Father on earth, He who descended from the heavens without anyone knowing his coming, if it were not his Father alone and the Paraclete, Holy Spirit of truth. He dwelt in your womb and you carried him nine months. The heavens rejoiced and the earth was in gladness by the birth of your Son. The Angel announced the joy. And the army of the heavens Glorified Him by saying: "Glory to the Lord in the heavens and peace on earth to men, objects of his benevolence” And the shepherds in Bethlehem were in admiration of what they had seen and heard. The Magi, having seen the star, came

from a distant land to adore your Son and render you homage. This star which led them from the East and brought them to Bethlehem, stopped above You, where you were, you with your Child. And these Magi having seen that, rejoiced with a great joy. They entered to Him, stood before Him and fell to the earth, and adored Him and opened their treasures and offered him gifts: gold, myrrh and incense And they said: "We have brought You a gift. And we offer You incense because of your glorious name, O our God; because of the sin of your people, so that you may accept his prayers and his sacrifices, to You who came to forgive him his sin. May your Son give us his clemency as a share. Pray for us, O Holy One.

4. You are purer than the pure, O chosen Virgin, who remained in the Temple, like a tabernacle made of incorruptible wood, adorned with pure gold and covered with precious stones, with pearls; which illuminates, whose price is considerable. Thus you remained | in the Temple and the Angels brought you your food daily. Thus you remained twelve years receiving consolations from the Angels. Your drink was the drink of life. And your food was the heavenly bread. David, your father, psalmodied, on the lyre, and sang a psalm by saying through the prophetic Spirit: "Listen, my daughter, and see, and lend your ear. Forget your people and your father's house, for the king has desired your beauty; for he is your Lord. And you will prostrate yourself before him” May your Son give us his clemency as a share. Pray for us, O Holy One.

5. Gabriel, Angel among the pure Angels, exempt from the corruption of the ancient Angels, who stands before the Lord of the universe, rejoiced you with good news and said to you: "Rejoice, O joyful one. The Lord is with you. You are blessed among women. You have found grace with the Lord of the universe. And behold, you will conceive and give birth to a Son and you will name him by the name of Jesus. He will be great and will be named Son of the Most High. And the Lord God will give him the throne of David, his father. And he will reign over the house of Jacob eternally. And his reign will be without end. The Holy Spirit will come upon you. And the power of the Most High will take you under its shadow. And he who will be born of you will be Holy and he will be named the Son of the Lord of the universe." May He who is born of you give us his clemency as a share. Pray for us, O Holy one.

6. You are she who was the palace of holiness, the sanctuary of the Holy of Holies, the veil of light, and the throne of ineffable glory. We compare you, O Holy Mary, to the sanctuary of the holy of holies, because of the Holy Spirit who came upon you and the power of the Most High who took you under its shadow, and to the veil of light, for the Word of the Father, Jesus Christ, our Savior, hid himself

in your womb, the Lord who is above the universe, whom the Angels, Archangels, Thrones and Powers, Dominations and Virtues, Principalities and Judges glorify. And when you gave birth to your Son on earth, in the manger, the many-eyed Cherubim and the six-winged Seraphim | girded you with a crown and clouds of light enveloped you. The Archangels and the armies of Angels and the hosts of heaven stood before you with fear and trembling. And there, the Cherubim and Seraphim praised you with another glorification that was not of the number of their heavenly glorifications nor of their ancient glorifications. And when they saw their Lord, who gathers all creatures, dwelling in your womb, He who nourishes all creatures, sucking your milk like a little child, they sought Him in the highest of heavens and there they found Him with his Father and the Holy Spirit, as before. And when they saw the humility of Our Lord, they lifted their eyes toward the empyrean. They spread their wings and glorified their Lord saying: "Glory to the Lord of the universe in the heavens." And they saw you again in the cave, you alone with your child, they said: "Peace on earth." And when they saw that he had clothed himself in our flesh which he had received from you, they prostrated themselves saying: "Peace to men, objects of his complacency." The Prophet David prophesied in the Psalm saying: "From heaven, the Lord of the universe looked down; from the palace he prepared for himself, he saw all the children of men." The King desired your beauty and said: "This is the place of my rest for eternity. It is there that I will dwell, for I have chosen it." May your Son give us his clemency as a share. Pray for us, O Holy one.


[p. 72] {#po.33.p72}

7. We compare you, holy and blessed, glorified and blessed, honored and exalted, to the golden urn in which the bread of life descended from the heavens, bread which gives life to all those who believe in Him and who eat of it in faith and with a grateful heart in the company of those who stand at his right hand. May your Son give us his clemency as a share. Pray for us, O Holy one.
ጺ ለንቲ ፡ ውአቱ ፡ ተቅዋም ፡ ዘወርቅ ፡ዘሊገብራ ፡ አደ፡ኬንያ ፤ ዘሰብአ ፡ወሊያጎትዉሾ ፡ ውስቴታ ፤ ማጎቶተ ፡ ለላ ፡ ለሊሁ ፡ ዝርኝ ፡ ለብ ፡ ዝርፃዛን ፡ ዘአምዝርፃን ፡ መጽለ ፤ ንበኪ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ መልዕልቴኪ ፡ ወለብርሀ ፡ በመለኮቱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ለጽናፈ ፡ ዓለም ፤ ሰደደ ፤ ጽልመተ ፡፤ አምላዕለ ፤ ሰዝአ ፤ ወለድንክ ፡ በቃሉ ፡ ማሐየዊ ፡ አንዘ ፡ ይብል ፤ ለ ፤ ውአቱ ፡ ብርኝ ፡ ለዓለም ፡ አመኑ ፡ በዝርዛኑ ሞ ፡ ወለንሶስዉ ፡ አንዘ ፡ ብክሙ ፤ ብ ርፃነን ። ወልድኪ ፤ ሣህሉ ፡፤ ይክፍለነ ። ሰለሊ ፡ ለነ ፤ ቀድስት ።
8. You are the golden candelabrum which the hand of a human craftsman did not make and upon which one does not light a lamp. But this candelabrum is the light of the Father, light of light. This one came toward you and remained upon you, and illuminated by its divinity all the ends of the earth. | It chased away the darkness from above men and saved us by its life-giving voice saying: "I am the light of the world. Believe in his light and walk while the light is in you." May your Son give us his clemency as a share. Pray for us, O Holy one.


[p. 73] {#po.33.p73}
9. Lord of all, Lord of all; light of light, true Lord of true Lord of all, who was born and was not made, consubstantial,

to his Father, by whom all things are in heaven and on earth, who, because of us men, and because of our salvation, descended, became man and was incarnate of the Holy Virgin Mary. And by his coming, || illuminated us and announced to us joy and gladness, and brought us closer to his Father, and led us on the path of life and gave us eternal life through faith in him. The Prophet Isaiah was astonished in the prophecy by the commandment of Jesus and said: "The light is your commandment on earth." The Priest Zechariah, a just and pure old man who fulfilled the commandments of the Lord of the universe, was astonished, struck with stupor, opened his mouth and said: "In the clemency and mercy of our God who watches over us from the height of the heavens: He has risen to show his light to those who were sitting in darkness and in the shadow of death. He has directed our feet on the path of peace to lead us." May your Son give us his clemency as a share. Pray for us, O Holy one.
ናስተማስለኪ ፡ ሶአገዝአትነ” ፡ በማዕጠንት ፤ ዘወርቅ ፤ ዘውስተ ፤ አደዊሆሙ ፡፤ ለሊ ቃ ፡ ቫህናት ” ፡ ሰማያውያን ፡ አለ፤ይክወኑ ፡ ጸሉተ ፤ ሎሙ ፡ቅቶሳን ፡መዛይምናን” ፡ አምዲበ ፡ ምድር ፡ በውስተ ፡ ማዕጠንቶሙ ፡ ከማሁ ፡ በስአለተ ፡ ስምኪ ፡ ያዐርጉ ፡ ስ አለቶቀሙ ፡ ለአጓለ ፤ አመሕያው ፡ ውስተ ፡ ማጎደረ ፤ ሥሉስ ፡ ቀቶስ ፡ ለዝ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ወ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፤ ወታስተስርዩ ፤ ኀጢለተ ፡ ሕዝብኪ ፡ ተበውሐ ፡ ለኪ ፤ አምለዝ ፡ወ ወልድ ፤ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቀቶስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ተኩኒ ፡ ተንከተመ ፡ ለውሉደ ፡ ሰዝአ ፤ ለሕይወት ፡ ዘለባለም ፣ ለኪ ፡ ይደሉ ፤ ክመ ፣ ትኩኒ ፥ መድ-ኺቶሙ ፣ ለመፃይምናን ፣ ሕዝብኪ ፤ ኦመ ድኽተ ‹ ሹሉ ፡ ዓለም ። ወልድኪ ፡ ሣህሉ ፡ ይክፍለ ። ሰለሊ ፡ ለ ፡ ቅድስት ።
10. We compare you, O our Sovereign, to the golden censer which is in the hands of the heavenly archpriests who have the prayer of all the Saints and of all the believers who are on earth, in their censers. Likewise, by the invocation of your name, they make the prayers of the children of men rise into the dwelling of the thrice-Holy: Father, Son and Holy Spirit; so that you may ask for pardon for the sin of your people. || was granted to you, from the Father and the Son and the Holy Spirit, that you be for the children of men, the bridge [leading] to eternal | life. It is fitting that you be for us, the salvation of the believers, your people. O Salvation of the whole universe. May your Son give us his clemency as a share. Pray for us, O Holy one.

ዢ1. ለንቲ ፡ ውአቱ ፡ ዕፅ ፥ ቡሩክ ፡ ዕፀ ፡ ሕይወት ፡ ዕፀ ፡ መድጊትተ ፡ ህየንተ ፡ ወዕፀ ፡ ሐ ይወት ፡ ዘውስተ ፡ ገነት ፡ ዘኮንኪ ፡ ዕፀ ፡ ሕይወት ፡ በዲበ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወፍሪኪኒ ፡ ፍሬ ፡ ሕ ይወት ፤ ውአቱ ፤ ወዘበልዐ ፤ አምኔሁ ፡ ሕይወተ ዘለባለም የሐዩ ኞ ። ጽጊ ፤ ዘለስተር ለየ ፥ አምኔኪ ፤ ዘሠናየ ፣ መዐዛ ፡ ይምዕዝ ፡ በነበ ፡ አለ ፡ የለምርዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሹሉ ፡ ለጽናፈ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ዘበአንቲለሁ ፡ ተነበየ ፤ ዊት ፡ በመዝሙር ፡ አንዘ ፡ ይብፅ ፡ ክርቤ ፡ ወሚዓ ፡ ወስሊክ ፡ አም ፤ ለሰባስኪ ። ሰሎሞን ፡ ለቡኪ ፡ ተነበየ ፤ ወይቤ ፡ ጹና ፡ ለንፍ ኪ ፡ ክመ ፡ ዴና ፡ ስጊን ። ገነት ፤ ዕዷትዎ ፡ አጎተየ ፡ መርባት ፣ ገነት ፡ ዕዱትኞ፡ ዐ ዘቀት ፡ዓትምት ፡ ፍናወ ፡ ዚለኪ ፡ ገነት ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍሬ ፡ ለቀማሕ ፡ ቆዕ ፡ ምስለ ናርይስ ። ናርይስ ፥ ምስለ ፤ መጽርይ ፤ ቀጺመታት ፡ ወቀናንሞስ ፤ ምስለ ፡ ኛሉ ፡ ዕፀወ ፣ ሊባኖ ስ ፤ ክርቤ ፤ ወዐልው ፣ ምስለ ፡ ዥሉ ፡ መቀድመ ፡ ዕፍረታተ ፡ 1ቀዐ ፡ ገነት ፡ ፀዘቀተ ፤ የ ፡ ሐይወት ፡ ዘይውሐዝ ፤ አምሊባኖስ ። ወልድኪ ፡ ሣህሉ ፡ ይክፍለ ። ሰለሊ ለ ፤ ቀድስት ።
11. You are the blessed tree, the tree of life and the tree of salvation. In place of the tree of life that was in Paradise, you became the tree of life on earth. Your Fruit is a fruit of life. And he who eats of it will live with eternal life. A flower appeared [coming] from you, which bears a pleasant perfume to those who made it known to all the ends of the world and about which David prophesied in the psalm saying: "Myrrh, oil of myrrh and cinnamon exhale from your garments." Solomon your father, prophesied and said: "The odor of your nostrils is like the odor of incense. Enclosed garden, my sister bride, enclosed garden, sealed well, your canals water the garden with its fruits, the vine flower with nard, nard with saffron, fragrant reeds and cinnamon with all the trees of Lebanon. Myrrh and aloes with all the first fruits of ointments. Source of the garden, well of water of life that flows from Lebanon." May your Son give us his clemency as a share. Pray for us, O Holy one.
12. Rod of Aaron which sprouted in the temple without sowing, and which was not watered with water and which was rendered worthy of the priests. Like her, you
በትረ ፥ ለሮን አንተ ፥ ሠረፀተ ፡ ዘአንበለ ፤ ተክል ፤ ወሊሰቀይወዋ ፣ ማየ ፤ በቤተ ፡ መ ቀደስ ፥ ረሰያ ፤ ድልወ ፤ ለካህናት ፤ ከማፃኞ ፣ ለንቲኒ ፤ ነበርኪ ፤ ውስተ ፤ ቤተ ፤ መቅደ ስ” ፡ በቅድስና ፣፤ ወበንጵሕ ፡ ወወፃአኪ ፡ አምቤተ ፡ መቀልስ ፡ በክዝር ፡ ወበዐቢይ ፣ ፍሥሐ ፡ ወሠረፀ ፡ አምፅኪ ፡ ፍሬ ፡ ሐይወት ፡ ዘበለማን ፡ አገዚአ! ፤ ወመድንኒ ፣ ሊየሱ ስ ፥ ክርስቶስ ። ኦቀድስት ፡ ዘአንበለዎ ፡ ሩካቤ ፤ ረክብኪ ፡ ወልደ ፤ በክመ ፣ ተጠየቂዮ ፣ ለመልለክ ፡ ወይቤለኪ ፡ መንፈስ ፣ ቀቶስ ፡ ይመጽአ ፡ ላዕሌኪ ፡ ወጎይለ ፤ ልሁል ፡ ይጹ ልለኪ ሞኖ ። ወልድኪ ፡ ሣህሉ ፡ ይክፍለነ ። ሰለሊ ፡ ለ ፡ ቀድስት ።
remained in the Temple, in holiness and purity. And you came out of the Temple with honor and with great joy. And the fruit of true life sprouted from you: Our Lord and Savior Jesus Christ. O Holy one, without carnal commerce, you found a Son when you questioned the angel carefully, and he said to you: "The Holy Spirit will come upon you and the power of the Most High will take you under its shadow." May your Son give us his clemency as a share. Pray for us, O Holy one.
ለኪ ፡ ይደሉ ፤ ለአገብርትኪ ፥ ወለአአማትኪ ፡ ትስለሊ ፡ ለ ፤ ንበ ፥ ወልድኪ ፤ ለአ ለ፤ ለመ ፤ በስመ ፤ ወልድኪ ፤ ሶምልአተ ፤ ጸጋ ፡ ሶሙሓሐዘ ፣ ፍሥሐ ፡ ፈድሩደ ፡ ብ ኪ ፡ ገርማ ፤ ራአይ ዘየዐቢ ፤ አምኪሩቤል።፡ አለ ፤ ብዙኃት ፡ ለዕይንቲሆሙ ፡ ወሱራፌል ፡ አለ ፡ ፪-ክነፊሆሙ ፤ አልክቱ ፡ ይክደኑ ፣ ገጾሙ ፡ ወአገረሆሙ ፣ በለርለያ ፥ ትአምርተ ፤ መስቀል ፡ ክመ ፡ ይድጉኑ ፡ አምአሳት ፡ ዘይወፅአ ፡ አ መለኮተ ፤ ወልይኪ ፤' ወለንቲሰ ፣ ኮንኪ ፤ ማጎፈደ ፤ ለመለኮት ፣ ወሊያወውፀየ ኪ ፤ አሳተ ፣ መለኮት ። ጾርኪ ፤ ነበልባለ ፤ አሳት ፡ ወሊያውዐየኪ ፡ ነበልባለ ፣ መለኮት ” ፣ ተመሰልኪ ፡ ዕፀ ፡ ጳጦስ ፡ ዘርአየ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ በደ ፤ አሳት ፥ ወዕፀታ ፣ ሊውዕየ ፤ ከማሁ ፤ ሊያውዐየዚ ፡ አግዚለ ፤ ንይላት ፤ ዘለንታሁ ፣ አሳት ፤ ፍጹም ፤፡ ለ ንቲ ፡ በለማን ፥ ዘኮንኪ ፤ ምክሐ ፤ ለዘመደ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፤ ወብኪ ፡ ቀሩባ ፡ ኮነ ፤ አምድ ር ፡ ለማጎደር ፤ ውስተ ፤ ለርያም ፡ በአንተ ፣ ተሠገዎቱ ፣ ለወልደ ፣ ለምላክ ፡ አምኔኪ ፣ ሰላሊት ”* ፡ ለነፍሳቲ ፤ ሕይወተ ፤ ኦጸጋፓዊተ ፡ ምሕረት ፤ ለአለ ፥ የለዎኑ ፥ በጸሉታ ፤ ሰለሊ ፡ ለ ፤ ኀበ ፤ አገዚአነ ፡ ወመድንኒነ ፡ ሊየሱስ ፡ ክርስስ ፣ ያጽንሀዐነ ፡ በርትዕት ፤ ናይማኖት ፤ ውስተ ፤ ለሚ ፤ ዚለሁ ፤ ወዘለቡሁ ፡ ወዘቀቶስ ፡ መንፈሱ !" ፤ ይጸገወ' ዛህሎ ፤ ወምሕረቶ ፡ ይስረይ ፡ ኃጢለተ! በብዝን ፣ ምሐረቱ ። ስብሐት ፤ ለኪ ፡ ኦወላ ዲተ ፡ አገዚለ ፡ ሹሉ ፤ ለኩቴት ፡ ወክብር ፡ ለለብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቀቶስ ፡ ይአዜ ኒ ፡ ወዘልፈኒ ፤ ወለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፤ ለሜን ፤። ወልድኪ ፣ ሣህሉ ‹ ይክፍለ ፡ ሰለሊ ። ለ ነ፤ ቀድስት።
13. It is fitting that you pray to your Son for your servants and your handmaids, for us, who believe in the name of your Son. O full of grace, O channel of joy, You possess fully a magnificence of an aspect that is greater than that of the many-eyed Cherubim | and the six-winged Seraphim. These cover their faces and feet in the form of the sign of the cross in order to escape the fire that comes out of the divinity of your Son. You also, you became a tower for the divinity and the fire of the divinity did not burn you. You carried the flame of fire and the flame of the divinity did not burn you. You are similar to the bush that Moses saw in the flame of fire, and its branches did not burn. Likewise the Lord of Virtues, complete fire in his totality, did not burn you. You who became in truth the pride of the Christian family. And through you, we have drawn closer from the earth toward the dwelling that is in the highest of heavens, because of the Incarnation of the Son of God in you. Petitioner of life for our souls, O distributor of mercy for those who believe in your prayer. Intercede for us with our Lord and Savior Jesus Christ, so that He may strengthen us in the right faith, by believing in Him, and in his Father and in the Holy Spirit. May he grant us his clemency and his mercy, may he forgive us our sins with his abundant mercy. Glory to you, O generator of the Lord of the universe. Thanksgiving and honor to the Father and to the Son and to the Holy Spirit, now and always and unto the ages of ages. Amen! May your Son give us his clemency as a share. Pray for us, O Holy one.


[p. 75] {#po.33.p75}

[A] 1. The Lord wanted to free Adam, afflicted and sad of heart, and to replace him on his ancient throne. Pray for us, O Holy one.
መንበሩ ። ሰለሊ ፡ ለ ፤ ቅድስት ። ፳ ሠረቀ ፤ በሥጋ ፣ አምድንገል ፤ ዘአንበለ ፤ ዘርለ ፡ ብአሲ ፤ መጽለ ፣ ወለድኀክ ። ለሔዋን ፡ አንተ ፡ ለስሐታ ፣ ከይሷ ፡ ፈትሐ ፤ ላዕሌፃ ፤ አገዚለብሔር ፡ አንዘ ፤ ይብፅ ፤ ብዙን ፤ ለበዝኖ ፤ ለሕማምኪ ፤ ወለጻዕርቪ ፤ ሠምረ ፡ ልቡ ኀበ ፡ ፍቀረ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ወለ
R He rose bodily from a Virgin, without the seed of man and | we were saved. 2 Eve whom the Serpent had seduced, the Lord judged her saying: "I will greatly multiply your sufferings and your pains." His heart deigned to have love for man and |! freed her. Pray for us, O Holy one.
ገዐዛ ። ሰለሊ ፤ ለ)፤ ቅድስት ። ሊየሱስ ፡ ክርስስ ፡ ቃል ፤ ዘተሰብለ ፤ ወንደረ ፡ ላዕሌ ፡ ወርሊ ፡ ስብሐቲሁ ፡ ክወ ፡ ስብሐተ ፡ ጸቶ ፡ ዋሕድ ፤ ለለቡሁ ፥ ሠምረ ፤ ይሣሀለ ። ሰለሊ ፤ ለ ፤ ቅድስት ።
3 Jesus Christ, Word who became man and dwelt among us, - and we saw his glory, - the glory that he received from his Father as only Son, deigned to be clement to us. Pray for us, O Holy one.


[p. 77] {#po.33.p77}
ገሽ ርአየ ፡ ሊሳይያስ ፤ ነቢይ ፤ በመንፈሰ ፤ ትንቢት ፡ ምሥጢሮ ፤ ለለጣ፦ሌል ፤ ወበአንተ ዝ ፡ ጸርጎ ፡ አንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ሐፃን ፡ ተወልደ ፤ ለ1 ። ወልድ ፡ ተወህበ ፡ ለ ። ሰለሊ ፡ ለ ቀድስት ።
4 The prophet Isaiah saw in the prophetic spirit the mystery of Emmanuel. And for this reason, he cried out, saying: "A child is born to us, a child is given to us." Pray for us, O Holy one.
ተፈሣሕ ፤ ወተሐሠይ ፡ ኦዘመደ ፡ አጓለ ፡ አመሐያው ፡ አስመ ፡ ለፍቀሮ ፡ አገዚለብሔር ፡ ለባለም ፣፤ ወመጠወ ፡ ወልዶ ፤ ዋሕደ ፥ ከመ ፣ ይሐየው ፥ ሉ ፤ ዘየለምን ፡ ቦቱ ፡ አስክ ፡ ለዓለም ። ፈ)ወ ፡ ለ ፡ መዝራዕተ ፡ ልዑለ ! ። ሰለሊ ፡ ለ ፡ ቀድስት ።
5 Rejoice, and exult, O human race, for the Lord loved the world and delivered his only Son so that all those who believe in Him may live eternally. He sent us his raised arm. Pray for us, O Holy one.
ዘሀሉ ፡ ወይሄሉ ፡ ዘመጽለ ፡ ወካዕበ ፡ ይመጽአ ፡ ሊዖሱስ ፡ ክርስየስ ፡ ቃል ፤ ዘተሰ ብለ ፤ ዘአንበለ ፤ ውላጤ ፡ ኮ ፤ ፍዱመ ፡ ሰብለ ሊተበዐደ ፡ ወሊተፈሪጠ ፡ በዥሉ ፡ ገ ብሩ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ዋሕድ ፡ ለላ ፤ ለሐቶ ፡ ራአይ ፡ ወለሐቶ ፡ ህላዌ ፣ ወለሐቆ ፡ መላኮት ፣ ዘአገዚለብሔር ፡ ቃል ። ሰለሊ ፡ ለ ፤ ቀድስት ።
6 He who was, and who will be; who came and who will come again, Jesus Christ, Word who became man through you, without change, became perfect man. The only Son was not changed, nor dissociated in all his works, but is one aspect, one nature, one divinity of the Word of the Lord. Pray for us, O Holy one.
፲ ተፈሥሒ ፤ ኦቤበተ ፡ ልሔም ፡ ሀገሮሙ ፡ ለነቢያት ፡ አስመ ፡ በጎቢኪ ፡ ተወልደ ፡ ክርስስ ፤ ዳገማይ ፡ ለም ፥ ክመ ፡ ያገብኦ ፡ ለለፃም ፡ ቀናሚ ፡ ብአሷ አምድር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገነት ፡ ይስዐር ፤ ፍትሐ ፡ ሞት ። ኦለዳም ፡ መሬት ፡ ለንተ ፤ ወትገብአ ፤ ውስተ ፡ መሬት ፣ ኀበ ፡ ሀለወት ፡ ብዝጎት-፣ ኀጢለት ፤ በህየ ፡ ትበዝነ ፣ ጸጋ ፡ አገዚለብሔር። ሰለሊ ፡ ለ ፤ ቀ ድስት ።
7 Rejoice, O Bethlehem, homeland of the prophets, for it is in you that Christ was born, second Adam, so that he might lead Adam, the first man, back from the earth into Paradise and that he might abolish the sentence of death: "O Adam, you are dust and to dust you shall return." Where sin had abounded, there the grace of the Lord will abound. Pray for us, O Holy one.
ጺ ተትፌጣሐ ፤ ወትትሐሠይ ፤ ሹሉ ፥ ነፍስተ ፤ ሰብአ ፥ ምስለ ፥ መላአክት ፤ ይሴብሐዎ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፤ ንጉሥ ፡ ይጸርጉ ፤ ወይብሉ ፤ ስብሐት። ለአገዚለብሔር ፤ በሰጣያት ፣ ወ ሰላም ፤ በምድር ፤ ሥምረቱ ፡ ለሰአ አስመ ፡ ሰዐረ ፥ ዘትካት ፤ ወነሠተ ፣ ምክሮ ፡ ለ ጸላሊ ፥ ወሠጠጠ ፥ መጽሐፈ ፡ ዕዳሆሙ ፡ ለለም ፡ ወለሔዋን ወረሰዮሙ ፡ ለገፀዝያ! ፡ ዘተወልደ ፡ ለ ፡ በሀገረ ፥ ዳዊት ፣ መድንኒ ፡ ሊቦሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ሰለሊ ፤ ለ ፡ ቀድስት ።
8 May all the souls of men rejoice and exult, may they glorify Christ the King with the Angels; may they cry out and say: "Glory to the Lord in the heavens, and peace on earth to men, objects of his benevolence." For He abolished the old [state of things], destroyed the plans of the Enemy and tore up the written debt of Adam and Eve. He freed them, He who was born for us in the city of David, our Savior Jesus Christ. Pray for us, O Holy one.


[p. 78] {#po.33.p78}
ቭርዛን ፤ ዘበለማን ፡ ዘያበርህ ፡ ለዥሉ ፡ ሰብአ ፤ ለአለ ፥ ይነበሩ ፤ ውስተ ፡ ባለም። በ አንተ ፣ ፍቅረ ፤ ሰብአ ፡ መጻአክ ፣ ውስተ ዓለም ፤ ወሉ ፤ ፍጥረት ፤ ተፈሥሐ ፤ በም ድለትክ ። አስመ ለድንንኮ ፥ ለለፃም ፣ አምስሐተት ፡ ወረሰደይካ ፤ ለሔዋን ፡ ለገባዚተ ፡ አምዳዕረ ፡ ሞት ። ወወሀብክ ፤ መንፈሰ ፤ ልደት ባረክናክ ፤ ምስለ ፥ መላአክቲዘ ። ሰለ
9 True light that illuminates every man who dwells in this world. Because of your love toward men you came into the world. And all creatures rejoiced at your coming: for you saved Adam from sin and You delivered Eve from the pain of death and You gave us the spirit of filiation. We bless You with your Angels. Pray for us, O Holy one.
ሊ፡፤ ለ ቀድስት ።
II Praise of Tuesday
ውዳሴ ፡ ዘሠሉስ ። ፊ ለክሊለ ፡ ምክሐ ፡ ወቀናሚተ ፡ መድኀኒተ ፡ ወመሠረተ ፡ ንጽሐ ፡ ኮነ ፤ በማርያም ፡ ድንገል ፤ አንተ ፡ ወለደት ፡ ለ ፤ ዘአገዚለብሔር ፡ ቃለ ፤ ዘኮነ ፥ ሰብለ ፥ በአንተ ፥ መድ ዝተ ። አምድጎረ ፡ ኮነ ፤ ሰብለ ፤ ጥዩቀ ፥ ለምሳላክ ፤ ፍጹም ፣ ውአቱ ፤ ወበአንተዝ ፤ ወ ለደቶ ፡ አንዘ ፡ ድንገል ፡ ይእቲ ፤ መንክር ፡ ጎይለ ፡ ወሊዶታ ፡ ዘሊይትገገር ። ሰለሊ ፡ ለ ፡ ቀድስት፤ ።
[A] 1 The crown of our pride, the principle of our salvation and the foundation of our purity are found in the Virgin Mary who gave birth for us to the Word of the Lord who became man because of our salvation. After he became incontestably man, he was perfect God. And that is the reason why she gave birth, being a Virgin. Admirable is the power of his ineffable birth. Pray for us, O Holy one.
ጆኤ አስመ ፡ በፈቃዱ ፤ ወበሥምረተ ፣ ለቡሁ ፣ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቀዱስ" መጽለ ፣ ወለድጎክ ።
R Indeed by his will and by the assent of his Father and of the Holy Spirit He came and saved us.
ዐቢይ ፤ ውአቱ ፤ ስብሐተ ፤ ድንገልናኪ ፡ ሶጣርያም ፤ ድንገል ፤ ፍጽምት ፤ ረከብኪ ፤ ሞገሰ ፤ እገዚለብሔር ፤ ምስሌኪ ፤ ለንቲ ፤ ውአቱ ፣ ሰዋስው ፡ ዘርአየ ፤ ያዕቆብ ፤ አምድ ር ፡ ዘይበጽሐ ፡ አስከ ፡ ሰማይ ፤ ወመላአክተ ፡ አገዚለብሔር ዖዐርጉ ፤ ወይወርቶ ፤ ው ስቴታ ። ሰለሊ ለ ቀድስት ።
2 Great is the glory of your virginity, O Mary perfect Virgin. You have found grace, the Lord is with you. You are the ladder that Jacob saw, which goes from the earth to heaven and on which the Angels of the Lord ascend and descend. Pray for us, O Holy one.
ለንቲ ፥ ውአቱ ፡ ዕፅ ፡ ዘርአየ ፥ ሙሴ ፤ በነደ ፣ አሳት ወሊትውዒ ፣ ዘውአቱ ፡ ወልደ ፡ አገዚለብሔር ፡ መጽለ ፤ ወንደረ ፤ ውስተ ፤ ክርሥኪ ፡ ወአሳተ ፡ መለኮቱ ፡ ሊያውዐየ ፡ ሥ ጋኪ ። ሰለሊ ፤ ለ ፥ ቀድስት ።
3 You are the tree that Moses saw in the flame of fire and which does not consume, which is the Son of the Lord. He came and dwelt in your womb; and the fire of his divinity did not consume your flesh. Pray for us, O Holy one.
ለንቲ ውአቱ ፡ ገራህት ፤ ዘሊተዘርለ ፤ ውስቴታ ዘርአ ፤ ወፅለ። አም፤ኪ ፡ ፍሬ፡ ሐይወት ። ለንቲ ፥ ውአቱ ፤ መዝገብ ዘተሣየጠ ፥ ዮሴፍ ፤ ወረከበ ፥ በውስቴታ ፡ ባሐርየ ፡ ዕንቄ ፤ ክቡረ ፤ ዘውአቱ ፡ መድጌጊኒ ፡ ሊየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡፤ ተጸውረ ፡ በክርሥቪ ፡ ወወለ ድኪዮ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዓለም ። ሰለሊ ፡ ለነ ፡ ቅድስት ።
4 You are a cultivable land in which no seed was sown. The fruit of life came out of you. You are the treasure that Joseph bought and in which he found a pearl; | precious stone which was our Savior Jesus Christ. He was carried in your womb. And you gave birth to Him in the world. Pray for us, O Holy one.


[p. 79] {#po.33.p79}
ተፈሥሒ ፡ ኦሶወላዲተ ፤ አገዚአ ፤ ሐጫቶሙ ፡፤ ለመሳአክት ፡ ተፈሥሒ ፡ ኦንጽሕት ፡ ዜ ናሆሙ ፡ ለነቢያት ፤ ተፈሥሒ ፡ አስመ ፡ ረከብኪ ፡ ሞገሰ ፤ አገዚለብሔር ፡፤ ምስሌኪ ፤ ተፈ ሥሒ ፡ አስመ ፡ ተወክፍኪ ፡ ቃሉ ፡፤ ለመልለክ ፤ ፍሥሓ ፡ ኛሉ ፡ ዓለም ፤ ተፈሥሒ ፡ ኦወላ ዲተ ፡ ፈጣሬ ፡ኩሉ ፡ ዓለም ። ሰለሊ ፡ለነ ፡ ቅድስት ።
5 Rejoice, O generator of the Lord, gladness of the Angels. Rejoice, O Pure one announced by the prophets. Rejoice, for you have found grace; the Lord is with you. Rejoice, for you accepted the word of the Angel, joy of the whole world. Rejoice, O generator of the Creator of the whole world. Pray for us, O Holy one.
ተፈሥሒ ፡ አስመ ፡ ድልወ ፤ ተሰመይኪ ፡ ኦሶወላዲተ ፡፤ ለምላክ ፤ ተፈሥሒ ፡ ኦመድገጊኺ ታ ፤ ለሔዋን ፤ ተፈሥሒ ፡ አንተ ፡ ለጥበውኪ ፡ ሐሊበ ፡ ለዘይሴስዮ ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ፍጥረት ፤ ተፈሥሒ ፡ ሶቅድስት ፤ አጸሞሙ ፡ ለኩሉሙ ፡ ሐያዋን ፤ ናንቃዐቶ ፡ ኀቤኪ ፤ ትስለሊ ፡ በ አንቲለ!። ሰለሊ ፤ ለነ ፤ ቅድስት ።
6 Rejoice, for it is worthy that you be called "Bearer of God." Rejoice, O Salvation of Eve. Rejoice, for you have suckled Him who nourishes every creature. Rejoice, O Holy One, mother of all the living. Let us lift our eyes toward you. Pray for us, pray for us, O Holy One.
፲. ኦድንግል ፤ ሶቅድስት ፤ ሶወላዲተ ፡ አገዚአ ፡ አስመ ፡ ወለድኪ ፡ ለነ ፤ ንጉሠ ፡ መንክ ር ፡ ምሥጢር ፡ ገደረ ፤ ላዕሌኪ ፡ ለመድጊተ ፡ ዚለነ ፤ ናርምም ፡ አስመ ፡ ሊንክል ፡ ፈ ጵሞ ፡ ጥንቁቀ ፡ ነጊረ ፡ በአንተ ፡ ዕበዩ ፡ ለውአቱ ፡ ገባሬ ፡ ሠናያት ፡ በብዙን ፡ መንክ ር ፡ ራአይ ። ሰለሊ ፡ ለ ፡ ቀድስት ።
7 O Virgin, O Holy One, O Bearer of the Lord, for you have given birth to the King for us. Admirable mystery! He dwelt in you for our salvation. We are silent, for we are unable to explain exactly His perfection because of the greatness of this Author of goods, varied and admirable in aspect. Pray for us, O Holy One.
ጺ ቃለ ፤ ለዝ ፡ ሕያው ፡ ዘወረደ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ደዝረ ፡ ሲና ፡ ወወሀበ ፡ ሕገ ፤ ለሙሴ ፡ ወ ክደ ፤ ርአሰ ፤ ደብር ፤ በጊሜጫ ፡ ወጢስ ፤ በጽልመት ፡ ወነፋስ ፤ ወበድምፀ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ለቀ ርንት ፤ ይጌሥጵ ፡ ለአለ ፤ ይቀውሙ ፡ በፍርሀት ። ሰለሊ ፡ ለ ፡ ቅድስት ።
8 The living Word of the Father who descended upon Mount Sinai and gave the Law to Moses and covered the summit of the mountain with fog and smoke, with darkness and with wind and with the noise of the sound of trumpets, instructed those who stood in fear. Pray for us, O Holy One.
ውአቱኬ ፡ ዘወረደ ፡ ንቤኪ ፤ ኦደዝር ፡ ነባቢት ፤ በትሕትና ፡ መፍቀሬ ፡ ሰብአ ፤ ተሰብ ለ ፡ አምኔኪ ፤ ዘአንበለ ፡ ውላጤ ፡ ፍጹወ ፡ ሥጋ ፡ ነባቢ ፡ ዘክማነ ፤ በመንፈሰ ፡ ጥበዝ ፤ ለምላክ ፡ ንደረ ፡ ላዕሌሃ ፤ ኮነ ፡ ፍጹመ ፡ ሰበለ ፡ ክመ ፡ ያድጎኖ ፡ ወይስረይ ፡ ገጢለቶ ፤ ለለም ፡ ወያንብሮ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ ወያግዝኦ ፡ ገበ ፡ ዘትካት ፡ መንበሩ ፡ በዕበየ ፤ ሣህሉ ፡ ወምሕረቱ ። ሰለሊ ፡ ለ ፤ ቅድስት ።
9 He who descended upon you, O mountain endowed with reason in humility, the friend of man, became man through you, without change, perfectly flesh endowed with reason like us. By the Spirit of wisdom, God dwelt upon her. He became perfect man, in order to save him and to forgive the sin of Adam, to replace him in the heavens and to lead him back toward his ancient throne, by the greatness of His clemency and His mercy. Pray for us, O Holy One.


[p. 80] {#po.33.p80}
ዕበያ ፡ ለድንገል ፡ ሊይትከክሀል ፤ ለተነገሮ ፤ አስመ ፡ አገዚአ ፡ ኀረያ ፤ መጽለ ፡ ወገደ ረ ፤ ላዕሌሃ ፤ ዘየኀድር ፡ ውስተ ፤ ዝርሃን ፡ ገበ ፡ ለልቦ ፡ ዘይቀርቦ ፤ ተጸውረ ፡ በክርዣ ፡ ፪ተ ፡ ለውራን ፡ ዘሊይትረለይ ፤ ወዘሊይትዐወቀ ፡ ወለደቶ ፡ ማርያም ፡ አንዘ ፡ ድንገል ፡፤ ይእቲ ። ሰለሊ ፡ ለ ፤ ቀድስት ።
10 The greatness of the Virgin cannot be explained, for the Lord chose her. He came and dwelt upon her, He who dwells in light, where no one approaches Him. He was carried in her womb for nine months, He who is invisible and unknowable. Mary gave birth to Him while remaining a Virgin. Pray for us, O Holy One.
ዘውእቱ ፡ አብን ፡ ዘርእየ ፣ ዳንጴል ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ዘተበትክ ፡ አምደብር ፡ ነዋጎ ፡ ዘአንበለ ፡ አድ ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ ቃል ፤ ዘወፅለ ፤ አምገበ ፡ ለብ ፡ መጽለ ፤ ወተሰብለ ፤ አምድንገሪ ፤ ዘ አንበለ ፡ ዘርለ ብአሲ ፥ ወለድንዘ ። ሰለሊ ፥ ለ ፥ ቀድስት ።
11 This is the stone that the Prophet Daniel saw, which was detached from a high mountain without the help of a hand; it is the Word who came forth from the Father. He came, became man through the Virgin, without the seed of a man, and He saved us. Pray for us, O Holy One.
ካንኪ ፤ ዐድቀ ፤ ንዱሐ ፤ ወሙዳየ ፤ ለሚን ፤ ርትዕት ፤ ዛይማኖቶሙ፤።፣ ለቀቶ4ን ፡ ለበዊ ፤ ኦንጽሐት ፣ ወላዴተ ፡ ለምላክ ፡ ድንገል ፡ ጎትምት ፣ ወለድኪ ፡ ለ ፡ ቃለ ‹፡ ለብ ፡ ሊየሱስ ፤ ክርስቶስ ፤ መጽለ ፡ ለመድእተነ። ሰለሊ ፡ ለ ፡ ቀድስት ።
12 You have been the pure branch and the vessel of the orthodox faith of our holy Fathers. O pure Bearer of God, sealed Virgin, you have given birth for us to the Word of the Father, Jesus Christ. He came for our salvation. Pray for us, O Holy One.
ለንቲ ፤ አሙ ለዝርዛን ክብርት ፤ ወላዲተ ፡ አገዚአ ፤ እንተ ፡ ጾርኪዮ ለቃል ፡ ዘሊ ይትረለይ ፤ አምደጎረ ፤ ወለድኪ ፤ ኪያሁ ” ፡ ነበርኪ ፡ በድንገልና ፤ በስብሐት ፤ ወበባር ኮት ፤ ያዐብዩኪ ። ሰለሊ ለ ፡ ቀድስት ።
13 You are the mother of the glorious light, bearer of the Lord, who have carried the invisible Word. After having given birth to Him, you remained in virginity. Let them magnify you with glorification and with blessing. Pray for us, O Holy One.
ለይ ፡ ልሳን ፡ ዘይክል ፡ ነቢበ ፡ ዘይትናገር " ፣ በአንቲለኪ ። ሶድንገል ፡ ንጵሐተ ፣ አሙ › ለቃለ ፤ ለብ ። ኮንኪ ፣ መንበሮ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ለዘይጽውርዎ ፣ ኪሩቤል ። ናስተበፅዐኪ ፣ ኦቡ ርክት ፤ ወንዘክር ፤ ስመኪ ፤ በኩሉ ፤ ትውልደ ፥ ትውልድ ። ኦርግብ ፣ ሠናይት ፣ አሙ ፡ ለአገዚአ ፤ ሊያሱስ ክርስቶስ ። ሰለሊ ፤ ለ ፤ ቀድስት ።
14 What tongue can say what has been explained concerning you? O pure Virgin, Mother of the Word of the Father. You have become the throne of the King whom the Cherubim carry. We proclaim you blessed, O blessed one. And we make mention of your name in the generations of generations, O pretty Dove, Mother of Our Lord Jesus Christ. Pray for us, O Holy One.
ተፈሥሒ ፥ ኦማርያም ፡ አም ፤ ወለመት ፡ አስመ ፤ ለዘውስተ ፡ ሐፅንኪ ፤ ይሴዝልዎ ፤ መላአክት ፡ ወኪሩቤል ፤ ይሰገቶ ፤ ሉቱ ፡ በፍርሀት ፡ ወሱራፌል ፤ ዘአንበለ ፅርዐት ። ይሰ ፍሑ ፡ ክነፊሆሙ ፣ ወይበሉ ፤ ዝንቱ ፡ ውአቱ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ መጽለ ይስረይ ፡ ጎጢ
15 Rejoice, O Mary, Mother and Servant, for the Angels glorify Him who is in your womb, the Cherubim adore Him with fear; the Seraphim extend their wings without ceasing and say: "This is the King of glory, He has come to forgive the sins of the world by the greatness of His clemency." Pray for us, O Holy One.


[p. 81] {#po.33.p81}

### III Praise of Wednesday
ለተ ፥ ዓለም ፡ በዕበየ ፡ ሣህሉ ። ሰለሊ ፡ ለ ፡ ቀድስት ። ፲፲ ፡ ውዳሰ ፡ ዘረቡዕ ። ፊ ሉ ፡ ሰራዊተ ፣ ሰማያት ፤ ይብሉ ብፅዕት ፤ ለንቲ ፡ ሰማይ ዳገሚት ፡ ዲበ ፤ ምድ ር ፤ ኖጎተ ፤ ምሥራቅ ማርያም ፡ ድንገል ፡ ክብካብ ፣ ንጹሐ ፥ ወመርዓ ፡ ቅቶስ። ነጸረ ፡ ለብ ፥ አምሰጣይ ፡ ወሊረክበ ፡ ዘክሟማኪ ፡ ፈነወ ፣ ዋልዶ ፡ ወተሰብለ አምዕኪ ። ሰለሊ ፥ ለ ፤ ቀድስት ።
1 Let all the armies of the heavens say: "You are blessed, second heaven on earth. You are the Gate of the Orient, Virgin Mary, the pure wedding and the holy nuptial banquet. The Father looked from the height of heaven and found no one like you. He sent His only [Son] and He became man through you." Pray for us, O Holy One.
፳ ሉ ፡ ትውልድ ፡ ያስተበፅዑኪ ፡ ለኪ ፡ ለባሐቲትኪ ኦአገዝአተ ፥ ወላዲተ ፡ ለምላክ ።
R All generations will proclaim you blessed, you alone, O our Sovereign, Bearer of God.
ዳኝ ተነበዩ ፤ ላዕሌኪ ፤ ዐቢያተ ፤ ወመንክራተ ፤ ኦሀገረ ፡ አገዚለብሔር ፡ አስመ ፤ ኮንኪ ፡ ለንቲ ፤ ማጎደረ ፥ ለፍታሐን ። ኩሎሙ ፡ ነበሥተ ፡ ምድር ፡ የሐውሩ ፡ በብርዛንኪ ፡፤ ወለሐ ዛብኒ ፤ በቋናልኪ። ኦማርያም ፤ ሉ ፤ ትውልድ ፡፤ ያስተበፅዑኪ ፡ ወይሰገቶ ፡፤ ለዘተወ ልደ አምኔኪ ፤ ወያዐብይዎ ። ሰለሊ ፡ ለ ፡ ቅድስት ።
2 They have prophesied concerning you great things and wonders, O city of the Lord; for you have been the dwelling of the joyful. All the kings of the earth walk in your light, and the peoples in your splendor. O Mary, all generations will proclaim you blessed and will adore Him who was born of you; and will magnify Him. Pray for us, O Holy One.
ለንቲ ፤ ዘበለማን ፤ ደመና ፡ አንተ ፡ ለስተርለይኪ ፡ ለ ፤ ማያ ፡ ዝናም ። ትአምርተ ፡ ዋሕ ፡ ረሰየኪ ፡ ለብ ፡ መንፈስ ቅቶስ ፡ ኀደረ ፡ ላዕሌኪ ፡ ወኀይለ ፤ ልዑል ፡ ጸለለኪ ። ኦማርያም ፡ ለጣን ፡ ወለድኪ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ለብ ፡ ዘይነብር ፡ ለዓለም ፡ መጽለ ፡ ወለድ ጎዝ ፤ አምንጢለት ። ዐቢይ ፡ ውአቱ ፡ ክብር ፡ ዘተውህበ ፡ ለክ ፤ ኦገብርሌል ፡ መልለክ ፡ ዜናዊ ፤ ፍሠሐ ፡ ገጽ ። ሰበክ ፡ ለ ፡ ልደተ ፡ አገዚአ ፤ ዘመጵለ ፤ ንቤነ ፡ ወለብሰርካ ፡ ለጣርያም ፡ ድንገል ፤ ዘአንበለ ፤ ርስሐት ፤ ወትቤላ ፤ ተፈሥሒ ፤ ሶምልአተ ፤ ጸጋ ፤ አገ ዚለብሔር ፡ ምስሌኪ ። ሰለሊ ፡ ለ ፡ ቅድስት ።
3 You are truly the cloud by which you showed us the water of rain. The Father made you the image of His only Son. The Holy Spirit dwelt upon you, and the power of the Most High covered you with its shadow. O Mary, you have truly given birth to the Word, the Son of the Father who dwells eternally. He came and He saved us from sin. Great is the honor that was given to you, O Gabriel, Angel announcer. With a joyful face, he announced to us the birth of the Lord who came toward us. And you rejoiced, through the good news, Mary the Virgin without stain, and you said to her: "Rejoice, O full of grace, the Lord is with you." Pray for us, O Holy One.
ረከብኪ ፡ ጸጋ መንፈስ ፤ ቀቶስ ፡ ንደረ ፡ ላዕሌኪ ፣ ወንይለ ፤ ልሁል ፣ ጸለለኪ ። ሶማ ርያም ፤ ለማን፤ ፡ ወለድኪ ፡ ቅቶሰ ፤ መድጉ ፡ ለሉ ፡ ዓለም ፤ መጽለ ፤ ወለድን፤። ሰ
4 You have found grace, the Holy Spirit dwelt upon you and the power of the Most High covered you with its shadow. O Mary, you have truly given birth to the Holy One, Savior of the universe. He came and saved us. Pray for us, O Holy One.
ለሊ ፡ ለ ፡ ቅድስት ። ገብረ ፡ ድንገል ፡ ይሴብሐ ፡ ልሳን ፡ ዮም ፡ ንወድሳ ፣ ለማርያም ፡ ወላዲተ ፤ ለምላክ ፡ በአንተ ፤ ዘተወልደ ፤ አምሄ8 ፡ በሀገረ ፡ ኖዊት ፣ አገዚአ ፡ ወመድጌኒ ፣ ሊየሱስ ፤ ክርስ ቶስ ። ንድ ፥ ቫልክሙ ፡ ለሕዛብ ፡ ናስተብፅዓ4 ፡ ለጣርያም ፡ አስመ ፡ ኮነት ፤ አመ ፣ ድን ግለ ፤ ወፅሙረ ፤ ተፈሥሒ ፡ ሶድንገል ፤ ንጽሐት ፡ አንተ ፡ ለልባቲ ፡ ርስ ፥ ዘመጽለ ፡
5 Our tongue glorifies the works of the Virgin. Today, we praise Mary, bearer of God, because of Him who was born of her, in the city of David, Our Lord and Savior Jesus Christ. Come, all nations. We will proclaim Mary blessed, for she was at the same time Mother and Virgin. Rejoice, O pure Virgin, who is without stain, the Word of the Father came and became man through you. Rejoice, O immaculate, perfect, stainless vessel. Rejoice, O reasonable paradise, dwelling of Christ who was the second

Adam, because of Adam, the first man. Rejoice, O Bearer of the Only-Begotten, who was not separated from the bosom of His Father. Rejoice, O pure Wedding, adorned with all the beauty of glory. He came and became man of you. Rejoice, O burning bush that the fire of divinity did not consume. Rejoice, O Servant and Mother, Virgin and Heaven who carried in the flesh the Celestial One who rides the Cherubim. And for this reason, let us rejoice and sing with the holy Angels in joy and gladness, and say: "Glory to the Lord in the heavens, and peace on earth to men, objects of His benevolence." For He delighted in you, He to whom are honor and glory. Pray for us, O Holy One.
የዐቢ ፤ ክብራ ፥፤ ለማርያም ፤ አምዥሉሙ ፡ ቀቶ4ን ፤ አስመ ፤ ድልወ ፡ ኮነት ፤ ለተወክ ር ፡ ቃለ ፡ ለብ ፡ ዘይፈርህዎ ፡ መላአክት ፡ ወያለኩተዎ ፡ ተጉዛን ፡ በሰማያት ፡ ዶረቶ ፤ ጣርያም ፡ ድንገል ፡ በክርሣ ፤ ይአቲ ፡ ተዐቢ ፤ አምኪሩቤል ፤ ወትፈደፍድ ፣ አምሱራፊል ፡ አስመ ፡ ኮነት ፡ ታቦተ ፡ ለለሐቶ ፡ ዘአምቀድስት ፡ ሥላሴ ፤ ዛቲ ፡ ይአቲ ፤ ሊየሩሳሌም ፡ ሀገሮሙ ፡ ለነቢያት ፤ ወማጎደረ ፤ ፍሥሐሆሙ ፡ ለኩሉሙ ፡ ቀቶ4ን። ሐዝብ ፡ ዘይነብር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ጽልመት ፤ ወጽላሉተ ፡ ሞት ፡ ብርፃን ፣ ፀቢይ ፣ ሠረቀ ፣ ላዕሌሆሙ ፣ አገዚለ ብሔር ፡ ዘየዐርፍ ፤ በቅዱሳሁ ፣ ተሰብለ ፤ አምድንገል ፡ ለመድጌኒተ ፡ ዚለ ፤ ንድ ፡ ርአ ዩ ፡ ዘንተ ፣ መንክረ ፡ ወዘምሮ ፡ ዘምሩ ፡ በአንተ ፥ ምሥጢር ፡ ዘተክሥተ ፡ ለ ፤ አስመ ፡ ዘሊይሰባአ ፡ ተሰብለ ፤ ቃል ፡ ተደመረ ፡ ወዘለልቦ ፡ ጥንት ፡ ኮነ ፡ ቀድመ ፡ ወለዘለልቦ ፡ መዋዕል ፤ ኮ ፤ ሉቱ ፡ መዋዕል ። ዘሊይትዐወቀ ፣ ተከሥተ ፣ ወዘሊይትረለይ ፡ ተርአየ ፤ ወልደ ፡ አገዚለብሔር ፡ ልያው ፡ ጥዩቀ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ሰብለ ፤ ሊየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣ ዘትማልም ፡ ወዮም ፡ ወክመ ፡ ውአቱ ፡ አስከ ፡ ለዓለም ፡ ለሐቶ ፡ ህላዊ ፥ ሉቱ ንስገድ ፤ ወንሰብሕ ። ሰለሊ ፤ ለ ፤ ቀድስት ።
6 The honor of Mary is greater than that of all the Saints, for she was worthy to receive the Word of the Father whom the Angels fear and to whom the Watchers render thanks in the heavens. The Virgin Mary carried Him in her womb: she is greater than the Cherubim and surpasses the Seraphim; for she became the Ark for one [of the persons] of the Holy Trinity. She is Jerusalem, the city of the Prophets, and the dwelling of the joy of all the saints. Upon the people who dwelt in darkness and the shadow of death, a great light has risen. The Lord who rests in His saints became man of the Virgin for our salvation. Come, see this prodigy. And sing psalms because of the mystery that has been revealed to us, for He who is not man became man. The Word was united [to our nature]. And He who has no beginning had a beginning. And He who had no days had days. He who was not known was revealed. And He who is invisible was seen. The Son of the living Lord became man indisputably, Jesus Christ, yesterday and today, and He will be so until eternity, one single essence. We adore Him and glorify Him. Pray for us, O Holy One.
እ. ሐዝቀሌል ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ኮነ ፤ ስምዐ ፣ በአንቲለፃ ፤ ወይቤ ፣ ርሊኩ ፤ ኖጎተ ፤ በምሥራቀ ፥ ኅቱም ፡ በዐቢይ ፡ መንክር ፡ ማጎተም ፤ ለልቦ ፡ ዘቦለ ፡ ዘአንበለ ፡ አገዚለ ፡ ጎያላን ፡ ቦለ ፡ ውስቴታ ፤ ወወፅለ ።'ሰለሊ ፤ ለ ቀድስት ።
7 The Prophet Ezekiel was a witness concerning her: He said: "I saw a gate toward the Orient, closed by an admirable seal. No one entered it, except the Lord of Hosts. He entered it and came out of it." Pray for us, O Holy One.

8 The gate therefore is the Virgin who gave birth for us to a Savior. After she had given birth to Him, she remained in virginity as before. Blessed is the fruit of your womb, O bearer of the Lord. He who came and saved us from the hand of the pitiless Enemy. You are perfect and blessed! You have found grace with the King of glory, the true God. To you belong greatness and honor above all those who dwell on earth. The Word of the Father came and became man through you. And He walked with men; for He is merciful and a friend of man. He saved our souls by His holy coming. Pray for us, O Holy One.
፲፣ ውዳሴ ፤ ዘንሙስ ።
IV Praise of Thursday
፲, ራ ዕፀ ፤ አንተ ፡ ርአየ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ በነደ ፡ አሳት ፤ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ፣ ወለዕዱቂፃ ፤ ሊተውዒ ፡ ትመስል ፡ ማርያም ፡ ድንገል ዘአንበለ ፤ ርዥስ ። ተሰብለ ፡ አምኒ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ለብ ፡ ወሊያ ውፀያ ፡ አሳተ ፥ መለኮቱ ለድንገል ፤ አምድጎረ ፤ ወለደቶ ፣ ድንግልናሃ ፡ ተረክበ ፡ ወመ ለኮቱ ፤ ሊተወለጠ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ወልደ አጓለ ፥ አመሐያው ፡ ለምላክ ዘበለማን ፡ መጽለ ፡ ወ ለድንክ ። ሰለሊ ፡ ለ ፤ ቀድስት ።
[A] 1 The tree that Moses had seen in the flame of fire in the desert and whose branches were not consumed is similar to the Virgin Mary, without stain. The Word of the Father became man through her and the fire of His divinity did not consume the Virgin. After she had given birth to Him, her virginity was found [intact] and her divinity was not changed. The Son of man is truly God: He came and saved us. Pray for us, O Holy One.
፳ ናዐብየኪ ፡ ል ፥ ኦአገዝት ፤ ወላዲተ ፥ ለምላክ ፥ አስመ ፤ ሣህልቪ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ልጎ ።
R We all exalt you, O Our Lady, bearer of God, may your clemency be upon us all.
ሯ. ተምክሕተ ዥሪ ድንገል ፤ ማርያም ፡ ወላዲተ ፡ ለምላክ ፡ ዘበአንቲለዛ ፡ ተስዕረ ፡ ዘ ቀሚ ፤ መርገም ፡ አንተ ፡ ንደረት! ፡ ዲበ ፡ ዘመድነ ፡ በዕልወት ፡ ዘገብረት ፡ ብአሲተ ፡ በልዐት ፤ አምዕፅ ። በአንተ ፣ ሔዋን፤ ፣ ተዐጽወ ፡ ኖጎተ ገነት ፡ ወበአንተ ፣ ጣርያም ፤ ድንገል ፣ ተር5ወ ፥ ለ ፤ ዳገ ከፈለ ፤ ንብላዕ ፤ አምዕፀ ፣ ሐይወት ፣ ዘውአቱ ፡ ሥ ጋሁ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ወደሙ ፡ ክቡር፤ ፡ በአንተ ፡ ፍቅረ ፥ ዚለ ፤ መጽለ ፤ ወለድኀዘ ። ለ ይ ልቡና ፡ ወለይ ፡ ነቢብ ፤ ወለይ ፡ ሰሚዕ ፡ ዘይክል ፡ ለአምሮ ፡ ዝንቱ ፤ ምሥ ጢር ፤ መንክሪተ ፡ ዘይትነበብ ፤ ላዕሌሃ ። አገዚለብሔር ፣ መፍቀሬ ፡ ሰብአ ፤ ፪ ውአቱ ፤ ባሐቲቱ ፡ ቃለ ፤ ለብ ፥ ዘሀሎ ፤ አምቀድመ ፤ ባለም ፤ በመለኮቱ አንበ ለ ፡ ሙስና ፡ አም ለብ ፡ መጽለ ፤ ወተሰበለ ፤ ወልድ ፤ ዋሐድ ፡ አምቀድስት ፡ አሙ ። አምድጎረ ፡ ወለደቶ ፤ ሊማሰ ድንገልናዛ ። ወበአንተዝ ገህደ ፥ ኮነት ፤ ከመ ፣ ወላዲ ተ ፡ ለምሳክ ፡ ይአቲ ። ኦዕሙቀ ፡ ብዕለ ፡ ጥበቡ፤፡ ለአገዚለብሔር ፡ ክርሥ ፡ ዘፈትሐ ፤ ላዕሌፃ፤ ፡ ትለድ ፡ በዳዕር ፤ ወሐማም ፣ ወሐዘ)፤ ልብ ፤ ወኮነት ፥ ፈልፈለ ፤ ሐይወተ ፡ ወ ወለደት ፡ ዘአንበለ ፡ ዘርለ ፡ ብአሲ ፤ ዘይስዕር ፤ መርገመ ፣ አምዘመድነ ፤ ወበአንተዝ ፡ ንሰብሖ ፡ አንዘ ፡ ንብል ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ለክ ፡ ኦመፍቀሬ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ቴር ፡ ወመድን ፡ ነፍሳቲነ ። ሰለሊ ፡ ለነ ፥ ቅድስት ።
2 The pride of us all: it is the Virgin Mary, bearer of God, for, through her, was abolished the curse of old that had remained upon our race by the prevarication that the woman committed by eating from the tree. Through Eve, the gate of paradise was closed and through Mary it was opened to us again. It was given to us to eat of the fruit of the tree of life which is the flesh of Christ and His glorious blood. Out of love for us, He came and saved us. What intelligence, and what word and what ear could know the wonders that have been pronounced concerning this mystery. The Lord, friend of man, He is one [Himself] only. The Word of the Father who existed before the world, in His divinity without corruption, came from the unique Father. And the only Son became man through His holy Mother. And after she had given birth to Him, her virginity was not corrupted. And for this reason, it was evident that she was the bearer of God. O deep wealth of the Wisdom of the Lord. The womb concerning which it had been decreed: "You will give birth in pain, and in suffering and in sadness of heart," became a spring of life and gave birth without the seed of man to Him who destroyed the curse of our race. That is why, let us glorify Him by saying: "Glory to you, O good friend of man and savior of our souls." Pray for us, O Holy One.


[p. 84] {#po.33.p84}
ጅ ሶዝ ፡ መንክር ፣ ወዕጹብ ፡ ንይለ ፤ ከርሣ ፤ ለድንገል ፤ ወላዲተ ፡ ለምላክ ፤ ዘአንበለ ፤ ዘርአ ። ስምዐ ፡ ኮነ ፡ መልለክ ፡ ዘለስተርለዮ ፡ ለዮሴፍ ፡ አንዘ ፡ ይበል ፡ ክመዝ ፡ አስወ ፣ ዘይትወለድ ፤ አምዛ ፡ አመንፈስ ፡ ቀዱስ ፡ ቃለ ፤ አገዚለብሔር ፡ ውአቱ ፡ ዘተሰበለ" ፡ ዘአንበለ ፥ ውላጤ ። ወለደቶ ፡ ማርያም ፡ ምክዕቢተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፣ ፍሥሓ ፡ ወይቤ ፤ ትወል ዲ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ወይሰመይ ፡ ለማኑሌል ፡ ዘበትርጓሜሁ ፡ አገዚለብሔር ፡ ምስሌ ። ወዓዲ ፡ ይሰመይ ፡ ሊየሱስፃ ፤ ዘያድጎኖሙ ፡ ለሐዝቡ ፡ አምንጢለተሙ ፡ ያድጎክ ፤ በጎይሉ ፤
3 O this admirable and astonishing power of the womb of the Virgin, Bearer of God, without seed! The witness of it was the Angel who appeared to Joseph saying thus: "Indeed, that which is born of Her, of the Holy Spirit, is the Word of the Lord who became man without change, Mary gave birth to Him in double the joy." And he said: "You will give birth to a son and he will be called Emmanuel, which means: 'The Lord is with us.' And he will also be called Jesus, who will save his people from their sins." He will save us by His power and will forgive our sin. Indeed we have indisputably known Him as a God who was man. Glory to Him until the centuries. O admirable this birth of God through Mary, through the holy Virgin. She enclosed the Word, the seed did not precede
ወይስረይ ፤ ጎጢለተነ ፡፤ አስመ ፡ ጥዩቀ ፡ ለአመርናሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ለምላክ ፡ ውአቱ ፡ ዘኮ ፡ ሰብለ ፡ ሉቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ አስከ ፡ ለዓለም ። ሶዝመንክር ፡ ልደተ ፡ ለምላክ ፤ አማር ያም ፡ አምቀድስት ፤ ድንገል ። ለገመረቶ ፤ ለቃል ፤ ሊቀደሞ ፡ ዘርአ ፡ ለልደቱ ፡ ወሊ ለማሰ ፡ በልደቱ ፣ ድንገልናዛ አምንበ ፡ ለብ ፡ ወፅለ ፥ ቃል ፡ ዘአንበለ ፡ ድካም ፡ ወአ ምደንገል ፤ ተወልደ ፡ ዘአንበለ ፤ ሕማም ። ሉቱ ፡ ሰገቶ ፡ ሰብለ ፡ ሰገል ፤ ለምጽሉ ፤ ዕጣ ፡ ክመ ፡ ለምላክ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወርቀ ፡ አስመ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ውአቱ ፡ ወክርቤ ፡ ዘይትወሀብ ፤ ለሞቱ ። ማሐየዊ ፡ በአንቲለ ፡ ተወክፈ ፡ በፈቃዱ ፡ ፡ ውአቱ ፡ ባሕቲቱ ፡ ቴር ፡ ወመ ፍቀሬ ፡ ሰዝአ ። ሰለሊ ፡ ለ ፡ ቅድስት ።
His birth nor was her virginity corrupted by His birth. The Word came forth from the Father without pain. The Magi adored Him. They brought incense as to God, gold because He was King. And the myrrh that was given for His death. The Vivifier accepted voluntarily because of us, one alone is good and friend of man. Pray for us, O Holy One.
ሶዝመንክር ፡ ነሥለ ፤ ፪ደ ፤ ዐፅመ ፡ አምገቦሁ ፣ ለለዳም ፣ ወለሐኩ ፥ አምኔሁ ፤ ብአ ሷተ ፡ ወኩሎ ፡ ፍጥረተ ፡ አጓለ ፥ አመሐያው ፡ ተውህበ ፡ አገዚአ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ለብ ፡ ተሰብለ ፡ አምቀድስት ፡ ድንገል ፤ ወተሰምየ ፡ ለማኑሌል ፡ ወበአንተዝ ፡ ንስለል ፡ ጎቤሃ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ጊ ዜ ፡ ክመ ፡ ታስተስሪ ፡ በአንቲለ ፡ ንበፍቁር፡ወልፃ ። ቴርት ፡ ይአቲ ፡ በንበ ፡ ሉው ፡ ቅቶሳን ፤ ወሊቃነ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፤ አስመ ፤ ለምጽለት ፤ ሉሙ ፡ ዘኪያሁ ፡ ይጸንሑ ፡ ወለነቢያት ኒ ፤ ለምድለት ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ለዘበአንቲለሁ ፡ ተነበዩ ፤ ወለሐዋርያት ፡ ወለደት ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ዘሰ በኩ ፥ በስሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ለጽናፈ ፡ ዓለም ፤ ለሰማዕት ፣ ወለመፃይምናን ፣ ወፅለ ፤ አምዛ ፡ ዘተጋደሉ ፤ በአንቲለሁ ፤ ሊየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ብዕለ ፤ ጸጋ ፣ ጥበቡ ፡ ዘሊይትዐ ወቅ ። ንዓሥሥ ፡ ዕበየ ” ፡ ሣህሉ ፡ አስመ ፡ መጽለ ፡ ወለድን ። ሰለሊ ፡ ለ ፡ ቅድስት ።
4 O wonder! He took a single bone from the side of Adam and with that, He fashioned the woman and all human creatures. The Lord, Word of the Father, was given, He became man through the Holy Virgin and was called Emmanuel. And for this reason, we pray to her all the time so that she may ask for forgiveness for us for our sins, from her beloved Son. She is good for all the Saints and the Patriarchs, for she brought them Him whom they were awaiting. And for the Prophets, she brought them Him concerning whom they had prophesied. And for the Apostles she gave birth for them to Him in whose name they would preach in all the ends of the world. For the martyrs and for the believers, there came forth from her Him for whom they would fight: Jesus Christ, rich in grace and whose wisdom is not known. Let us seek the greatness of His mercy, for He came and saved us. Pray for us, O Holy One.


[p. 85] {#po.33.p85}
ጹ. መሐለ ፡ አገዚለብሔር " ፡ ለያዊት ፡ በጽድቀ ፡ ወሊይኔስሐ ፡ አስመ ፤ አምሩሬ ፤ ክር ሥክ ፡ ላነበር ፡ ዲበ ፡ መንበርክ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ተወክር ፡ ውአቱ ፡ ዳድቀ ፡ ክመ ፡ አዎኔሁ ፡ ይትወለድ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ በሥጋ ፤ ፈቀደ ፡ ይጎሥሥ ፡ ወይርክብ ፣ ማጎደሮ ፣ ለአገዚለብሔ ር። ፡ ቃል ፤ ወፈጸመ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ በዐቢይ ፡ ትጋህ ፤ ወአምዝ ፡ ጸርን ፥ በመንፈስ ፣ ወይቤ ፡ ናሁ ፥ ሰማዕናሁ ፤ በሌፍሬታ ፤ ወማጎደሮ ፡ ለለምላክከ ፤ ያዕቆብ ፤ አንተ ፣ ይአቲ ፡ ቤ ተ0 ሰሐሔም”። ዘንረያ ። ለማኑሊል ። ይትወለድ ፤ ውስቴታ ፤ በሥጋ ፡ ለመድጊተ ፡፤ ዚለነ ። ካዕበ ፡ ይቤላ ፡ ካልአ ፡ አምነቢያት ፡ ወለንቲኒ ፤ ቤተ ፡ ልሔም ፤ ምድረ ፡ ሌፍራ ታ ፡ ሊትቴሐቲ ፡ አምነገሥተ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ አስመ ፡ አምኔኪ ፤ ይወፅአ ፤ ንጉሥ ፣ ዘይርአዮ ሙ ፣ ለሕዝብየ ፡ አስራጴል ፤ ኦዝር ፡ ለአሉ ፤ አለ ፡ ተነበዩ ፡ ዘበ፳ቶ ፤ መንፈስ ፡ በአ ንተ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ሉቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ምስለ ፤ ጌር ፤ ለቡሁ ፣ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቀዱስ ” ፡ አምይ አዜ ፡ ወአስክ ፡ ለዓለም ። ሰለሊ ፡ ለ ቀድስት ።
5 The Lord swore to David in truth and He did not repent of it: "For I will place of the fruit of your womb upon your throne." And when this just man received the Christ who was to be born of him in the flesh, he wanted to seek and find a dwelling for the Word of the Lord and he realized it with great care. Then he cried out in spirit and said: "Come, we have heard it in Ephrata." And the dwelling of the God of Jacob is this Bethlehem that Emmanuel chose so that He might be born in it corporally for our salvation. And another of the Prophets said: "And you, Bethlehem, land of Ephrata, you will not be humiliated by the kings of Judea, for from you will come forth the King who nourishes my people Israel." O this discourse for all those who have prophesied in a single spirit concerning Christ. Glory to Him, with His good Father and the Holy Spirit, from now and until the centuries. Pray for us, O Holy One.
ያዊት ፤ ዘነገሠ ፤ ለአስራሌል ፤ ለመ ፤ ይትነሥሉ ፤ ላዕሊሁ ዕልዋን ፣ ፈተወ ፣ ይስተ ይ ፡ ማየ ፡ አምዐዘቆተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ፅሔም ፤ ፍጡ ፡ ተንሥሉ ፡ መላህቀተ ፡ ሐራሁ ፣ ወተቃ ተሉ ፥ በውስተ ፣ ትዕይንተ ፡ ዕልዋን ፤ ወለምድሉ ፤ ሉቱ ፡ ውአተ ፣ ማየ ፣ ዘፈተወ፣ ይ ስተይ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርአየ ፣ ውአቱ ፤ ጻድቅ ፣ ከመ ፣ ለጥብቡ ፣ ወመጠዉ ፡ ነፍሶሙ ፣ ለቀት ል ፤ በአንቲለሁ ፡ ክዐወ ፣ ውአተ ፣ ማየ ፣ ወሊሰትየ ፡ አምፅሁ ። ወአምዝ ፣ ተቱለቄ ፡ ሉ ቱ ፡ ጽድቀ ፡ አስክ ፡ ለዓለም ። ለማን ፡ መዙ ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ ጣዕማ ፡ ለዘባለም ፡ ወክዐዉ ፣ ደሞሙ ፡ በአንተ ፣ አገዚለብሔር ፤ ወተዐገሠ ፣ ሞተ ፤ መረረ በአንተ ፤ መንገሥተ ፡ ሰ
6 David who reigned over Israel, when the rebels rose up against him, wanted to drink water from the cistern of Bethlehem. Immediately the chiefs of his army rose up and made war in the camps of the rebels. And they brought Him this water that he desired to drink; but when this just man saw how they had wanted, and had delivered themselves to murder because of him, he poured out this water and did not drink it, and afterwards, it was imputed to him as justice forever. Truly the martyrs despised the flavor of this world; and poured out their blood because of the Lord and endured patiently a bitter death because of the kingdom of the heavens. Be merciful to us according to the greatness of your clemency. Pray for us, O Holy One.
ጣያት ፤ ተሣሀለ ፡ በክመ ፡ ዕበየ ፤ ሣህልክ ። ሰለሊ ፡ ለ ፤ ቀድስት ። ለሐ ፡ ዘአምቀድስት ፡ ሥላሴ ፡ ርአዮ ፡ ትሕትና! ፡ አክ ፤ ሰማየ ፡ ሰማያት ፤ መ ጵለ ፡ ወንደረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ክርሠ ፡ ድንገል ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ሰብለ ፡ ከማጣ ፡ ዘአንበለ ፤ ጎጢለት ፤ ባሐቲታ ፤ ወተወልደ ፤ በቤተ ፤ ልሔም ፡ በከመ ፡ ሰበኩ ፡ ነቢያት ። ለድንኀክ ፤ ወቤዘወነ ፡ ወረሰየነ ፤ ሕዝበ ፡ ዚለሁ ። ሰለሊ ፡ ለ ፤ ቅድስት ።
7 One person of the Holy Trinity, seeing our humility, inclined the heaven of heavens, came and dwelt in the womb of the Virgin. He became man like us, with the sole exception of sin. And He was born in Bethlehem as the Prophets preached. He saved us and redeemed us and made us His people. Pray for us, O Holy One.
ኘዥ ውዓሴ ፡ ዘዐርብ ።
V Friday Praise


[p. 86] {#po.33.p86}
ፊ ቡርክት ፤ ለንቲ ፥ አምለንስት ፤ ወቡሩክ ፤ ፍሬ ፥ ክርሥኪ ፤ ኦማርያም ፡ ድንገል ፡ ወላ ዲተ ፡ ለምላክ ፡ ዘአንበለ ፥ ርዥስ ሠረቀ ፡ ለ ፡ አምኒ ፡ ፀሐየ ፤ ድድቅ ። ወለቀረበ ፡ ታሕተ ፡ ክነፊሁ አስመ ፡ ውአቱ ፡ ፈጠረ ። ሰለሊ ፡ ለ ፡ ቅድስት ።
[A] 1 You are blessed among women; and the fruit of your womb is blessed, O Virgin Mary, Mother of God, who are without stain! It is from you that the sun of justice rose for us, and He placed us under His wings, for it is He who created us. Pray for us, O Holy One.
፳ ለኪ ፤ ለባሐቲትኪ ፤ ኦአገዝአተ ፤ ወላዲተ ፤ ለምላክ ፤ አመ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ለንቲ ፤ ናዐብ የኪ ፤ በስብሐት ፤ ወበውዳሴ።
R You alone, O our Sovereign, Mother of God, mother of the light; we magnify you with glorification and with praise!
ቡርክት ፤ ለንቲ ፤ ተዐብዩ ፤ አምሰጣይ ፣ ወትክብሪ ፤ አምድር ፡ ወላዕለ ፤ ሉ ፤ ዓሊ ናት ፤ መኑ ፡ ዘይክል ፡ ቢበ ፡ ዕበየኪ ፤ ወለልቦ ፤ ዘይመስል ፡ ኪያኪ ፤ ኦማርያም ፡ መላ አክት ፡ ያዐብዩኪ ፡ ወሱራፌል ፤ ይሴብሑኪ ፣ አስመ ፡ ዘይነብር ፣ ዲበ ፣ ኪሩቤል ፡ ወሱ ራፌል ፡ መጽለ ፡ ወንደረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ከርሥኪ ፡ መፍቀሪ ፤ ሰአ ፤ ለቀረበ ፡ ንበሁ ፡ ዘ ዚለ ፡ ሞተ ፡ ነሥለ ፡ ወአንቲለሁ ፡ ሕይወተ ፤ ወሀበ ፤ ዘሉቱ ፡ ክብር ፣፤ ወስብሐት ። ሰ ለሊ ፡ ለ ፡ ቅድስት ።
2 You are blessed! You are greater than the heaven. You are more honored than the earth, and you are above all intelligences. Who will be able to express your greatness? No one resembles you. O Mary, the Angels magnify you and the Seraphim glorify you; for He who sits upon the Cherubim and upon the Seraphim came and dwelt in your womb. The friend of men made us approach Him: He took our own death and gave us His own life. To Him, honor and glory. Pray for us, O Holy One.
ቡርክት ፡ ለንቲ ፥ ማርያም ፤ ወቡሩክ ፡ ፍሬ ፤ ክርሥኪ ፡ ኦድንገል ፡ ወላዲተ ፤ ለምላ ክ ፡ ምክሖን ፡ ለደናገል ፤ ዘአምቀደመ ፡ ባለም ፡ ህልው ፡ ተሰብለ ፤ አምንፅኪ ፤ ብሉየ ፡ መዋዕል ፤ ወፅለ ፤ አምክርሥኪ ። ሥጋ ፡ ነሥለ ፣ ወመንፈሰ ቀቶሰ ፡ ወሀበነ ፤ ወረሰየ! ዕሩያነ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፤ በብዝን ፡ ጊሩቱ ። ለንቲ ፣ ተዐብዩ ፣ አምብዙኃት ፡ ለንስት ፡ አለ ፡ ! ሥላ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ ወክብረ ፤ ኦጣርያም ፡ ወላዲተ ፡ ለምላክ ፡ ሀገር ፡ መንፈሳዊተ ፡ ዘንደረ ፡ ላ ዕሌሃ ፤ አገዚለብሔር ፡ ልሁል ፤ አስመ ፤ ዘይነብር ፣፤ ዲበ ፤ ኪሩቤል ፡ ወሱራፌል ፤ በአ ሬጎኪ ፤ ሐቀፍኪዮ ፤ ወዘይሴሲ ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ዘሥጋ ፡ በብዝን ፡ ጊሩቱ ፡ ለዘ ፤ ለጥባተኪ ፡ ወጠበወ ፡ ሐሊበ ፤ ዘውአቱ ፤ ለምላክ ፡ መድን4 ፡ ኛሉ ፡ ይርዕየ ፤ ፡ አስክ ፡ ለዓለም ፡ ንስ ገድ ፡ ሉቱ ፡ ወንሰብሖ ፡ አስመ ፡ ውአቱ ፡ ፈጠረ ። ሰለሊ ፡ ለ ፡ ቀድስት ።
3 You are blessed, O Mary. And the fruit of your womb is blessed, O Virgin, Mother of God, cause of the glory of Virgins. He who exists before the world was made man through you, in the ancient days. He came out of your womb. He took our flesh, and gave us the Holy Spirit. He made us His equals with Him by His abundant goodness. As for you, you are greater than many women, who have received grace and honors. O Mary, Mother of God, spiritual city that the Most High Lord inhabited, for He who sits upon the Cherubim and the Seraphim, you embraced Him in your hands. And He who nourishes all flesh by the abundance of His goodness seized your breasts and sucked the milk. He who is our God, the Savior of all, may He nourish us into eternity. Let us adore Him and glorify Him, for it is He who created us. Pray for us, O Holy One.


[p. 87] {#po.33.p87}
ፋ ጣርያም ፡ ድንገል ፡ ሙየ ፡ ፅፍረት ፡ ነቅዐ ፡ ፈልፈለ ፡ ማየ ፡ ሐይወት ፡ ፍሬ ፡ ከርሣ ፡ ለድን ፡ ሹሉ ፡ ዓለመ ወሰዐረ ፤ አም8 መርገመ ፡ ወገብረ ፡ ሰላመ ፡ ማአከሌነ ፡ በ ወመስቀሉ ፥ ወበትንሣለሁ ፡ ቀድስት ፤ ለገብኦ ፡ ለብአሲ ፡ ገመ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ገነት ። ሰለሊ ፥ ለ ፡ ቅድስት ።
4 Virgin Mary, vessel of ointment, source from which gushes the water of life. The fruit of your womb saved the whole world and abolished the curse [of which we were the object] and made peace in our midst by His Cross and by His holy resurrection and led man back again into Paradise. Pray for us, O Holy One.
ኢ. ጣርያም ፡ ንጽሐት ፣ ድንገል። ወላዲተ ፤ ለምላክ።፡ ማአምንት ፣ ሰላሊተ ፤ ምሕረ ት ፡ ለውሉደ ፡ ሰብአ። ሰለሊ ፡ ለ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወልድኪ ፡ ይስረይ ፡ ጎጢለተ' ። ሰለሊ ፡ ለ ፡ ቀድስት ።
5 Mary, pure Virgin, Mother of God, faithful mediatrix of mercy for the children of men. Pray for us to Christ, your Son, so that He may forgive our sins. Pray for us, O Holy One.
ጣርያም ፡ ድንገል ፤ ትጸርጎ ፤ በቤተ ፡ መቅደስ ፣ ወትብፅ ፣ የለምር ፣ አገዚለብሔር ፡ ክመ ፡ ለልብየ ፡ ዘለለምር ፡ ባዕድ ፡ ወሊምንተጊ ፡ ዘአንበለ ፤ ድምፀ ፡ ቃሉ ፡ ለመልለክ ፡ ዘለብሰረኒ ፤ በክብር ፣ ወይቤለኒ ፣ ሰላም ፤ ለኪ ፡ ኦቅድስት ፤ ድንገል ፤ ጾርኪ ፤ ዘሊይጸ ወር ፡ ለገመርኪ ፣ ዘይሊተገመር ፡ወለልቦ ፡ ዘያገምሮ ፣ ምንትኒ ፡ ይበዝነ-፣ ውዳሰኪ ፡ ሶምልአተ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ በሉ ፡ ክብር ፡ አስመ ፡ ኮንኪ ፡ ለንቲ ፡ ማጎደረ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ለብ ። ለንቲ ፡ ውአቱ ፡ መንጦላዕት ፡ ስፍሐት ፤ አንተ ፤ ታስተጋብሶሙ ፡ ለመዛይምናን ፡ ሕዝበ ፡ ክርስቲ ያን ፡ ወትሜህሮሙ ፡ ሰጊደ ፡ ለሥሉስ ፡ ማሐየዊ ። ለንቲ ፡ ውአቱ ፡ ዘጾርኪ ፡ ፀምደ ፡ አሳት ፣ ዘርአየ ፤ ሙሴ ፡ ዘውአቱ ፡ ወልደ ፡ አገዚለብሔር ፡ መጽለ ፡ ወንደረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ክርሥኪ ፡ ኮንኪ ፡ ታቦቶ ፣ ለፈጣሬ ፤ ሰጣያት ፤ ወምድር ። ጾርኪዮ ፣ በክርሥክ ፡ ዘተ ፡ ለውርጎ ፤ ለንቲ ፡ ማአምንት ፡ ለዘሊያገምርዎ ፡ ሰማያት ፤ ወምድር ፣ ኮንኪ ፤ ተንክተ መ ፡ ለዕርገት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ብርዛንቪ ፤ የዐቢ ፤ አምብር ፡ ፀሓይ ። ለንቲ ፡ ውአ ቱ ፡ ምሥራቅ ፡ ዘወፅለ ፡ አም፤ኪ ፡ ኮክብ ፡ ብሩህ ፡ ዘነጸርዎ ፡ ቅፍሳን ፡ በፍሥሓ ፡ ወበ ሐጫት፤ ፡ ዘፈትሐ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሔዋን ፡ ትለድ ፡ በጻዕር ፡ ወሕማም ። ለንቲሰ ፣ ማርያም ፡ ሰሟዕኪ ፡ ዘይብል ፤ ተፈሥሒ ፡ ሶምልአተ ፡ ጸጋ ፤ ወለድኪ ፡ ለ ፡ ንጉሠ፣፡አገዚለ፡ሹሉ፤ ፍጥረት ፤ መጽለ ፤ ወለድጎ፤ ፤ አስመ " ፤ መሓሪ ፥ ውአቱ ፥ ወመፍቀሬ ፤ ሰብአ ። በአንተ ዝ ፡ ንወድሰኪ ፡ በከመ ፡ ገብርሌል መልለክ ፡ አንዘ ፡ ንብል ፡ ቡርክት ፣ ለንቲ ፣ ማርያ ም ፡ ወቡሩክ ፡ ፍሬ ፡ ከርሥኪ ፡ ተፈሥሒ ፡ ሶምልአተ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ አገዚለብሔር ፡ ምስሌኪ። ሰለሊ ፡ ለ ፡ ቅድስት ።
6 The Virgin Mary cried out in the Temple and said: "The Lord knows that there is nothing in me other than what I know, nothing except the sound of the voice of the Angel who gladdened me with the good news in [my] honor and said to me: "Peace to you, O Holy Virgin. You have carried Him who is not carried. You have contained Him who is not contained. And there is nothing that contains Him. Your praise is manifold, O full of grace, in all honor; for you, you have become the dwelling of the Word of the Father. You are the Veil stretched out that gathered the faithful into a Christian people and taught them the adoration of the life-giving Trinity. You are she who carried the pillar of fire that Moses saw, which is the Son of the Lord. He came and dwelt in your womb. You became the ark for the Creator of the heavens and the earth. You carried Him nine months in your womb. You were faithful to Him whom the heavens and the earth do not contain. You were a bridge for the ascension to heaven. Your light is greater than the light of the sun. You are the Orient from which came forth the luminous star that the Saints saw in joy and gladness. He who placed upon Eve the sentence: "You will give birth in pain and in suffering," you, Mary, you heard Him say: "Rejoice, O full of grace." You gave birth for us to the King, the Lord of all creatures; He came and saved us; for He is merciful and a friend of man. That is why we praise you like the Angel Gabriel, saying: "You are blessed, Mary, and the fruit of your womb is blessed. Rejoice, O full of grace. The Lord is with you. Pray for us, O Holy One.


[p. 88] {#po.33.p88}
፡፡
[A] VI Praise of the First Sabbath [Saturday]
ውሴ ፡ ዘቀፃዒትተ ፡ ሰንበት ። እ ራ ንድሐት ፤ ወብርህት ፣ ወቅድስት፤፡ በዥሉ ፤ አንተ ፡ ሐቀፈቶ ፡ ለአገዚአ ፡ በአሪኃ ፤ ወሉ ፡ ፍጥረት ፣ ይትፌሥሑ ፡ ምስሌፃ ፡ አንዘ ፡ ይጸርጉ ፣ ወይብሉ። ሰለሊ ፡ ለ)፣ ቀ ድስት ።
1 Pure and luminous, and altogether holy is she who embraced the Lord with her hands. All creatures rejoice with her, while they cry out and say: "Pray for us, O Holy One."
ሽ:ን ተፈሥሒ ፤ ኦምልአተ ፡ ጸጋ ፤ ተፈሥሒ ፡ አስመ ፡ ረክበኪ ፡ ምገሰ ፤ ተፈሥሒ ፡ አገዚ ለብሔር ፤ ምስለኪ ።
R Rejoice, O full of grace. Rejoice for you have found grace. Rejoice, the Lord is with you.
ናስተበፅዕ ፤ ዕበየኪ ፤ ኦገርምት ፤ ድንገል ፤ ወንፈኑ፤ ፤ ለኪ ፡ ፍሥሐ ፥ ምስለ ፡ ገብር ሌል ፡ መልለክ ፤ አስመ ፣ አምፍሬ፤ ፡ ክርሥኪ ፡ ኮ ፡ መድ“ተ ፡ ዘመድነ ፤ ወለቀረበ ፡ ንበ ፡ አገዚለብሔር፤ ለቡሁ ። ሰለሊ ፤ ለ ፤ ቅድስት ።
2 We proclaim your greatness blessed, O august Virgin, and we address to you a greeting with the Angel Gabriel, for through the fruit of your womb came the salvation of our human family; and He made us approach His Father. Pray for us, O Holy One.
ክመ ከብካብ ፡ ዘለልቦ ፤ ጥልቀት ፤ መንፈስ ፤ ቅዱስ ፥ ኀደረ ፤ ላዕሌኪ ፥ ወንይለ ፤ ል ዑል ፡ ጸለለኪ ፤ ኦማርያም ፡ ለማን ፤ ወለድኪ ፡ ቃለ ፤ ወልደ ፡ ለብ ፡ ዘይነብር ፡ ለዓለም ፡ መጽለ ፤ ወለድንክ ፤ አምጎጢለት፤ ። ሰለሊ ፤ ለ ፤ ቅድስት ።
3 You resemble a wedding without stain, the Holy Spirit dwelt in you, and the power of the Most High covered you with its shadow. O Mary, you truly gave birth to the Word, the Son of the Father who dwells eternally. He came and saved us from sin. Pray for us, O Holy One.
ለንቲ ፤ ውአቱ ፡፤ ዘመድ ፤ ዘአምሥርወ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ወለድኪ ፡ ለ)፤ በሥጋ ፣ መድን ፤ ሊየሱስ ፤ ክርስቶስ ፤ ዋሕድ ፡ ቃል ፤ ዘአምለብ ፤ ዘአምቀድመ ፡ ባለም ፡ ህልው" ፤ ኀብ
4 You are the shoot of a family descended from David. You gave birth for us in the flesh to Our Savior Jesus Christ, the unique Word who proceeds from the Father, who was before the world existed, who hid Himself and took from you the form of a servant. Pray for us, O Holy One.
ለ ፡ ርአሶ ፡ ወነሥለ ፣ አምፅኪ ፡ ለርለያ ፡ ገብር ። ሰለሊ ፡ ለ ፥ ቅድስት ። ፡ ኮንኪ ፥ ገሚተ ፡ ሰማይ ፤ ዲበ ፤ ምድር ፣ ኦወላዲተ ፤ ለምሳላክ ፤ ሠረቀ ፡ ለኒ ፤ አም ፤ኪ ፀሓየ ፡ ድድቅ ፡ ወወለድኪዮ ፡ በክመ ፡ ትንቢተ ፡ ነቢያት ፡ ዘአንበለ ፡ ዘርአ ፡ ወ ሊሙስና ። ሰለሊ ፤ ለ ፤ ቅድስት ።
5 You were the second heaven on earth, 6 Mother of God, without impurity for us, the sun of justice rose through you. And you gave birth to Him, according to the prophecy of the Prophets, without human seed, nor by corruption. Pray for us, O Holy One.


[p. 89] {#po.33.p89}
ለንቲ ፤ ውአቱ ፡ ደብተሬ ፤ አንተ ፤ ተሰመይኪ ፤ ቅድስተ ፤ ቀቶሳን ፣ ወውስቴታ ፡ ታቦ ት ፤ በወርቀ ፣ ልቡጥ ፤ አምዥለፄ ፣ ወውስቴታ ፡ ጽላተ ፡ ኪዳን ። መሶበ ፡ ወርቀ ፡ አንተ ፤ መና ፡ ዓቡአ ፤ ዘውአቱ ፤ ወልደ ፤ አገዚለብሔር ፤ መጽለ ፤ ወጎደረ ኀበ፣ ማርያም ፡ ድንገል ፤ ዘአንበለ ፤ ርስ ። ተሰብለ ፤ አምኔ7ፃ ፡ ቃለ ፤ ለብ ፣ ወወለደቶ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ለንጉሠ ፣ ስብሐት ፤ መጵለ ፤ ወለድንክ ፤ ትትፌሣል ፡ ገነት ፡ አመ፣በገፅ ፡ ነባቢ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ለብ ፡ ዘይነበር ፡ ለዓለም ፤ መጽለ ፤ ወለድኀጎክ ፤ አምንጢለት። ሰለሊ ፡ ለ ፤ ቅድስት ።
6 You are the Tabernacle that is called: "Holy of Holies," inside of which is the Ark entirely covered with gold and inside of which are the tables of the Covenant. Golden urn in which is hidden the manna which is the Son of the Lord. He came and dwelt in the Virgin Mary without stain. The Word of the Father was made man through her. And she gave birth in the world to the King of glory. He came and He saved us. Paradise, the mother of the rational Lamb rejoice. The Son of the Father who dwells eternally came and saved us from sin. Pray for us, O Holy One.
ተሰመይኪ ፡ አመ ፤ ለክርስቶስ ፤ ንጉሥ ፤ አምድኅረ ፡ ወለድኪ ፤ ኪያሁ ፡ ነበርኪ ፡ በ ድይንገልና ፤ በመንክር ፡ ምሥጢር ፣ ወወለድኪዮ ፡ ለለማኑሌል ፤ በአንተዝ " ፣ ዐቀበኪ ፡ አንበለ'" ፤ ሙስና ። ሰለሊ ፡ ለ ፤ ቅድስት ።
7 You are called the mother of Christ the King. After you had given birth to Him, you remained in virginity by an admirable mystery; and you gave birth to Emmanuel. It is for this reason that He kept you from corruption. Pray for us, O Holy One.
ጺ ለንቲ ፤ ውአቱ ፤ ሰዋስው ዘርአየ ፤ ያዕቆብ ፣ አገዚለብሔር ፡ ላዕሊሁ ፡ አስመ ፡ ጾ ርኪ ፤ በክርሥኪ ፡ ዓቱም ፤ ዘሊይትዐወቀ ፤ አምቫለፄ ። ኮንኪ ፡ ለ ፤ ሰላሊተ ኀበ ፡ አ ገዚአ ፡ ሊየሱስ ፤ ክርስቶስ ፤ ዘተሰብለ ፤ አምኔኪ ፤ በአንተ ፡ መድጊትነ ። ሰለሊ ፡ ለ ፡ቀድስት ።
8 You are the ladder that Jacob saw, the Lord is upon it. For you carried in your sealed womb Him who is not known from all sides. You were for us the mediatrix to Our Lord Jesus Christ who became man for our salvation. Pray for us, O Holy One.
ናሁ ፡ አገዚአ ፡ ወፅለ ፡ አምኔኪ ፡ ኦቡርክት ፡ ጽርሕ ፡ ንጽሕት ፡ ያድንን ፡ ኛሎ ፡ ዓለ መ ፡ ዘፈጠረ ፤ በዕበየ ፤ ሣህሉ ። ንሰብሖ ፤ ወንወድሶ ፡ አስመ ፡ ውአቱ ፡ ጌቴር ፡ ወመፍቀ ሬ ፡ ሰዝአ ። ሰለሊ ፡ ለነ ፡ ቀድስት ።
9 Behold, the Lord has come out of you, O Blessed One, O pure Palace, in order to save the Universe that He had created by the greatness of His clemency. Let us praise Him and glorify Him, for He is good and the friend of man. Pray for us, O Holy One.
10 Rejoice, O full of grace, Virgin without stain, pure creature, honor of the whole world. Light that does not go out. Indestructible sanctuary,
ተፈሥሒ ፡ ኦሶምልአተ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ ድንገል ፤ ዘአንበለ ፡፤ ርዥስ ፤ ልሕዥት ፡፤ ንጽሕት ፡ ክብረ ፡ ሹሉ ፤ ዓለም ። ብርዛን ፤ ዘሊትጠፍአ ፤ መቀደስ ፡ ዘሊትትህሠት ፡ በትረ ” ፡፤ ዛይማኖት ፡ ዘሊተጸነን ፤፡ ምስማኮሙ ፡ ለቅዱሳን " ። ሰለሊ ፡ ለ ፤ ንበ ፡ ወልድኪ ፡ ጌቴር ፡ መድጌጊኒነ ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ ወይሣሀሰለነ ፡ ይስረይ ፤ ንጢለተነ ። ሰለሊ ፡ ለነ ፤ ቅድስት ።
Staff of the faith that does not bend; support of the Saints. Pray for us to your good Son, Our Savior, to have pity on us, to be clement and to forgive us our sins. Pray for us, O Holy One.

[E] VII Praise of the Holy Sabbath of the Christians [Sunday]
ውዳሴ ፡ ዘሰንበተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ቅድስት ፤ ። ል ተሰመይኪ ፡ ፍቅርተ ፤ ኦቡርክት ፡፤ አምለንስት ፡ ለንቲ፤ውአቱ ፡ዳገሚት ፡ቀመር፡አንተ ፡ ትሰመይ ፡ ቅድስተ ፤ ቅዱሳን ፤ ወውስቴታ ፡ ጽላተ ፡ ኪናን ፤ ፲ ቃላት ፡ አለ ፡ ተጽሐፋ፤ በለዳብዒሁ ፡ ለአገዚለብሔር ። ቀዲሙ ፡ ዚነወዐነ ፥ በየውጣ ፡ አንተ ፡ ይአቲ ፡ ቀዳሜ ፡ ስ ሙ ፡ ለመድንኒነ ፡ ሊየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፤ ዘተሰዝለ ፡ አምኔኪ ፡ ዘአንበለ ፤ ውላጤ ፤ ወኮነ ፡፤ ዐራቄ ፡ ለሐዲስ ፤ ኪዳን ፤ በውሒዘ ፡ ደሙ ፡ ቀቅቶስ ፤ ለንጵሖሙ ፡፤ ለመዛይምናን ፡ ወለሐ ዝዝ ፡ ንጹሓን ። ሰለሊ ፡ ለ ፤ ቅድስት ።
1 You have been called "beloved," blessed among women. You are the second Tabernacle that is called Holy of Holies and in which are the tables of the Covenant: the ten words that were written by the fingers of the Lord. Previously He had informed us by means of the "iota" which is the first letter of the name of Our Savior Jesus Christ who became man through you, without change. And He became mediator for the New Testament. By the shedding of His holy blood, He purified the believers and the people of the pure. Pray for us, O Holy One.

And by His coming, He enlightened us, we who dwelt in darkness and in the shadow of death. And He directed our steps on the path of peace, by the mystery of His holy wisdom. Pray for us, O Holy One.

9 It is more fitting for you than for all other saints that you pray for us, O Full of grace. You are greater than the Patriarchs and above all you are more glorious than the Prophets. You possess a nobility of appearance that is greater than the nobility of the Seraphim and Cherubim. You are truly the glorification of our race and the intercessor for the life of our souls. Pray for us, Our Lord and Savior, Jesus Christ, so that He may strengthen us in the right faith (that is to say) in the faith in Him; that He may grant us His clemency and His mercy, that He may forgive our sins through the abundance of His mercy. Pray for us, O Holy One. And because of this, we all magnify you, O Our Lady, Bearer of God at all times, let us pray and cast our eyes toward you, so that we may find clemency with the Friend of man.

Glory to the Holy Spirit, to this name, in the ages of ages. - XVIII, Reading of the Gospel.


[p. 106] {#po.33.p106}
፦ ጂኘሂ1፲1 ለንበዝ ፡ ወንጌል ።
The Deacon says: "Stand and listen to the Holy Gospel, the story of Our Lord and Savior Jesus Christ. The Priest says:

Have mercy on us whom You have created. f) Forgive the sins of your people; and have mercy on your creatures. O Lord. Exterminate us! Do not put us to shame, O Lord, in your presence. h) Our Lady, cast your eyes upon us. Pray for us, your children and save us from all affliction and all sorrow. i) Be with us by day, and in the midst of us by night, O Lord; do not distance yourself from us. j) Be with us, be for us [our] help. So that the peoples do not say: "Where is his God? Wage war against these quantities of taxes that trouble the kingdom [of our King] and that upset our King himself. And when the King goes to war: Take the shield and the spear and rise up to help him. Draw your sword and besiege the enemies of our King. Guard him in his comings and in his goings. And when he remains in good health and in peace: Crush his enemies under his feet: guard the kingdom and the armies of our King. Guard him for us like the apple of the eye and by the shadow of your wings, protect our King These invocations must be said 21 times alternately to the right and to the left. Before you say them once all together. M29 - XXXVI, Supplication (Mastabqu'e) for the King [SEVENTH PERIOD] M30 - XXXVII, Psalm 117.

### Cant. I
፲ሕ፥፡፳ጳ። ፻፳ 8እ24 መኑጆ ፡ ይመስለክ ፡ አምነ ፡ ለማልክተት ፡ አገዚኦ ። ወመኑ ፡ ከማክ ፡ ስቡሕ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ቅዱሳን” ።
Ex. 15, 11a St. 24 Who resembles You among the gods, O Lord? 11b 25 And who is like You, glorious among the saints?

### Cant. II
ማሐ፡፪። ፲35. መገቦሙ ፡ ወመርሖሙ ። ወፀዐቀቦሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዝንተ ፡ ባይን ። ማሐ፡፻። ፲30. አስመ ፡ ይንን ፤ አገዚለብሔር ፡ ሕዝቦ ። ወይትናበዝአ ፡ በአንተ ፤ ለገዝርቲሁ ።
Dt. 31, 10c 31 And He directed and led them, 10d 32 and kept them as the apple of the eye. Cant. III Dt. 32, 36a 45 For the Lord will judge His people, 36b 46 and will speak in favor of His servants.

### Cant. IV
ማሕ፡፪። ወተፈሣሕኩ ፤ በለድጎኖትክ ። አስወ ፡ ለልቦ ፡ ቀቅቶስ ፡ ክመ ፡ አገዚለብሔር ።
1 Sm. 2, 1d 4 And I have rejoiced in Your salvation; 2a 5 for no one is holy like the Lord.

### Cant. V
ማሕ፥፡፳። አነገር ፡ ጽድቀክ ፤ በዳዓናየ ። ወትረ ፡ አሴዝሕክ ፤ ወአዜምር ፡ ለክ ።
Is. 38, 19c 26 I will expose Your justice in my salvation. 20a 27 Without ceasing, I will glorify You and sing psalmodies to You.

And on the day of the Sabbath we address to You the glorification and thanksgiving that we send up to You with humble adorations. In your light, let us see light and in justice, let us see your face. My lips shall rejoice when I glorify You and sing psalms to You, my God, holy one of Israel. M43 - LII, Supplication (Mastabqu'e) for the King. [NINTH PERIOD] D12 - EII, 'Esma la-älam: Two in Ge'ez.
