፲6 ወበከመ ሣህልከ ተዘክረኒ ። ፻5 ውሰተ ፡ አደክ ላመሐፅን ፡ 1ፍስየ ። በዝዉኒ ፡ አገዚለብሔር ፡ ለምላክ ፡ ጽድቀ ። ፻5. ለትኩን ፡ አገዚኦ-፤ ምሐረትከ ፤ ላዕሌ ወበክወ ፡ ላዕሊክ ፡ ተወከል ። ፻9. አገዚኦ ፡ በመዓትክ ፡ ሊትቅሥፈኒ ። ወበመቆሠሩትከ ፡ ሊተገሥጂ ። ፻6. ስዎቧኒ ፥ አገዚኦ ፡ ጸሉትየ ፡ ወስአለትየ ። 13ከ ወለፅምለኒ ፡ ለንብዕየ ፣ ወሊትጸመዉኒ ። ፻6 ለንተ አገዚኦ ፡ ሊታርሕቅ ፤ ሣሀለክ ፣ አምኒየ ። ምሐረትከ ፡ ወድድቀክ ፡ ዘልፈ ፡ ይርክጧ ። ፻6. ፐ ለንሰ ፥ አቤ ፡ አገዚኦ ፡ ተሣሀለኒ ። ወስረይ ፡ ላቲ ፡ ለነፍስየ ፡ አስመ ፡ ለበስኩ ፡ ለክ ። ፻.45, ል፡፥ ብርዛነ ወጽድቀክ ። አማንቱ ፡ ይምርሓሒ ፣ ወይሰዳኒ” ደብረ ፡ መቀደስክ ። ፻5. ተንዎአ ፤ አገዚኦ" ፡ ርድለ ። ወለድጎክ ፡ በአንተ ፡ ስምክ ፻: ዓፅቪኒ ፤ ወለንጽሐኒ ፤ አምንጢለትየ ። ዌ. ወአምለበሳል ሱ፤ ለንድሒ ። ወመንፈሰከ ቀቶሰ ፤ ሊታውፅአ ፤ አምላዕሌየ ። ዕስየኒ ፤ ፍሥሐ ፣ ወለድጎናተክ ። ፻6. አገዚኦ ፡ በስምክ ፡ ለድጎኳ ። ወበገይልከ ፡ ፍታል ፡ ሊተ ። ፲፻8. ለፅምለኒ ፣ አገዚኦ ፤ ጸሉተየ ። ወሊትትሀየል ፡ ስአለተየ ። ፻6. ለድጎኳ ፡ አገዚኦ ፡ አምፀርየ ። ወለንገፈኒ ፤ አምአለ ፣ ቆሙ ፡ ላዕሌየ ። ፻ሊ ስም፪ ፡ ለምላኪየ ፡ ስአለተየ ። ወለፅዎለ ፡ ጸሉትየ። ። ፲6. ስምዐኒ ፤ አገዚኦ ፥ ጸሉትየ ዘሰለልኩ ፤ ኀቤከ ።

ለይኩን” ፡ ለጫን ። አገዚለብሔር ፤ የሀቦ ፤ ለመፍቀሬ አገዚለብሔር” ፡ ንገሠነ ፡ ሊዮ ለስ” ፡ በልዮ ፡ በ ፡ ጎለየ ፤ በለቋበዒሁ ፡ ገበ ፡ ለንጻረ ፡ ፀሩ ፡ ወጸላ አቱ ፥ ዘርሑቅ ፣ ወዘቅፋብ ፡ በገይለ ፡ መስቀሉ ፡ ለሊየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ገሩ ፤ ለይኩን ፡ ለጫን” ።
c) May the Lord give to the friend of the Lord, Our King N., what he must think in his intelligence and [the strength] in his fingers when he is facing his enemies. And may his adversaries, near and far, be subjected under his feet by the power of the Cross of Jesus Christ. So be it! Amen!

At this hour when all creatures are silent for a little while; in order to praise the Lord, the stars, the trees, and the waters keep watch, and all the hosts of the angels serve [the Lord]. On this day with the souls of the righteous. Alleluia! They glorify the Lord.

M12b - XVB, For the feast of the Angels, the following prayer is said: a) For His name alone is exalted. Those who fear the Lord do not neglect to pray to Him at the hour of midnight; for at that [moment] the stars of the heaven, the light of the sun and of the moon, the lightning and the clouds, the Angels and the Archangels, the Glories and the Dominations and all the [heavenly] hosts, the abysses, the sea, the rivers, the springs, and the wind, the fire and the water and the dew and all the rain that descends and all the souls of the righteous and the saints glorify Him. Those who pray without ceasing are counted in the thought of the Lord. [While you act thus, believers, exhort one another to pray].

[A] b) 1) All the hosts of the spiritual angels are flames of fire, and the holy Cherubim and the six-winged Seraphim glorify Thee with a loud voice and sing psalmodies with a voice that is not silent and say: "Holy! Holy! Holy!
3ይ፡ሐጆ። ቀቶስ ”* ፡ ቀድስ ፡ ቀዱስ ፡ አገዚለብሔር ”* ፡ ጸባሶት ፡ ፍጹም ፡ ምሉአ ፡ ሰማያተ ፡ ወምድረ ፡ ቀድሳተ ፡ ስብሐቲክ ።
2) The people: Holy, holy, holy is the Lord of hosts. The heavens and the earth are completely filled with the holiness of thy glory.
ወቱልቀሙ ፡ ለአሙንቱ ፡ መላአክት ፡ ወሊቃነ ፡ መላአክት ፡ መናብርት ፡ ፊ ወሥልጣናት ፡ ለጋአዝት ፡ ንይላት ፡ ወሊቃናት ፡ ኪሩቤል ፡ ወሱራፌል ። ይዳርጉ ፤ ወይብሉ ፡ ይትባረክ ፡ አገዚለብሔር ፡ ለምላክ ፤ አስራሊል ፡ወ ፡ ቡርክት ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ አንተ ፡ ለሐዛብ "”* ፡ ትመስል ፡ ጎይለ ፤ ሰማያት ፡ መዓልተ ፡ ወሊሊተ ፡ በነፍስ "* ፣ ቀዱስ ”* ፣ ወበልብ ንጹሕ ”፡፣ ይዜምሩ”፡ ፡ ክመዝ ፡ አንዘ ”* ፡ ይበሉ ። ሰረገላሁ ፤ ለአገዚለብሕር”" ፡ ፳ ለአላፍት ፡ ወትአልፊተ ፡ ለአላፍት ፤ ይሴብሕዎ ”* ፡ ለአገዚለበሔር ፡ በ ደብረ ፡ ሲና ፡ ቅቶስ ። ሰማያት ፤ ወኩሉ ፤ ዘውስቴቶተን”። ፡ ይገንዩ ፡ ለክ ፡ አስመ ፡ ለንተ ፡ ሰቀልኮ ፡ ለሰማይ ፡ ክመ ፡ ቀመር ፡ ወሣረራ ፤” ፡ ለምድር ፡ ወለጵናዕካ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ማይ ። ወፈጠርክ ፤ ጽልመተ ፡ ወብዝርኝነ ፤ ወጉለቆሙ ፡ ለክዋክብት ፤ ፣ በምልሶሙ ፣ ወሰመይኮሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ በበለስማቲሆሙ ። ዥሉ ፡ ፍጥረት ፤ ይዜንዉ ፡፤ ዕበየክ"” -፡ ወዕፀወ ፣ ገናዎኒ ፡ ለንተ ፡ ለብ ቄልክ ፤ ወበአንተዝ ፡፤ መፍትው"” ፣ ኩሉ ፡ ፍጥረተ ፡ ሰዝአ ፡ ያዐርጉ ፤ ለክ ፤ ስብሐተ ፤ ወለኩቴተ ፤ መሥዋዕተ ፣፤ ወቱርባነ ፤ ወዕጣ ፤ ለአገዚአ !፣፥ ወመድንኒነ ፡ ሊየሱስ ፡ ክርስተስ ፡ ዋሕድ ፡ ወልድክ ። ለንተ ፤ ው አቱ ፤ ባሕቲትክ ፡ ለኃዜ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ጌር ፤ ወመፍቀሬ ፡ ምሕረት ፡ ወገባሬ ፡ ሠናያት ፡ አስመ ፡ ለክ ፤ ስብሐት ፡ወአቲጊዝ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፣፤ ለጫን" ።
[A] c) The assembly of these Angels, of these Archangels, of these Thrones, of these Powers, of these Dominations, of these Virtues, of these Principalities, of these Cherubim and of these Seraphim cries out and says: "Blessed be the Lord, God of Israel, and blessed be this Church of the Nations: it resembles the host of the heavens. Day and night, with a holy soul and a pure and perfect heart, they sing psalmodies thus and say: "The chariot of the Lord is made of myriads of myriads, of myriads of myriads [of angels]. They glorify the Lord on the holy mountain of Sinai. The heavens and all that they contain render Thee humble homage, for it is Thou who hast suspended the heaven like a vault, who hast given foundations to the earth and hast established it upon the water. Thou hast separated the darkness from the light and Thou hast counted the stars in their totality and Thou hast named them all, each by its name. All creatures proclaim thy greatness, [like] the trees of the field that Thou makest to grow. And because of this, it is fitting that all creatures and man should cause glorification and thanksgiving, sacrifice, offering, and incense to ascend toward Thee, [toward] Our Lord and Savior Jesus Christ, thy only Son; Thou alone, Thou governest all. Thou art good, a lover of mercy, and Creator of good things. That is why, to Thee be glory and power for ever and ever.

[A] d) Thou art in heaven and Thou art on earth, and Thou art on the sea. Thou who hast gathered all creatures from the ends of the earth, and whose greatness possesses no limits. For it is not we who have said this, but it is Thou who hast said: "I am the Lord, the God who stands above the heavens. And I am present in all places. And there is no God except Me." Thy word is truthful, and no one resembles Thee. Holy Lord, Lord of the righteous and soul of all that is in thy hand. To Thee befit glory and exaltation, the immutable throne and the indefatigable and immeasurable power. Announce his greatness; and admirable is the beauty of his appearance. No one can approach his dwelling, nor evaluate his knowledge, his wisdom. His justice does not fail. He does not desire that one should tempt him and does not seek help. He has neither beginning nor end. His reign does not pass away. No one can overcome his strength, nor count

the host of his angels who serve Him. Thou art the Father of mercy, the Creator of all creatures and the Creator of the Law. Thou exterminatest the impious and Thou rewardest the righteous, Lord, Father of Our Lord and Savior Jesus, Lord of those who serve Him humbly. There is no lie in Him. He does not allow Himself to be corrupted and the thanksgivings that are owed to Him are innumerable. To Thee befit honor, glory, and adoration. May every rational soul find rest with Thee for ever and ever. Amen!

Alleluia! Ps. 150, 1a 1 Glorify the Lord in his saints! [A] Alleluia! 1b 2 Glorify Him in the strength of his power! Alleluia! 2a 3 Glorify him in his might! Alleluia! 2b 4 Glorify Him according to his immense greatness! Alleluia! 3a 5 Glorify Him to the sound of the trumpet! Alleluia! 3b 6 Glorify Him on the psaltery and on the harp! Alleluia! 4a 7 Glorify Him on the tambourine and in joy! Alleluia! 4b 8 Glorify Him with the stringed instrument, and on the lyre! Alleluia! 5a 9 Glorify Him on the sistrum with the pleasant sound! Alleluia! 5b 10 Glorify Him on the sistrum and in jubilation! Alleluia! 6a 11 Let every breath glorify the Lord! Alleluia! Glory to the Father and to the Son and to the Holy Spirit! Alleluia! In the ages and in the ages of ages! Alleluia! Alleluia! Alleluia! Alleluia! While we say this, we shall not perish.

to your Holy name, and that we may glory in your glory. Be our refuge, our power, our help, in the moment of our affliction, O Lord. M35 - LXXXVII, The Lord has reigned [A] 1 Archangel Michael, ask for us and Saint Gabriel, cause our prayer to ascend. 2 The four spiritual Animals, glorifiers and psalmists, pray for us. Angels of heaven, cause our prayer to ascend.

Alleluia! Glory to the Father and to the Son and to the Holy Spirit. Alleluia! Alleluia! Forever and ever!

9 Angel Michael, ask and pray for us and for the souls of us all. My angel said to me: "The angel of the Testament of Christ pleased Him more than the [other] angels". 10 Zechariah saw the pedestal of the lampstand entirely of gold, the walls of precious stones, and the foundations of justice of the Holy Church. 11 To your priests, O Lord, to your priests who have pleased You, You say to them: "Enter into the upper room of your sanctuary, there where the power of your glory dwells." 12 Blessed archbishop Peter, last of the martyrs! The people elected Peter and said to him: "Sit on the chair as bishop". 13 The just, the meek are sons of light and the king said [it] to them. 14 Blessed are the prophets who tasted death. The horses of fire and the chariot of fire came. Elijah ascended to the heavens. 15 Ananias, Azarias, and Misael said: "We will not worship the statue of gold that you have made. It is our God and it is our King who has delivered us from your hand".
ጴስ ፡ ቆስ ። ጻድቃን ፣ አሙንቱ ፡ የዋዛን ፡ ውሉደ ፡ ብርግን ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ወይቤሉሙ ። ብፁዓን ፤ አሙንቱ ፡ ነቢያት ፡ አለ ፡ ይጥዕምዎ ፡ ለሞት ፡ መጽለ ፡ ለፍራሰ ፡ አሳት ፡ ሰረገላ ኞ ፡ ዘአሳት ፡ ሊልያስ ፡ ዐርገ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማያት ። ይቤሉ ፤ ለናንያ ፡ ወዐዛርያ ፡ ወሚሳሌል ፤ ለምሳለ ፤ ዘወርቀ ፡ ዘገበርክ ፡ ሊንሰገድ ፡ ለክ ፡ ሀሉ ለምላክ ፤ ወሀሎ ፡ ንጉሥነ አምአደ ክ ፡ ውአቱ ፡ ያድጎዘ ። ፍሬ ፡ ምናጦስ ፡ ስሙ ፡ ለለባ።” ፡ ሰላማ ፡ ከጣቲ ፡ ብርዛን ፡ ም ሥያመ ፣ ትፍሥሕት ፥ መዝገበ ፣ ረድሌት ፣ መምህረ ፡ ሐገ ፡ ለለሐዛብ ። ገብርሌልፅ ፤ ለብሰራ ፡ ለማርያም ፡ ተፈሥሒ ፡ ይቤላ ፡ ሊሳይያስ ፡ ይቤላ ፡ ሐግስዩ ፡ ጣዕዋ ፣ ሰሉሞን ፡ ይቤላ ፡ ደብተራ ። 18ኞ፤ ዘበመትልወ ፤ በባለ ፤ አገዚአክ ፡ ኮነ ፤ በባፅክ ። ብፁዕ ፡ አስጢ ፋኖስ ፥ አስመ ፡ ሹሉ” ፡ ነባሪ ፡ ወቀናዩ ፡ ዕረፍተ ፡ ኮነ ፡ በባልክ ። በአንተ ጽድቀ ፡ ወሐይወት ፤ ርአሰዎ ፡ ዘለንደየ ፤ አምሐ ውዘ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ዘጉየ ፡ በገበ ፡ አገዚሉ ፡ ተዐስየዎ ፡ ፀቢየ ፡ ብፁዕ ፡ ሊ ባኖስ ፤ ይለ ፡ ጸሉቱ ፤ ለርለየ ፡ ዘቢጦ ፤ ኩኩሐ ፤ ለውሐዘ ፡ ማየ ። ባሐረ ፤ ጥበባት ፡ ለበ ፡ ልሳናት ፡ ንስር ፡ ሰራሪ ፤ ልቦለ”፣ ስ ብክት ፤ ዮሐንስ ፤ ወንጌላዊ ፤ ራአዮዎ ፣ ዓቡላት ፣ ወነገሩኒዎኞ" ፣ ቀሱም ፡ በፄወ ፤ መለኮት ።
16 Frumentius is the name of Abba Salamä, Revealer of the light, reservoir of joy, treasure of help, doctor of the Law for the nations. 17 Gabriel announced the good news to Mary. He said to her: "Rejoice". Isaiah called her "His new ewe". Solomon called her "Tabernacle". 18 Immediately after the feast of your Lord, your feast takes place, blessed Stephen, for for every servant and for every master your feast is a rest. 19 As for justice and life, he who renounces the pleasures of the world and who flees from his master is rewarded greatly. The blessed Libanos showed the power of his prayer. By striking the rock, he made water flow. 20 Ocean of science, father of languages, eagle who flies above the preaching, John the Evangelist had the vision of secrets and his discourses are seasoned with the salt of divinity. 21 He who concludes a pact with his Lord, who converts numerous crowds; the blessed Cyricus, toward whom no one was favorable, received the crown [of martyrdom] on his head. 22 You are, to Christ, the ewe who appeared [coming] from a distant land. In Bethlehem, the lion's cub knelt. She gave birth to the August One. 23 Jerusalem said: "My King and my God has been born. He whom the choirs of the [heavenly] armies glorify has appeared in the assembly of the Saints".

24 Gabra Manfas Qeddous dwelt with the ferocious beasts in a distant desert, clothed in hair, he who loved the poverty of the flesh. And upon him, he built a dwelling of mercy. 25 May heaven listen and may the earth lend an ear: the armies of angels rejoice, for today the wood of the Cross has been found thanks to the empress Helena. 26 Yärëd the musician, priest of the Lord, glory of the Resurrection, announces that the Law is the strap [of his shoe], that the Gospel is his sandal. Help us. Come into his living place. May your Enemy be repelled like dust. 27 The Lord ascended in jubilation and Our Lord to the sound of the trumpet. All his angels preached his Resurrection and announced his Ascension to the heavens.

28 For in his prayer and in his faith, having girded his loins, Abbä Garimä in the morning sowed and in the evening harvested his field. He was a just man in his patience. 29 Jesus chose twelve apostles, established them as pastors, and said to them: "He who listens to you listens to me. He listens to Him who sent me". 30 The child and his mother both accomplished their [spiritual] combat. The noise of the cauldron [was] like that of the thunder of the rainy season. The holy Martyrs did not fear death. 31 Blessed is Abbä Salamä, for he was the prophet and the apostle who was sent to our land. While we were in darkness, he revealed the light to us. Blessed is Abbä Salama. 32 These virgins loved You and followed You, they said to You: "Lord open to us, let us enter into the chamber of the kingdom of the heavens". 33 Bless, keep our assembly in peace, while we accomplish the work of our assembly. Having grouped ourselves, welcome us. 34 Pastor of day, guardian of night whose wings are of fire, who guards without ceasing the camp of the tents of justice, archangel Michael. 35 Perfect tent of Paul, Dwelling of justice that the Mighty One made. It emigrated from what grows old toward what does not grow old. On his harp, David his father sang it.
ደናገል ፤ አማንቱ ፤ ለፍቀራክ ፤ ወተለዋ ፤ ድጎሬክ ፡ አንዘ ፤ ይብላ ከ ፡ አገዚኦ ፡ ለርጎወ ፡ ንባአ ፡ ታዕክ ፡ መንገሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ። 33ኞ መነበረ ፣ ባርክ ፣ ፅቀብ *" ፡ በሰላም አንዘ፤ ፣ ንገብር ፡ ገብ ረ ፥ ማጎበረ ፥ ተጋቢለ፤ ፡ ተወከፍ ፡ ተወክፍ ፡ ለ ። ኖላዊ ፤ ዘመባልተ ፡ ጎላዊ ፡ ዘሌሊት ፡ ክነፊሁ ፡ ዘአሳት ፡ ዘየዐቀብ ፡ ወትረ ፡ ትዕይንተ ፣ ደብተራ ፣ ዘፓድቀ ፣ ዘሚካሌል”። ፡ ሊቀ ፡ መላአክት ። ደብተራ ፡ ፍጽምት ፡ ዘጳውሊ ። ለማጎደሩዎ ፡ ዘጽድቀ”፡ኔ ፡ ዘገብራ ፡ ክሃሊ ። ፈለሰት ፡ አም ፡ ዘይበሊ ፡ ኀበ ፤ ሊይበሊ ። በመሰንቀሁ ፡ አንዘ ፤ የሐሊ ። ዳዊት ፡ ለቡሃ፡ ፡ ዘወልፃ”” ፡ ወዓሊ ። ተክለ ፡ ዛይጣኖት ፣ ቋመወ ፡ አምገድለ ፡ ቀዱሳን ፣ ምክዕቢተ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይፍረይ”፡ ፣ 8ወ፳፻ ፤ ሊያጽዝ፤ ትልሞ ፤ ወሊገብለ፣ ድንሪተ ፡ አ መንፈስ ፡ ቀቶስ ፡ ነዲኦ ፤ ዕሴተ ”* ። ኀረይክ ፡ ለክ ፡ ጎረይክ ፤ ለክ፡ ፣ ለብርፃም ፡ ዐርክ ፡ ይስሐቀ ፤ ንዱሐ ፡ ወያዕቆዝ ፡ ላአከ፡ ፡ በዘንረየክ ፤ ተሰባሐክ ። 38፥ ይቤሉ ፡ አገዚለብሔር ፡ ለለብርፃም ፣ ሊታዐቢ ፡ ርአሰክ ፡ አስመ ፡
36 Takla Haymanot suffered doubly through the asceticism of the saints in order to produce fruit at thirty, sixty, a hundred [for] one. He did not bend his furrow and did not turn back, having received from the Holy Spirit the final reward. 37 You chose, You chose Abraham as friend, Isaac the pure, and Jacob as servant. By Him who chose You, You were glorified. 38 The Lord said to Abraham: "Do not exalt yourself, for where pride is, there is ruin. Better is humility in love". 39 The angel said to Tobit: "I am the angel Raphael, one of the seven archangels who was sent toward Tobit."

40 Abba Yoannes, guard and ask for the fruits of the earth, the first grass, and the last ear of grain, guard and ask for the fruits of the earth. 41 The angel said to Zechariah: "I have been sent to you to tell you and to announce this to you: Elizabeth your wife will bear you a son and you will name him by the name of John". 42 The Cross is the light for the whole world, the foundations of the Church. As David said in the psalm: "He built his sanctuary in the highest of the heavens". 43 The Cross is our power. The Cross is our strength, the salvation of our soul. The Jews did not believe, But we, we have believed. We who have believed, by his Cross, save us. 44 Jesus, the skillful [Creator], embraced his soul on his breast and from his mouth gave him the kiss, Fasiladas, so that you may rest from [your] pains. At the moment of the ascension to the highest of the heavens while the angels will sing psalmodies to You. 45 We have heard [tell] of your wonders, Eustathe who dwelt in the place of Armenia, of the miracles that took place in Ethiopia. So that he does not spread his net over our assembly, fight our Adversary, come to us quickly, our father, who have been adorned with the beauty of the topaz.

46 The beloved brothers James and John, sons of Zebedee, full of glory, filled with blessings, were, by their faith, the servants of Christ. 47 The light descended toward the tomb of the man of the Lord. And his tomb gave life to the sick. May the prayer of the Servant of Christ be with us. 48 The wicked servants rushed upon him and dragged him with ropes and brought him near the sacred enclosure and could not [overcome] the opposition of Stephen. 49 Abba Yohanni did not build a church in this world. He dwelt in the mountains. He circulated in caves while waiting for the reward of his hope. 50 And the appearance of this throne that the four Animals carry [was] like incandescent fire on the inside; a great light within it like the rainbow of the rainy season. Lightning surrounded it. 51 The woman says, etc.

[E] 1b 2 Let them glorify Him from the highest of heavens! 2a 3 Let all his angels glorify Him! 2b 4 Let all his hosts glorify Him! 3a 5 Let the sun and the moon glorify Him! 3b 6 Let all the stars and the light glorify Him! 4a 7 Let the heaven of heavens glorify Him! 4b 8 Let the water that is above the heavens 5a 9 glorify the name of the Lord! 5b 10 For He spoke and they were. 5c 11 He commanded, they were created. 6a 12 He established them for the ages and for the ages of ages. 6b 13 He gave them orders and they do not transgress them. 7a 14 From the earth, glorify the Lord. 7b 15 Sea monsters and all the abysses. 8a 16 Fire and hail, frost and snow, winds and storms, 8b 17 Which execute his orders. 9a 18 Mountains and all you, hills. 9b 19 Fruit trees and all you, cedars. 10a 20 Reptiles and all animals: 10b 21 Those that crawl and birds that fly. 11a 22 Kings of the earth and all peoples. 11b 23 Princes and all you, judges of the earth.

Alleluia!

Ps. 13, 1c 3 There is not one who does good, there is not a single one. 3g 12 Their feet are agile to shed blood. 6a 21 and you have confounded the plans of the poor. Ps. 14, 3a 5 and who does not act with guile with his tongue? Ps. 15, 1b 1 Keep me, O Lord, for I have trusted in You. 5b 10 [It is] You who will restore my inheritance... 10a 19 For You will not abandon my soul in Sheol. Ps. 16, 2b 5 May my eyes see your justice. 6b 14 Stretch your ear toward me and hear my voice... 10b 23 And the arrogance of their mouth speaks... 14b 32 Divide them in two in their life. Ps. 17, 4 5 When I called upon the Lord, I was delivered from my enemies. 8a 14 The earth trembled and was shaken 11b 23 [He] flew on the wings of the wind. 15b 32 He multiplied his lightnings and shook them... 19a 41 and they had come to me on the day of my affliction. 23b 50 And He did not cast his justice far from me... 27b 59 And with the perverse, you will be perverse. 31c 68 And He is faithful with all those who trust in Him. 36a 77 [He] gave me a protector for my salvation... 39b 86 And they fall under my feet... 44a 95 He saved me from the speech of the peoples. 48a 104 My God entrusted the care of my vengeance to me... 51c 113 To David and to his posterity unto the ages. Ps. 18, 6a 9 And He, like the bridegroom coming out of his chamber... 10a 18 The fear of the Lord is pure and lives forever. 14b 27 If they do not dominate me; at that moment, I will be pure. Ps. 19, 4a 5 and may He remember for you all your sacrifices... 7c 14 By the power of the salvation of his right hand Ps. 20, 3a 3 You have given him the desires of his soul. 7b 12 And you will make him rejoice by showing him the gladness of your face... 12a 21 For they have directed iniquity against you.

the salvation of my soul. O my God and I have trusted in You | I have trusted in You. Toward You, I have taken refuge. Do not cast me away forever and do not forget me in your mercy. O Lord, give us grace like the grace of David, before You, O Lord. Before You, O Lord, grant us to find grace, and before Christ, your Son and your Holy Spirit, the life-giver. When we remember Zion and we do not forget Jerusalem, before all the nations and the kings of the earth. In the courtyard of the house of the Lord and in the midst of You, Jerusalem

It is better for us that we please the Lord than to please men. It is better for us that we please the Lord. Give us wisdom, wisdom and counsel, knowledge. Grant us happiness, O Lord. Accept us, You who accepted the repentance of Peter, likewise accept my repentance as a sinner. And to You, the glory and the thanksgiving and the honor unto the ages of ages. O Lord, to You befits greatness, to Christ, to the Creator of all things, to Him who governs the universe. May He give us his mercy every morning and we, as is fitting, will give thanks to the Lord. Never cease [singing] the glory of the Father, the thanksgivings to the Son, the blessing of the Holy Spirit. God who does not judge according to appearances in the morning, remember us and cast a look upon our work. You are the good Shepherd, vigilant, who does not sleep. Be with us. And just as You have kept us from evening to morning, keep us, O Lord, from morning to evening

M14 - XIX, [Invocations] [G] Have mercy on us, Father, Alleluia! Be merciful to us, Son, Alleluia! Merciful Holy Spirit, remember us in your kindness. (Three times) We address [our] glorification to You and we send up to You [our] thanksgiving. Have mercy on us, Merciful One, obtain our pardon. Save us and protect our soul and our body, Christ, Son of the Lord. Our God and our Savior, Jesus Christ, be merciful to us. In the abundance of Your mercy, blot out my sin and send upon us your kindness. For kindness comes from You. Alleluia! Have mercy on us, merciful Father, and be merciful to us. Give your kindness, Merciful One, do not exterminate us by remembering the past. In your mercy, for You are merciful; and your kindness is abundant for all who call upon You. They call upon You as is fitting, You who hear unceasingly, who are powerful in salvation. Powerful in salvation, O our God, for justice is his word. We ask the Father to send us his kindness, for the Father has the power [to grant] what has been asked of him. Alleluia! Glorification is fitting for him. Alleluia! Thanksgiving is fitting for him. Alleluia to Christ, to the Lord of all things. Alleluia! Alle! Alleluia! And now, Who is my hope? if not the Lord. Into your hands, O Lord, I commit my soul. To the God of mercy, I commit my soul. To the King of glory, I commit my soul. To my Lord and to my God, I commit my soul. From every evil work, save my soul. Come, let us pray to Him, let us pray to Him and implore the mercy of our just God; for justice is his word, You who can do all things and to whom nothing is impossible, God of the poor and hope of the desperate, we take refuge in You. (To be said three times).

Three Priests have the Cross adored while saying: "Without sickness, nor suffering; without pain, nor fatigue, in joy and in peace, as today, may the Lord bring us, bring you". Let them say thus, these words alternately. End of Terce.

### VI. [MONDAY EVENING OFFICE] IN 'ARARAYE
፲ ዘሰርክ ፡ ለራራይ ። ፲፲. ቀቶስ አገዚለብሔር ፡ ቀቶስ ፡ ምሕረትከ ፡ በል ።
M1 - II, Holy is the Lord. [Kidän]
፲ኛ, ወአምዝ ፤ መስተብቀፅ ፡ በአንተ ፡ አለ ፡ ይነገቶ ፡ በለራራይ ፡ በል፤ ። ፔ. ወአምዝ ፡ ብፁዓን ፡ አለ ፡ ተንድገ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ በል ። መዝ ፡ ።
M2 - IV, Then: Supplication (Mastabqu'e) for Travelers in 'aräräye. M3 - V, Then, Ps. 31: Blessed are those whose sins are forgiven.
ሃ፲[, መስተበቀዕ ፡ በአንተ ፣፡ ዝናማት ፡ በለራራይ ፡ በል ። ፲ ሃ፲11, ምዕፊፍ ፤ ተፈሥሑ ፡ ጻድቃን ፤ በአገዚለብሔር ፡ በል ። መዝ ፡ 8፪ ።
M4 - VII, Supplication (Mastabqu'e) for the Rains in 'aräräye. M5 - VIII, Ps. 32: Rejoice, O righteous, in the Lord.
፳, መስተብቀፅ ፡ በአንተ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ በል ። ፳፲ ወአምዝ ፡ ለዳበዒሁ ፡ በል ፡ ወቀ; ፡ ደብተራ ፡ በተባርዮ ። 3:1፲፲. ለአመ ፡ ይከውን ፡ ቀፅ ፡ ደብተራ ፡ አስመ ፡ ረሰየ! ፤ በገዕዝ ፣ በል ። ወአምዝ ፡ይክውን ፡ ለቋብዒሁ ፡ፍሑቃት ፡አስመ ፡ረሰየ ፡ በለራራይ ፡ በፅ 51ኛ, ወድጎሬሁ ፤ ቋውሉስ ፤ ሐዋርያ ፤ ወገብረ ፤ በል ። ፳፡, ወአምዝ ፡ ለልቦ ፡ ይትባረክ ።
M6 - X, Supplication (Mastabqu'e) for the King. M7 - XI, Then: His fingers ('Arbä'et (31) alternately with "the tent of service" ('Arbä'et(-)). M8 - XIII, If one has said "the tent of service" (-) the Liton is chanted in Ge'ez. If [on the contrary] one has said: His fingers (31) the Liton is chanted in 'aräräye. -- XIV, Then reading from St. Paul and the Acts of the Apostles. (M) - (XV), There is no Yetbärak (Canticle IX).
፪7፲፲, ወይበል ፡ ዲያቀን ፡ ዛሌ ፡ ሉያ ፡ ዛሌ ፡ ሉያ ፡ ዛሌ ፡ ሉያ ፡ ዛሊ ፡ ሉያ ፡ ዛሌ ፡ሉያ ፡ ዛሌ ፡ ሉያ ፡ን ፡ ንስገድ ፡ ቁሙ ።
M9 - XVII, The Deacon says: "Alleluia (six times). Let us go, let us bow down. - Stand up!"
፳፲, ወያንብብ ፡ ወንጌለ ። ፳፲ጂ, ወድጎሬሁ ፥ ኪን ፡ ለክ ለለብ ፥ ንዌድሰክ ፤ አገዚኦ ፡ ለክ ፤ በአምልሳ ። ፳ጂ, ወአምዝ ፡ ፻ት ።
-- XVIII, Reading of the Gospel. M10 - XIX, Then evening Kidän. M11 - D6 - XX, Then Salast.
፳፳1, ወሰላም ። 8:81፲፲, ወድጎፊሁ ለድጎክ ፤ ሕዝበክ ። ፳8፻71, ወአምዝ ፣ አገዚሶ ፤ መሐረ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ በል ።
M12 - D7 - XXI, Saläm. M14 - XXIII, After that: Prayer: "Save us who are your people". M15 - XXVI, Then [Invocation] "O Lord, have mercy on us, Christ". (Three times).

### TUESDAY
ዘሠሉስ ። ፲ ስብሐተ ፡ ነገህ ።

M4 - VI, Me'ezäl of Tuesday [Ps. 33-50]. Ps. 33, 3b St. 4 Let the meek hear and rejoice. 8a 13 The angel of the Lord shall encamp around them... 12c 22 I will teach you the fear of the Lord... 17a 31 The face of the Lord is against those who do evil... 21b 40 not one of them will be broken. Ps. 34, 3b 5 Say to my soul: "I am your help". 8a 14 Let the net which they do not know come upon them... 11a 23 Witnesses of iniquity have risen against me... 15a 32 They gathered against me and rejoiced... 18b 41 and I will glorify You in the midst of a numerous people. 23a 50 Arise, O Lord, and hear my cause... 27b 59 and let them say continually: "Great is the Lord!" Ps. 35, 4b 6 And he did not want to understand to do good. 8a 15 And as You have multiplied Your mercy, O Lord... 12c 24 and let not the hand of the sinner shake me... Ps. 36, 3c 7 And He will feed you with His riches. 8a 16 Leave anger and cast away wrath. 12c 25 He will gnash his teeth against him. 16a 34 Better is the little that is in righteousness... 20b 43 The enemies of the Lord when they have been honored and exalted... 24c 52 For the Lord supports his hand... 28b 61 And He will not cast away the righteous. 31b 70 And his steps do not stumble. 36a 79 And when I returned, I found him no more... 40c 88 And He will snatch them from the hand of the sinners... Ps. 37, 5b 8 Like a heavy burden, they weigh upon me. 10b 17 And my groaning is not hidden from You. 14c 26 and like a mute who does not open his mouth... 19a 35 And I will confess my transgressions. Ps. 38, 2a 1 I said: "I will guard my mouth, so that I may not sin with my tongue."
ወሶበ አገብአ ፤ ኀጣአክዎ ። ወያነገፎሙ ፡ አምአደ ፡ ኃጥላን ። ፻, ጢ,. ጩክ ክቭ ፡ ጆር ፡ ክቡድ ፡ ክበደ ፡ ላዕሌየ ። ፲ፐ ወገባርስኒ ፡ ሊይትንባአ ፡ አምኔክ ። ወክወ ፡ በፃም ፡ ዘሊይክሥት ፡ ለፉሁ ። ወአነገር ፡ ጌጋይየ ። አቤ ፤ ለዐቀብ ፡ ለፉየ ፣ ከመ ፣ ሊይስሐት በልሳን" ። ምንት ፡ አማንቱ ፡ ኙልቆን ፡ ለመዋዕልየ ። ወትዕገሥት]፪ኒ ፤ አምንቤክ ፣ ውአቱ፤ ። ወባሕቱ ፡ ከንቶ ፡ ይትሀወኩ"፡ ፡ ሦሉ ፡ ሰብአ ። ፻5. ወሰም፪ " ፡ ቃለ ፡ ስአለትየ ። ብፁዕ ፡ ብአሷ ፤ ዘስመ ኞ ፡ እገዚለብሔር ፣ ትውክልቱ ። ውስተ ፤ ርአሰ ፤ መጽሐፍ ፡ ተጽሕፈ ፤ በአንቲለየ ። ለንተ አገዚኦ ፡ ሊታርልቀ ፤ ሣህለከ ፤ አምያየ ” ። ለይገብሉ-"፡ ፡ ድጎሬሆሙ ፡ ወይትንፈሩ አለ ፤ ይፈቀሩቶ ፡ ሊተ ፤ አኩየ ። ከ አምዕለት አኪተ ፡ ያድጎኖ ፣ አገዚለብሔር ። ይባአ" ፡ ወይርለይ ፡ ዘክንቶ ፡ ይነበብ ። ለንተ ፡ አገዚኦ ፤ ተሣሀለኒ ፤ ወለንሥለኒ ፣ አፍድዮሙ” ። ጸምለት ፡ ነፍስየ ፡ ኀበ ፤ ለምላኪየ ፣፤ ሕያው ። መድን ፡ ገጽየ ፣ ለምላኪየ" ። ለዎንት ፡ ተንድዢ ፤ ወለምንት ፡ ትኩዝየ ጳንሶሱ ። ፻5. አምሕዝብ ፤ ውድላን ፤ አምጽድቀ ። ፲1 ኅበ ፡ ለምላኪየ ፡ ዘለስተፍሥሐ ፡ ለውርዙትየ ። ፲፻, ጣቀይኮሙ ፡ ለለሐዛብ ፤ ወሰደድኮሙ ። "ገእ. ዘለኮ ፡ በቀስትየ ፡ አትለመን” ። ወተማሠጡ፤ ጸላአተነ ። ዥሉ ፡ ለሚረ ፤ ቀድሜየ ፤ ውአቱጆ ፡ ኀፍረትየ ። ወደፈክጆ ፣ ጽላሉተ ፡ ሞት ። ወለምንት ፡ ትመይጥ ገጸከ ፡ አምክ ። ፻ሉ. ይዛኒ ፡ ላሰ ፡ አምውሉደ ፡ አጓለ ፡ አመሐያው ። ለሐዛብ ፡ ይወድቁ ፡ ታሐቴክ ።
5c 10 What is the number of my days? 8b 19 And my patience is from You. 12c 28 But all men are agitated in vain. Ps. 39, 2c 3 He heard the voice of my prayer. 5b 12 Blessed is the man who has put his trust in the name of the Lord. 8b 21 At the head of the book, it is written about me... 12a 30 You, O Lord, do not remove Your mercy from me! 15b 39 Let those who wish me evil retreat and blush! Ps. 40, 2b 2 The Lord will save him from the evil day. 7a 11 If someone enters and sees [me], he will speak vain [words] to me. 11a 20 You, O Lord, be merciful to me. Raise me up, I will pay them my debts. Ps. 41, 3a 3 My soul thirsts for my living God. 6c 12 My God is the savior of my face. 10b 21 Why do You abandon me and why must I walk in sadness? Ps. 42, 1b 2 [Deliver me] from the people of the transgressors of justice... 4b 11 toward my God who gladdens my youth. Ps. 43, 3b 5 You have afflicted the peoples and You have driven them out. 7a 14 It is not in my bow that I will trust... 11c 23 Our enemies were tearing us to pieces... 16a 32 All the day my shame is before me. 20b 41 and the shadow of death has covered us. 25a 50 Why do You turn Your face from us? Ps. 44, 3a 4 His beauty is more beautiful than that of the children of men. 6b 13 The peoples will fall under You. 10a 22 The daughters of the queen for Your honor. 14a 31 All the glory of the daughter of the king is within. 18b 40 It is for this that the nations will render You humble homage, O Lord. Ps. 45, 5b 8 The Most High has sanctified His dwelling. 10a 17 He has made these battles cease to the ends of the earth... Ps. 46, 3a 3 For the Lord is Most High and terrible. 8 12 For the Lord is king over all the earth. Ps. 47, 3c 5 The city of the great King... 9c 14 The Lord has founded it forever. 14a 23 Compel your hearts [to consider] His power. Ps. 48, 3b 4 Rich and poor... 8a 13 The brother will not save his brother; and will not save the man... 12c 22 And their dwellings from generation to generation... 16a 31 Only the Lord will save my soul from the power of Sheol, when they take me.

3a 5 M [It is He] who forgives you all your sins, 3b 6 T who heals all your diseases. 4a 7 [It is He] who saves your life from corruption, 4b 8 who crowns you with His kindness and His mercy. 5a 9 M who satisfies your desires with His blessing, 5b 10 T who renews your youth like [that of] the eagle? 6a 11 The Lord is the worker of kindness 6b 12 He does justice to all the oppressed. 7a 13 M He has shown His ways to Moses, 7b 14 T and His will to the children of Israel. 8a 15 The Lord is merciful and kind, 8b 16 slow to anger, rich in mercy and just. 9 17 M He does not chastise without ceasing and does not get angry always 10a 18 T He who does not treat us according to our sins; 10b 19 and has not repaid us according to our faults; 11a 20 For as much as the heaven is high above the earth, 11b 21 M so much has the Lord strengthened His mercy upon those who fear Him; 12a 22 T as much as the east is distant from the west. 12b 23 [so much] He has removed our sins from us. 13a 24 And as a father has pity on his children, 13b 25 M so the Lord has pity on those who fear Him; 14a 26 T For He knows our nature. 14b 27 Remember, O Lord, that we are dust. 15a 28 And man! His days are like grass! 15b 29 M And like the flower of the fields, so he blossoms. 16a 30 T for the spirit goes out from him and he exists no more; 16b 31 [He] knows his place no more. 17a 32 The kindness of the Lord extends from eternity and unto eternity, 17b 33 M upon those who fear Him. 17c 34 T And His justice [extends] upon the generations of generations 18a 35 upon those who keep His Law, 18b 36 and remember His commandments to execute them. 19a 37 M The Lord has prepared His throne in the heavens, 19b 38 T and He judges everything in His kingdom. 20a 39 Bless the Lord, all you, His angels 20b 40 strong and powerful, who execute His orders.

Ps. 142, 3a 6 When the Enemy besieged my soul, 3b 7 He trampled my life to the ground. 7c 18 And let me not become like those who descend into the pit. 8a 19 Make me hear Thy mercy in the morning. 12b 30 And annihilate all the oppressors of my soul, 12c 31 for I am Thy servant. Ps. 143, 4b 11 And his days, like a shadow, pass! 5c 12 O Lord, incline Thy heavens and descend. 10a 23 He who gives salvation to kings, 10b 24 who saved David, His servant, from the murderous spear. 14a 35 Their heifers are fat. 14c 36 And there are no breaches in their walls. Ps. 144, 3a 7 Great is the Lord and He [is worthy of] many thanksgivings 3b 8 and His greatness is unlimited. 8b 19 Slow to anger, rich in mercy and just. 9b 20 The Lord is good to those who wait for Him. 13 31 and just in all His works. 14a 32 And the Lord supports those who stumble. 20a 43 The Lord guards all those who fear Him, 20b 44 and || will annihilate all sinners. Ps. 145, 5a 8 Blessed is the man whose help is the God of Jacob 5c 9 and [who puts] his trust in the Lord, his God. 9c 20 And He supports the widow and the orphan 9c 21 and will destroy the way of the sinners. Ps. 146, 5a 9 Great is the Lord and great is His power. 5b 10 His wisdom is infinite. 10a 21 He does not take pleasure in the strength of the horse, 10b 22 and does not delight in the legs of man. Ps. 147, 5a 9 He who gives snow like wool 5c 10 || scatters the fog like ashes. Glory to the Father, etc.

### MONDAY
ዘሱይ ። በናገም ፡ ሰኑይ ፡ ለልቦ ፡ ለሥርቆት ፣ ወዋዚጣ ፡ ዘአንበለ ፡ ፻ት ፡ ወሰላም ። አምድጎረ ፡ ዕዝል ፡ አንዘ ፡ ናለኛቶ ፡ በል ። ድጎረ ፡ ጽወንየ ፣ ለትሩጽ ፡ በል ። ቀድመ ፡ ይትባረክ ፡ ክመ ‹ ታድጎክ ፡ በገዕዝ ፡ በፅ ።
[Rubric] The second Monday, no Asergot, nor Wäzëmä, but [antiphons] Salast and Saläm. Afterwards, [antiphon] ἘΖΙ, [Liton] after "While we give thanks, etc. Then vesper psalms, liton: "Let approach", etc. Before [the canticle]. Blessed be… liton "So that Thou mayest save us", in Ge'ez, etc.

M3 - D3-- VII, 'Arbä'et: "The star led them" (2) - Ps. 85: Incline Thine ear, O Lord, and 86: His foundations are on the holy mountains. M12 - D5 - XVII, Salast: "Do not distance yourselves". (45) - Ps. 101: Hear, O Lord, my prayer.

### TERCE

በለራራይ ። አገዚኦ ፡ መሐረነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ፻ጊዚ ፡ በል ። በፅዝል ። አገዚኦ ፡ መሐረ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ፻ጊዜ ፡ በል" ። መሐረ ፤ አገዚኦ ፤ ለምላክ ፡ በምሕረትክ ፡ ደምስስ ፡ ለበሳ ፡ መሐረ! ።
our faults. Have pity on us, O Lord and be merciful to us. In 'Aräräye. O Lord, have pity on us, O Christ. (Three times) In 'Ezl:
በዕዝል ። ክርስቶስ ፤ ክርስቶስ ፤ ክርስቶስ ፤ ለንተ ፣፤ ወልቶ ፡ ለቡሩክ ካሣቴ ፤ ዝርዛን ፤ ተሣሀለነ ። ወበንሉስ ፤ ዜማ ፡ አገዚኦ ፡ መሐረ ፤ ክርስቶስ ። ፻ጊዜ ፤ በል” ። ስብሐት ፤ ለአገዚለዝብሔር ፡ ኪያነ ፤ ዘፈጠረ ዋ ፤ ከመ ፣ ናምልኮ ። ስብሐት ፤ ለማርያም ፡ አመ ፡ ለምላክ ፤ አገዝአትነ ፤ ወመድኀኒተ1 ። ስብሐት ፤ ለመስቀለ ፤ ክርስቶስ ፡ ዕፀ ፤ መድጊትተ ፡ ንይል ፤ ወፀወን! ።
O Lord, have pity on us, O Christ. (Three times) Have pity on us, O Lord, our God. In Thy mercy, blot out our faults, have pity on us. O Lord, have pity on us, O Christ. In 'Ezl: Christ, Christ, Christ, Thou art the Son of the Blessed. Revealer of the light, have pity on us. In "small Chant": O Lord, have pity on us, O Christ! (Three times) Glory to the Lord, to Him who created us so that we might serve Him.
Glory to Mary, Mother of God, Our Lady, and our Salvation. Glory to the Cross of Christ, tree of salvation, our strength and our refuge.

[G-A] And let us all say, "O Lord, be merciful to us" with all our heart and with all [our] mind and let us all say "O Lord, be merciful to us". Lord, You who govern all, God of our fathers, we beseech Thee, hear [-us] and be merciful. You who made the heaven and the earth with all their worlds, we beseech Thee, hear [-us] and be merciful. You who answer the prayer of him who has prayed to Thee and who blesses the year of the righteous, we beseech Thee, etc. You who do not desire the death of the sinner, but who grant [his] conversion and [his] life, we beseech Thee, etc. For the peace of the whole world and the tranquility of the holy Churches, we beseech Thee, etc. For all the churches [which are] here, in communion with us; with fervor, we beseech Thee, etc.
ብአንተ ፡ ሰላም ፡ ለሉ ፤ ዓላም ፤ ወህድለት ” ፡ ለቀዱሳት ፡፤ ለብያተ ፡ ክርስቲያናት ፤ ና ። በለስተሐምሞ ፡ በአንተ ፤ ዘዝየ ” ፡ ቀዱሳት ፤ ዘአገዚለዝሔር ፡ ለብያተ ፡ ክርስቲያናት ፡ ወዘበዥሉ ” ፡ ትድምርት ፡ ና ። በአንተ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ሊቃነ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ አለ፤ ያረትሮ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ዘበለማን ”፣ ና ። በአንተ ፡ ጳጳስ ፡ ለባ ፡ አገሌ ” ፡ ወረድሌተ ” ፡ ክህነቱ ፡ ወንብረቱ ” ፡ ና። በአንተ ፡ ዘዝየ ፡ ክቡሬን ፡ ቀሳውስት ፡ ወአለ ፤ በክርስቶስ ፡ ዲያቆናት ፡ ወለ ኩሉሙ" ፡ ሥዩማን ፡ ወለሉ ፡ ሕዝብ" ፡ ና ። በአንተ ፡ ሠናያን ” ፡ ለምልኮ ፡ ነገሥት ” ፡ ወንጉሥነጆ ፡ አገሌ ፡ ወክሉ። ፡ ታዕካሁ ፡ ወሰፊዌቱ ፡ ና ። በአንተ ፤ ሐውዘ ፡ ለየራት ፤ ወሰምራት ፤ ወፍሬያተ ፡ ምድር ፡ ና ። በአንተ፤ ዛቲ፣ሀገር፣፤ወኩሉን፣፤ ለህጉር፣፤ወበሓውርት፡ረደሰያት፣፤ ለዕቋዳያት”። ወምኔታት ፤ አለ ፤ በፃይማናተ ፡ ዚለክ ፣ የኀድሩ ፡ ውስቴቶን ፡ ና። በአንተ።፡ ፍሬዎ።፡ አለ ፤ ይዴብሑ ፤ ለቀቶሳት ፤ ለብያተ ፡ ክርስቲያ ናት ፤ ወይዜክሩ ፡ ነግያነ ፤ ወምስኪና! ፡ ና ። በአንተ ፡ አለ ፡ ወሀቡ!3ኞ ፡ ምጵዋተ ፡ ክመ ፡ ንዘክሮሙ ፣ መድኀቴኒቶሙ ፡ና ። በአንተ ፤ ነጋድያንዎ ፣ ወለአለሂ ” ፤ በአገር ፤ የሐውሩ ፡ ወለፍያንሂ። ፡ ፍጡነ ፤ ክመ ፤ ትፈውዕሙ”፡ ና ። በአንተ ፤ አለ ፤ ውስተ ፡ መዋቀሐት ፡ ወባአስጆዎ ፣ ወቀንየት ፡ መረር ፡ ይ ዔልዉ ፡ ወውስተ ፡ ምዥናንሂ ፡ አለ ፤ ይትኩዙኖ ፡ ለኃዌ!ጆ ፡ ና ። በአንተ ፡ ሹሉ ፡ ነፍስ ፤ ሕዝንት ፤ ወትክዝተ ፡ ሣሀለ ፡ አለ ፡ ትፈቱ ፡ ና ። በአንተ ፡ዥሉ ፥ነፍስ ፥ክርስቲያናዌ " ፡ወሉ ፡ነፍስ ክርስቲያናዊት ፡ና ።
For all the archbishops, who rectify the teaching according to the truth, we beseech Thee, etc. For our bishop, Abba N., and the support of his priesthood and his life, we beseech Thee, etc. For the honorable priests here and those who are deacons in the Lord and all those who are established in dignity, and all the people, we beseech Thee, etc. For the fervent piety of the kings and of our king N., and of all his palace and his army, we beseech Thee, etc. For the purity of the air, the harvests and the fruits of the earth, we beseech Thee, etc. For this city and all the cities, the countries, the islands, the fields, and the monasteries in which dwell those who have faith in Thee, we beseech Thee, etc. For those who give fruits as tithes to the churches and who remember the poor and the needy, we beseech Thee, etc. For those who have given us alms so that we may remember them and their salvation, we beseech Thee, etc. For travelers and those who go on foot and the sick, so that You may heal them quickly, we beseech Thee, etc. For those who are in chains, in litigation and bitter servitude and for our brothers who will be judged before the courts, we beseech Thee, etc. For all sad and afflicted souls who call for mercy, we beseech Thee, etc. For all the souls of Christian men and all the souls of Christian women, we beseech Thee, etc. For the peoples who exist and who await from Thee riches and mercy; we beseech Thee, etc.


[p. 387] {#po.33.p387}
በአንተ ፤ አለ ፣ ይቀውሙጆ ፣ ሕዝዝ ፡ ወይጸንሑ ፡ ዘአምንበክ ፡ ብዕለ ወሣህለ” ፡ ና ። ለድጎን ፡ አገዚኦ ፤ ሕዝበ ፡ ኪያክ” ፡ ወባርክ ፡ ርስተክ ፡ ናስተበቱፀዐክ ፡ ስማዕ ፡ ወተሣሀል ፤ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፤ ለሜጫን"” ።
Save your people, O Lord, and bless your inheritance, we beseech Thee, hear [-us] and be merciful, in the ages of ages. Amen!
19፡9 ፳፳፳- ዕዝፅ ፡ ሰላም ፡ በፅ ።
M19 - D6 - XXX, Saläm in ἘΖΙ. M20 - XXXI, [Invocations]. [E] O Lord, have mercy on us, O Christ! In 'Ezl, - in a high chant. Three times. O Christ, O Christ, O Christ, You are the Son of the Blessed, Revealer of the light, be merciful to us. Homage, homage, homage to the Father and to the Son and to the Holy Spirit. Holy, holy, holy are You, Son of Mary. Son of the Lord, be merciful to us. Father of Our Lord and Savior Jesus Christ who alone is truly
፳፳፳፤ አገዚኦ ፡ መሐረ ፡ ክርስየስ ። በዕዝል ፡ በልዑል ፡ ዜጣ ። ፻ጊዜ በፅ ። ክርስቶስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ክርስየስ ፤ ለንተ ፤ ወልድ ፣ ቡሩክ ፡ ከሣቴ ፡ ብርፃን ፡ ተሣሀለነ ። ጋያ 2ዳ ፡ ፡ ለለብ ፣ ወለወልድ ፡ ወለመንፈስ ፡ ቀቶስ ። ቀቆስ ፡ ቀቶስ ፡ ቅቶስ ፡ ለንተ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ማርያም ፡ ወልደ ፡ አገዚለብሔር ፡ ለቡሁ ፡ ለአገዚአ ፡ ወመድንኒ ፡ ሊየሱስ ፡ ክርስየስ ፡ ዘውአቱ ፡ ባሐቲቱ ፡
your Son. You are His Father in justice. Have mercy on us and be merciful to us. Lord, we take refuge in You, O Lord, O Lord. O Lord, have mercy on us, O Christ! (Three times). Glory to the Lord, etc. Glory to Mary! Glory to the Cross of Christ. M21 - XXXII, Blessing of our father Yärëd. (for the three days of Great Adjurations) Protect us against the weight of our sins, O Lord of us all who erases and drives away all evil thoughts. Look upon us O Lord, and hear us all. Yes, our God, living God with the living, source for the thirsty, send upon us your mercy. May the mercy of the Lord which He grants from the ages, and unto the ages to those who fear Him, be upon us, Amen! in the ages of ages.
በለማን ፥ ወልድክ ፡ ውአቱ ፡ ዘበድድቀ ለቡሁ ፡ ለንተ ፥ መሐረ ፡ ወተሻ ሀለ ፥ እገዚለብሔር ፡ ቤክ ፡ ተማሕፀነ ፤ አገዚኦ ፥ አገዚኦ ። አገዚኦ ፡ መሐረ ፡ ክርስየስ ። ፻ጊዚ ፡ በል ። ስብሐት ፤ ለአገዚለብሔር ፡ በፅ ። ስብሐት ፤ ለማርያም ። ስብሐት ፣ ለመስቀለ ፡ ክርስየስ ። ቃለ ፥ በረክት ፣ ዘለቡ ፣ ያሬድ ፡፣ ካህን ። ዘይትነበብ ፡ በሠለስቱ ፡ ዕለታት ፥ ምህላት ፡ ምሉላት ።
To Him be glory, to Him who is our God, giver of honors. Alone, He can do all. Glory and honor to You, O Lord, greatness and honor and adoration to You, our God! Thanksgiving and humble homage to You, our God. Justice and peace to You alone, to You, our God, reign and riches; to You, cause of our pride and indulgence. To You befit, O our Creditor, greatness and honor, our Leader, have mercy on us, You who show mercy toward us, our hope, cause of our glorification, we, your servants and your handmaids. Free us from the servitude of the bonds of Satan and shameful thoughts. Free us, protect us, and forgive us, O our good God. O just Judge, be gentle toward us. Pray [to your Father], have mercy [on us]. Use mercy, grant us grace and love to accomplish the works of justice. [Make], O Just One, that we be without iniquity. Choose us, You who chose the tree of life in the midst of the garden: O Lord, life-giver and pure. You are the God of the pure, and of sinners, guide of travelers. O God who gives to those who pray to Him; who gives honor and grace and mercy to him who perseveres, have mercy on us, O Merciful One who possesses mercy. To You, thanksgiving. And may our Protector remain upon us; and make excellent dispositions penetrate into us when our mouths, our throats address You. Amen! Amen! So be it! So be it! O our God, we pray You to hear him who has prayed to You. Upon your servants and your handmaids, pour out the words of your tongue, upon the children, and upon the little ones, upon the sick, upon those who suffer, upon those who have a broken heart, upon all that exists and that will exist. Disperse and drive away and scatter the impure spirits.


[p. 388] {#po.33.p388}
ዝሸ58 ኘ ሰውር ፡አምላዕሌ ፤ ፡ ክበደ ፡ ኃጣውሊ ፡ አገዚለ ፡ ሹል ፡ ደምሳሴ ፡ ኛሉ ፡ ሊና ፡ አኪት ፤ ወፅግደ ፤ ነድር ፡ ላዕሌ ፡ አገዚሶ ፡ በአንቲለነ ፥ ወኩን ” ፡ ሰጣዔ ፡ ለኛል ” ፡ አወ ”* ፣ ለምላክ! ፣ ሕያው ፡ ምስለ ”' ፡ ሕያዋን ” ፣ ወለንቀዕተ ” ፡ ለድሙ ላን ። ፈኑ ፡ ለ3" ፡ ሣህልክ ” ፣ ላዕሊ ፡ ወጣህሉሰ ” ፡ለአገዚለብሔር ፡ አምዓለም ፡ ወአ ስከ ፡ ለዓለም ፡ ዲበ ፡ አለ ፡ ይፈርህዎ ፡ ዘወሀቦ ” ፡ ይኩን ፡ ላዕሊ ፡ ለጫን ፡ ለዓለ ፡
In your name, may all impure spirits [be driven away]. And procure happiness to every soul that You have created. And upon this sick person may there be a remedy and upon this man may there be a remedy! And upon this water may there be a remedy and make the prayer of the saints be accepted and considered as pleasant. And make this house prosperous. And grant good health to this city. And to these fields grant grain, peace and blessing. And to this church, grant peace, vigor and happiness. And upon this grain, upon this wheat, upon this millet, upon this spelt, upon these cereals, upon these lentils, upon these beans, upon this flax, upon this rye, and upon this cumin and upon all the works of man give [your] blessing. To the cows give milk. To the bulls grant strength. To the children, give growth. O Lord, to our King N., give peace and good health. O Lord, eternal King, to the bishops whose faith is orthodox grant peace. O Lord, to the priests grant a loyal priesthood. Yes, O Lord, and to the [other] ministers make them remain in an orthodox faith.
ባለም ” ። ሉቱ ስብሐት ፡ ለውአቱ ፡ ለምላክኖ ፡ ወፃቤ ፣ ክብር ኖ ፣ ባሕቲቱ” ፡ ይ ክል ፡ ኛሉ ። ስብሐት ፡ ወክብር ፡ ለክ ፡ አገዚኦ ፡ ዕበይ ” ፡ ወክብር ፡ ወሰጊድ” ፡ ለክ ፡ ለለምላክነ)” ። ለኩቴት ፤ ወገነይ ፡ ለከ ሞ ፣ ለለምላክነ” ፣ ጽድቀ ፣ ወሰላም ፡ ለክ ፡ ለባ ሐቲትክ ፡ ለክ ፤ ወስረይ ፡ ለነ ፡ ለምላክ ፤ ቴር" ። ወመኩንን ፡ ጻድቅ ፣ ወየዋህ ፡ ኩን ፣ ላዕሊ ፤ ጸሊ ፤ ወመሀክ “ ፣ ወገበር። ፡ ሣህለ ” ፡ ወሞገሰ ኖ ፡ ወፍቅረ ሞኖ ፡ ለምገባረ ፡ ጵድቅ ። ንኩን ፡ ኦጻድቀ “ ፡ ዘለልቦ “ ፡ ዐመፃ ፡ ጎርየ ፡ ዘንርይኮ ፡ ለዕፀ ፣ ሕይወት ፡ በማአከለ ፡ ገነት ፣ ኦአገዚኦ ” ፣ ወማ ሐየዊ ” ፤ ወንጹሐ ”፣ ለንተ-፥ ለምላኮሙ ፣ ለንጹሐን ፣ ወኃጥላን ” ፣ ኦአንገዳ"፣ ሃድያን ፡ ኦለምላክ ” ፡ ለዘሰለሉ ” ፡ ይሁብ ፣ ሐይወተ ” ። ለዘተዐገሠ ” ፡ ይሁብ " ፡ ክብ ረ ፡ ወሞገሰ ። ወጣህለ ” ፡ መሐረነ ” ፡ መሓሪ ” ፡ ዘለክ ፡ ምሕረት ” ፡ ወለክ ፡ ለኩቲት ፡ ወይ ንድር። ፡ ለንገድፍ ፡ ላሐ "፡ ፡ አምዐይንቲ ፡ ለሜን '”። ሶክርስቶስ ፤ ወልደ ”* ፡ ምሕረት ፤ ዘልመቆ ”* ፡ ቲሩት ””* ፡ ይኩን ፡ ላዕሊ ፡ ለጫን ”* ። ሀዝ ፡ ጸጋክ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ላዕሌነ ፡ ለጫን "" ። ወበመንፈስ ፡ ቀስ ?” ፡ ለጽንዐነ "" ፣ ለጫን ። ንጊ ፡ ለክ ፡ ለዓለም ፡ ወለባለመ ፡ ባለም ፡ ለጫን ። አወ ፡ ሉገፅን" ፡ ስረዩ ፡ ካህናት "" ፣ ጎበ ፤ ሰረይክሙ ‹ይስረይ ፤ አገዚለብሔር ፡ ለዳጳ ስ! ፤ ለባ ፤ አገሊ"= ፡ ማኅቶተ ፣ ሥጋሁ" ፡ ሊያማስን""፣ አገዚለብሔር ፣ መብሩፅ ተ" ፣ ሐዝብ ፣ ወነገድ ፡ ሊያጥፍአ ፡ እገዚለብሔር ። ንይለ ፡ ደም ?" ፡ ሊያደክም" ፡ አገዚለብሔር ። ትዕይንተ ፤ ደብተራ ፤ ሊያድፍን "* ፡ ወመካነ ፡ መደራ '” ፡ ሊያጥፍአ ፡ አገዚለብሔር ፡ የሀብክሙ ፤ አገዚለብሔር ፤ ቡሩክ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ዘዐምደ ”* ፡ ይተክል ፡ ወሠር 9"፣ይሰቀል፡ዘሊይርሕቅ፡አምአገዚለብሔር ፡ወሊይወጽአ፣ አምሰብአ ፤ የሀብክሙ” ። ሰለሉ ”* ፡ ወጸልዩ ፡ የሀብክሙ ፡ አገዚለብሔር ፡ ቡሩክ ፡ ክረምተ ፡ ዘለልቦ ፡ ለባር ፡ ወሐሩር ”* ፡ ዘለልቦ ፡ ጽንዕ ”* ፡ ወበረድ ”" ፡ ዘትልወ ፡ ያረዊ ፡ ዘሰሬፃ ፣ አድ""፣ ወ)84፣መስበልተ፣አትወት ፤ በዐውድ ፣ ወገብለት ፡ በማነበልት ” ፡ ዘየለክ ፡ ለበዓለ ፡ ቤት ፡ ለአንስሳ"” ፤ ወለሀገር ፡ ሊይርሐቅ ፤ አምአገዚለብሔር ፡ ወሊይወጽአ ” ፡ አም ሰብእ ፡ የሀብክሙ ፡ ሰለሉ ፡ ወጸልዩ ። ያሀብኮሙ ፡ ቡሩክ ፣ ጥረተ ፡ ዘአምርሑቀ "" ፡ ያስተርሊ ”* ፡ ዘዐምደ "* ፡ ያሰርጉ "”* ፡ ወማየ ፡ የሐምገ ፡ ወሣዕረ ፡ ዘይቀርፅ ፡ ወዐይ ፡ ጸላሊ ፤ ሊይቀርቦ ”" ፡ ዘሊይርሐቅ ፤ አምአገዚለብሔር ፤ ወሊይወጽአ ”” ፡ አምሰዝአ ፡ ሰለሉ ወጸልዩ ።
Make the unbelievers convert to You. O Lord, to the monks, grant a beautiful crown. Yes, O Lord, our God, break Satan before You, disperse the demons, and to the impious Jews, hang around their necks a chain of fire. O Living One whose name is holy, greatness and honors befit. O Holy One deliver us, your servants and your handmaids, from the bonds of our sin. By your beloved Son guard their paths. O our God, may joy and peace be upon us. O Most High, be our refuge and our rampart. O our Creator and our Author raise us up. O our Creator and our Author, raise us up before your holy and pure, elevated and complete glory. Take possession of us for your [heavenly] granaries and fill us, those who suffer, the lepers, with the Holy Spirit. Yes, O Lord, be for us our strength and our rampart. Amen! Give us grace and peace. Amen! Be for us indulgent and merciful. Amen! It befits You to be our strength and our rampart. Amen! Give a remedy to the flesh. Amen! Save us and deliver us. Amen! Protect us, O leader of the poor. Remove the tears from our eyes. Amen! O Christ, your Son, may the mercy, may the kindness that is customary to Him, be upon us, Amen! Give your grace that it may be upon us! Amen! And by the Holy Spirit, strengthen us. Amen! We render You humble homage, in the ages and in the ages of ages. Amen! Yes, Logsan, Forgive, priests. Those whom you will have forgiven, may the Lord forgive! To our Bishop, Abba N., may the Lord not corrupt the lamp of his flesh. May the Lord not exterminate the offspring of the people nor the stranger. May the Lord not weaken the power of the blood. May the Lord protect the military camps and may He not annihilate the place named Madara. May the blessed Lord give us a son who will plant the mast [of his house] and who will hang the beams upon it. May the Lord not move away and not go out from man. Pray that the blessed Lord give you a rainy season without drought nor heat, without violence, nor frost, that He may water the furrows. Ask and pray, that the Lord may stretch out his hand, [so that] the ears having grown, the products [of the earth] may return to the [grain] threshing floor and that they may return to the granaries, and that what is sufficient for the master of the house for the animals and for the city, may not move away from the Lord and may not go out [from the hands] of man. May the Blessed One give you riches that appear from afar, that He may adorn the mast [of the house] that He may trouble the water and strip the grass. Ask and pray that the eye of the Enemy may not approach, he of that which is not far from the Lord and which are not of man.

ኦአገዚዶ ፡ ላዕለ ፤ ንጉሥነ ”* ፡ አገሌ ፡ ሰላመ ”* ፡ ወዳና ፡ ረሲ ። ሶአገዚኦ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ዘለዓለም ፤ ለጳጳሳት ፡ ርቱዓ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ሰላመ ፡ ረሷ ። ሶአግዚኦ ”፡ ፡ ለቀሳውስት ፡ ርቱዐ ፡ ክህዙ ፡ ረሲ ። አወ ፡ አገዚኦ ”* ፡ ለዲያቆናት ፡ በርቱዕ ”* ፡ ዛይጣኖት ፡ ወላአካ ፡ ረስዮሙ ”* ። ንፉቃን ፡ ኀቤከ ፡ ይትመየጡ ፡ ረሲ ”* ። ኦአገዚኦ ፡ ለመነሳት ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ለክሊለ ፡ ይሠሉ ” ፡ ሀብ ። አወ ፣ አገዚኦ ፡ ለምላክነ ፤ ቀጥቀጦ ፡ ለሰይጣን ፡ በቅድሜክ ፡ ለለጋንንት ፡ ዝርዎው ፡ ወላዕለ ፡ ለይሁድ ፡ ከሓድያን ፡ ስቅል ፤ በክሳውዲሆሙ ”* ፡ መዋቀሐተ ፡ ዘአሳት ”* ፡ ኦ ሐያው ፡ ለስምክ ”" ፡ ይደሉ ፡ ዕበይ ” ፡ ወክብር ። ሶቀቆስ ፡ ለለገብርቲክ ፡ ወለለአማቲከ ፡ ለገዕዘ ” ፡አሟአሰረ ፡ ጎጢለተነ ” ፡ በፍቁር ፡ ወልድክ።፡ ፡ ዕቀብ ፡ ፍና9ነ)”” ። ሶለምላክ ፡ይኩን ፡ ፍሥሐ ፡ ወሰላም "* ፡ ላዕሌ ”"* ፡ ኦልዮል ፡ ክነ ”” ፡ ጸወነ ፡ ወጥቀመ ። ሶፈጣሪ ፡ ወገባሪ ” ፡ ለቁመነ ፡ ቀድመ ።” ፣ ስብሐቲክ "” ፡ ቀዱስ ”” ፣ ወንጹሕ ፥ ልዑ ል ፤ ወምሉአ” ፡ አመዝገብክ ”* ፡ ለጥርየ ፡ ወምላአ ፡ ላዕሊ ”* ፡ መንፈሰ ፡ ቅቆሰ ፤ ሕሙማን ”* ፡ ወዝልጉሳን ፡ አወ ፡ አገዚኦ ”* ፡ ጽንዐ ፡ ወጥቀመ ፡ ኩን ፡ ላዕሊ ፡ ለጫን ። ጦገሰ ወሰላመ ፡ ሀበ ፡ ለጫን ። መፃኪ ፡ ወመሓሪ ፡ ኩን ፡ ላዕሌ ፡ ለሜን "” ። ጽንዐ ፡ ወጥቀ ፡ ዘለከ ፡ ይደሉ ፡ ይዙን ፡ ላዕሌ ፡ ለጫን ። ሀብ ፡ ፈውሰ ፡ ለሥጋ *” ፣ ለጫን ። ለድጎክ ፡ ወባልሐ ፡ ለጫን ። ክድዝ ፣ ኦሊቀ ”” ፡ ነናያን ”” ፡ ለንገድፍ ፡ ላሐ "፡ ፡ አምዐይንቲ ፡ ለሜን '”። ሶክርስቶስ ፤ ወልደ ”* ፡ ምሕረት ፤ ዘልመቆ ”* ፡ ቲሩት ””* ፡ ይኩን ፡ ላዕሊ ፡ ለጫን ”* ። ሀዝ ፡ ጸጋክ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ላዕሌነ ፡ ለጫን "" ። ወበመንፈስ ፡ ቀስ ?” ፡ ለጽንዐነ "" ፣ ለጫን ። ንጊ ፡ ለክ ፡ ለዓለም ፡ ወለባለመ ፡ ባለም ፡ ለጫን ። አወ ፡ ሉገፅን" ፡ ስረዩ ፡ ካህናት "" ፣ ጎበ ፤ ሰረይክሙ ‹ይስረይ ፤ አገዚለብሔር ፡ ለዳጳ ስ! ፤ ለባ ፤ አገሊ"= ፡ ማኅቶተ ፣ ሥጋሁ" ፡ ሊያማስን""፣ አገዚለብሔር ፣ መብሩፅ ተ" ፣ ሐዝብ ፣ ወነገድ ፡ ሊያጥፍአ ፡ እገዚለብሔር ። ንይለ ፡ ደም ?" ፡ ሊያደክም" ፡ አገዚለብሔር ። ትዕይንተ ፤ ደብተራ ፤ ሊያድፍን "* ፡ ወመካነ ፡ መደራ '” ፡ ሊያጥፍአ ፡ አገዚለብሔር ፡ የሀብክሙ ፤ አገዚለብሔር ፤ ቡሩክ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ዘዐምደ ”* ፡ ይተክል ፡ ወሠር 9"፣ይሰቀል፡ዘሊይርሕቅ፡አምአገዚለብሔር ፡ወሊይወጽአ፣ አምሰብአ ፤ የሀብክሙ” ። ሰለሉ ”* ፡ ወጸልዩ ፡ የሀብክሙ ፡ አገዚለብሔር ፡ ቡሩክ ፡ ክረምተ ፡ ዘለልቦ ፡ ለባር ፡ ወሐሩር ”* ፡ ዘለልቦ ፡ ጽንዕ ”* ፡ ወበረድ ”" ፡ ዘትልወ ፡ ያረዊ ፡ ዘሰሬፃ ፣ አድ""፣ ወ)84፣መስበልተ፣አትወት ፤ በዐውድ ፣ ወገብለት ፡ በማነበልት ” ፡ ዘየለክ ፡ ለበዓለ ፡ ቤት ፡ ለአንስሳ"” ፤ ወለሀገር ፡ ሊይርሐቅ ፤ አምአገዚለብሔር ፡ ወሊይወጽአ ” ፡ አም ሰብእ ፡ የሀብክሙ ፡ ሰለሉ ፡ ወጸልዩ ። ያሀብኮሙ ፡ ቡሩክ ፣ ጥረተ ፡ ዘአምርሑቀ "" ፡ ያስተርሊ ”* ፡ ዘዐምደ "* ፡ ያሰርጉ "”* ፡ ወማየ ፡ የሐምገ ፡ ወሣዕረ ፡ ዘይቀርፅ ፡ ወዐይ ፡ ጸላሊ ፤ ሊይቀርቦ ”" ፡ ዘሊይርሐቅ ፤ አምአገዚለብሔር ፤ ወሊይወጽአ ”” ፡ አምሰዝአ ፡ ሰለሉ ወጸልዩ ።
